<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47865868' data-tags='Startups,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47865868' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wheelfront.com/this-alberta-startup-sells-no-tech-tractors-for-half-price/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alberta startup sells no-tech tractors for half price</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿尔伯塔初创以半价销售无科技拖拉机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47865868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2250</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47865868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 761</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47865868'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An Alberta startup is selling traditional, no-tech tractors at half the price of modern models, targeting farmers who value simplicity and repairability. By removing electronics, they cut upfront costs and maintenance headaches, challenging mainstream ag-tech pricing and support models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家阿尔伯塔初创企业以半价销售无科技的拖拉机，瞄准看重简单与易修的农民。去除电子系统后，制造与维护成本下降，这一做法可能影响主流农业科技的定价和售后模式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47840219' data-tags='Business,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47840219' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/04/tim-cook-to-become-apple-executive-chairman-john-ternus-to-become-apple-ceo/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>John Ternus to become Apple CEO</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>约翰·特诺斯将任苹果公司 CEO</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47840219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47840219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47840219'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple has announced a leadership transition with Tim Cook moving to executive chairman and John Ternus named CEO, signaling a planned succession. Ternus, a veteran hardware and product operations leader, would steer Apple’s product roadmap and hardware/software integration in the next era. The move underscores Apple’s emphasis on continuity and a hardware-centric strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果宣布高层交接，蒂姆·库克将出任执行董事长，约翰·特诺斯接任 CEO。特诺斯长期担任硬件/产品运营负责人，接任后将引领苹果的产品路线与硬件/软件协同。此举突显苹果在保持连续性和以硬件驱动战略方面的长期规划。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/04/tim-cook-to-become-apple-executive-chairman-john-ternus-to-become-apple-ceo/tile/Apple-John-Ternus-Tim-Cook_lp.jpg.og.jpg?202604202226' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/04/tim-cook-to-become-apple-executive-chairman-john-ternus-to-become-apple-ceo/tile/Apple-John-Ternus-Tim-Cook_lp.jpg.og.jpg?202604202226' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek v4 continues to push long-context capabilities, delivering more memory-efficient retrieval and scalable context processing for large language models. The update targets practical deployments that need to reason over very long documents without blowing through resources.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek v4 在长上下文能力上进一步提升，提供更高效的检索和可扩展的上下文处理，便于大模型在海量文档上进行推理而不耗费过多资源。此版本面向实际部署场景，改善长期上下文的可用性。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47879092'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI unveils GPT-5.5, the latest mid-cycle upgrade in the GPT-5 family. It emphasizes faster inference, safer defaults, and enhanced developer controls for production deployments, signaling another step in the rapid evolution of foundation models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 发布了 GPT-5.5，作为 GPT-5 线的中期升级。新版本强调更快的推理与更安全的默认设置，并为开发者提供更丰富的部署控制，再次推动大型语言模型的迭代速度。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Framework Laptop 13 Pro 延续了模块化、便于维修的设计，并升级了内部硬件选项。此次发布强调可升级硬件相对于一次性笔记本的可行性，对重视可持续性与自定义性的开发者与硬件爱好者尤为重要。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theolivepress.es/spain-news/2026/04/20/eu-to-force-replaceable-batteries-in-phones-and-tablets-from-2027/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>All phones sold in the EU to have replaceable batteries from 2027</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>到2027年，欧盟销售的所有手机将配备可更换电池</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47834195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1429</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47834195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1246</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47834195'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU will require replaceable batteries in all phones starting 2027, aiming to extend device lifespans and reduce e-waste. The rule could force redesigns, disrupt supply chains, and influence consumer costs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟将于2027年要求所有手机具备可更换电池，旨在延长设备寿命并减少电子垃圾。此举可能迫使厂商重新设计，影响供应链并提高一些消费者成本。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47907879' data-tags='Programming,Culture,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47907879' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techtrenches.dev/p/the-west-forgot-how-to-make-things' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The West forgot how to make things, now it’s forgetting how to code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西方忘记了如何制造，现在又忘记了如何编码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47907879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1060</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47907879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 739</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47907879'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Western economies have lost touch with making things, and now their coding skills are at risk as software becomes more opaque. The piece argues for a revival of hands-on engineering and practical tech literacy to stay competitive in a hardware-software world.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，西方在动手制造能力方面日益薄弱，如今软件日益复杂也让编程能力面临挑战。强调重建动手工程和实际科技素养，以在硬件与软件高度结合的时代保持竞争力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!YrAo!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fa56e63d9-5f03-432a-99de-2f46dd286b53_508x340.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!YrAo!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fa56e63d9-5f03-432a-99de-2f46dd286b53_508x340.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ChatGPT 图像 2.0</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47852835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1017</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47852835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 945</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47852835'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI unveils ChatGPT Images 2.0, expanding multimodal capabilities by enabling richer image generation and editing inside chats. It promises higher fidelity visuals, more robust prompting controls, and faster rendering, making it easier for creators and developers to prototype visual AI tools directly in conversation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 发布 ChatGPT 图像 2.0，扩展多模态能力，在对话中实现更丰富的图片生成与编辑。该更新带来更高保真度的图像、更强的提示控制和更快的渲染速度，为创作者和开发者在对话中原生原型化视觉 AI 工具提供新途径。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>面向 Windows 9x 的 Linux 子系统</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47861270'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An experimental Linux compatibility layer aims to run Linux userland on Windows 9x-era systems. It demonstrates the ongoing appeal of cross-OS compatibility, even in retro environments, and offers insight into how modern tooling could be adapted for older platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个实验性 Linux 兼容层尝试在 Windows 95/98 等旧系统上运行 Linux 用户空间。它体现了跨操作系统兼容性的持续吸引力，并为将来在老平台上应用现代工具提供了洞见。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47863217' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://qwen.ai/blog?id=qwen3.6-27b' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen3.6-27B: Flagship-Level Coding in a 27B Dense Model</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen3.6-27B demonstrates flagship-level coding capabilities in a compact 27-billion-parameter model, suggesting strong performance on programming tasks without massive scale. The result highlights progress in efficient, accessible model sizes for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen3.6-27B 在仅27亿参数的紧凑模型上展现旗舰级编码能力，表明在编程任务上已经具备出色性能，而无需大规模模型。此成果凸显了在高效、易获得的模型规模上实现进步。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47866697' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47866697' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fingerprint.com/blog/firefox-tor-indexeddb-privacy-vulnerability/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We found a stable Firefox identifier linking all your private Tor identities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>发现 Firefox 的稳定标识符可连接你所有的 Tor 身份</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 911</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 284</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47866697'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers found a persistent Firefox identifier can link a user&#x27;s private Tor identities across sessions, undermining anonymity. The finding underscores how browser storage features can erode privacy in privacy-focused tooling and calls for mitigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员发现 Firefox 的持久标识符可在会话之间关联用户的 Tor 身份，削弱匿名性。这一发现揭示了浏览器存储机制对隐私工具构成的潜在威胁，并需要相应缓解措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fingerprint.com/static/893bf56cdf5a22443c3b3ace0bb862df/blog_firefox_vulnerability.jpg' data-full='https://fingerprint.com/static/893bf56cdf5a22443c3b3ace0bb862df/blog_firefox_vulnerability.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47868867' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47868867' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/22/apple-fixes-bug-that-cops-used-to-extract-deleted-chat-messages-from-iphones/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple fixes bug that cops used to extract deleted chat messages from iPhones</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果修复漏洞，警方曾用其提取已删除的 iPhone 聊天记录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47868867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 869</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47868867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47868867'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple fixed a bug that allowed law enforcement to recover deleted iPhone chat messages, a rare privacy breach that persisted across devices. The patch closes this forensic loophole, reinforcing user data deletion and security. It underscores the ongoing tug-of-war between privacy protections and investigative tools in mobile platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果修复了一个以前被执法部门用来提取已删除 iPhone 聊天记录的漏洞。上述修补加强了消息删除的彻底性，提升了用户隐私的保护。此事再次凸显移动平台在隐私保护与执法需求之间的持续博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/iphone-pop-up-notifications.jpg?resize=1200,900' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/iphone-pop-up-notifications.jpg?resize=1200,900' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47824463' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/vercel-confirms-breach-as-hackers-claim-to-be-selling-stolen-data/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vercel April 2026 security incident</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 859</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 486</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47824463'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vercel disclosed a security incident from April 2026, detailing what happened, the affected services, and the steps they took to contain and remediate. The disclosure underscores how even modern cloud platforms can face complex attacks, stressing the importance of monitoring, rapid incident response, and proactive customer credential hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vercel 披露了 2026 年 4 月的安全事件，说明了事件经过、受影响的服务以及应对和修复措施。这次披露凸显了即便是现代云平台也会遭遇复杂攻击，强调监控、快速响应和客户凭据安全的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47876043' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47876043' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://socket.dev/blog/bitwarden-cli-compromised' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bitwarden CLI compromised in ongoing Checkmarx supply chain campaign</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bitwarden CLI 在持续的 Checkmarx 供应链攻击中被妥协</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47876043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 857</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47876043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 416</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47876043'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bitwarden CLI was compromised as part of a broader supply chain campaign tied to Checkmarx, illustrating how attackers weaponize developer toolchains. The incident underscores the risk of compromised updates and the need for strict verification of CLI binaries, signatures, and release channels.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bitwarden CLI 据称在与 Checkmarx 相关的供应链活动中被篡改，暴露了开发工具链的脆弱性。此事件强调应对 CLI 更新进行签名验证、固定版本、并监控 CI/CD 流水线中的异常更新。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/cgdhsj6q/production/65ef8dc5e66260e20fdf13cead82ebd41b705ee6-1018x666.png?w=1000&amp;q=95&amp;fit=max&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/cgdhsj6q/production/65ef8dc5e66260e20fdf13cead82ebd41b705ee6-1018x666.png?w=1000&amp;q=95&amp;fit=max&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47892074' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-04-24/google-plans-to-invest-up-to-40-billion-in-anthropic' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google plans to invest up to $40B in Anthropic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌拟向 Anthropic 投资最高400亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47892074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 805</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47892074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 811</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47892074'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google plans to invest up to $40B in Anthropic, signaling a deepening strategic partnership and potential equity stake. The move underscores Google&#x27;s aggressive push in AI and could accelerate Anthropic&#x27;s work while giving Google access to its models and IP.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌计划向 Anthropic 投资最高400亿美元，标志着双方关系进一步紧密，存在潜在股权安排。此举凸显谷歌在AI领域的扩张策略，可能推动 Anthropic 的研究进展，并让谷歌获得对方技术与知识产权的接入。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47855293' data-tags='Space,Business,Hardware,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47855293' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/spacex/status/2046713419978453374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SpaceX says it has agreement to acquire Cursor for $60B</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SpaceX宣布以60亿美元收购 Cursor 的协议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47855293' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 805</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47855293' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 962</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47855293'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX says it has an agreement to acquire Cursor for $60B, a deal that would dramatically expand its capabilities if confirmed. The acquisition would reshape SpaceX&#x27;s technology stack and could accelerate its push into new software and hardware domains, attracting regulatory attention given the scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX 表示已与 Cursor 达成以 600 百万美元收购的协议，若成真将大幅扩展其能力。此次收购可能重塑 SpaceX 的技术栈，推动其在软件和硬件领域的新拓展，同时也可能引来监管关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1697749409851985920/HbrI04tM_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1697749409851985920/HbrI04tM_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47879986' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47879986' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-04-23/meta-tells-staff-it-will-cut-10-of-jobs-in-push-for-efficiency' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta tells staff it will cut 10% of jobs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 将裁员 10%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47879986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 790</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47879986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 859</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47879986'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta told staff it will cut 10% of jobs, part of a broader efficiency push to rebalance costs as the company doubles down on AI initiatives. The layoffs spotlight the ongoing pressure on big tech to optimize headcount amid shifting strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 通知员工将裁减约 10% 的岗位，旨在提升运营效率并在加码 AI 项目之际重新平衡成本。裁员凸显大型科技公司在战略调整期对人力资源进行优化的普遍压力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47851948' data-tags='Privacy,Career,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47851948' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/sustainability/boards-policy-regulation/meta-start-capturing-employee-mouse-movements-keystrokes-ai-training-data-2026-04-21/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta to start capturing employee mouse movements, keystrokes for AI training</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 将开始捕获员工鼠标移动与按键用于 AI 训练数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 783</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 520</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47851948'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta plans to capture employee mouse movements and keystrokes to train AI models, raising significant privacy and workplace surveillance concerns. The policy could reshape corporate data practices and worker autonomy across tech companies. It spotlights the tension between improving AI capabilities and protecting user privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 计划开始捕获员工的鼠标移动和按键以用于训练 AI 模型，触发对隐私与职场监控的重大关注。此举可能重塑科技公司层面的数据实践与员工自主权，凸显提升 AI 能力与保护隐私之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://img.etimg.com/thumb/width-1200,height-900,imgsize-31734,resizemode-75,msid-130422612/tech/technology/meta-to-start-capturing-employee-mouse-movements-keystrokes-for-ai-training-data.jpg' data-full='https://img.etimg.com/thumb/width-1200,height-900,imgsize-31734,resizemode-75,msid-130422612/tech/technology/meta-to-start-capturing-employee-mouse-movements-keystrokes-for-ai-training-data.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47831621' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://awesomeagents.ai/news/github-fake-stars-investigation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub&#x27;s fake star economy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 的假星星经济</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47831621' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 783</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47831621' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 366</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47831621'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers allege manipulation of GitHub stars, suggesting fake engagement to inflate repository popularity. The investigation underscores how easily engagement metrics can be gamed and why more robust signals are needed for discovery and trust on code platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员指控 GitHub 仓库存在通过假互动提升星标数以美化热度的现象。此事凸显互动指标易被操纵的风险，并推动平台加强对发现与信任的更稳健衡量方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://awesomeagents.ai/images/news/github-fake-stars-investigation_hu_e6899b47d07205c8.jpg' data-full='https://awesomeagents.ai/images/news/github-fake-stars-investigation_hu_e6899b47d07205c8.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47903126' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/amateur-armed-with-chatgpt-vibe-maths-a-60-year-old-problem/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Amateur armed with ChatGPT solves an Erdős problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>业余者借助 ChatGPT 解决 Erdős 问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 725</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 508</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47903126'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An amateur uses ChatGPT to tackle a long-standing Erdős problem and reports a result that some observers find plausible. This episode underscores AI&#x27;s potential to assist mathematical thinking while highlighting the need for rigorous verification of AI-generated proofs. It also raises questions about the boundary between exploration and formal proof.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位业余爱好者借助 ChatGPT 尝试解决一个长期困扰的 Erdős 问题，称得出一个看似合理的结果。此事件凸显了人工智能在辅助数学思考方面的潜力，同时也强调对 AI 生成证明进行严格验证的必要性。它还引发了关于探索与正式证明边界的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/124ad289-58c2-4d51-87e8-aee854d10fc7/abstract-rubiks-cube.jpg?m=1777033076.511&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/124ad289-58c2-4d51-87e8-aee854d10fc7/abstract-rubiks-cube.jpg?m=1777033076.511&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnn.com/2026/04/23/politics/us-special-forces-soldier-arrested-maduro-raid-trade' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US special forces soldier arrested after allegedly winning $400k on Maduro raid</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美军特种部队士兵因涉嫌在马杜罗突袭案中获利被逮捕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47882645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 668</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47882645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 723</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47882645'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A US special forces soldier was arrested amid allegations of illicit gains linked to a Maduro raid, highlighting potential security and ethics concerns within covert operations. The case raises questions about governance, accountability, and the handling of sensitive operation proceeds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名美军特种部队士兵因涉嫌在马杜罗突袭案中获得40万美元收益而被逮捕，暴露了隐秘行动中的治理与伦理问题。这起案件引发对透明度、问责制以及敏感行动收益处理的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47835635' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47835635' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asteriskmag.com/issues/14/the-mystery-in-the-medicine-cabinet' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Acetaminophen vs. ibuprofen</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对乙酰氨基酚 vs. 布洛芬</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 646</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 427</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47835635'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Acetaminophen and ibuprofen treat different types of pain and have distinct risk profiles. Acetaminophen is gentler on the stomach but can damage the liver at high doses, while ibuprofen reduces inflammation but may upset the stomach and affect kidney function; dosing and medical context matter for safe use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对乙酰氨基酚和布洛芬在机制与风险上各有不同。乙酰氨基酚对胃部影响较小，但高剂量可能损伤肝脏；布洛芬具有抗炎作用，但可能引起胃部不适并影响肾功能，使用时需考虑炎症程度、年龄和肝肾健康。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://asteriskmag.com/media/pages/issues/14/the-mystery-in-the-medicine-cabinet/e71f2c3954-1776281832/dynomight-social-1600x900-crop-sharpen50.png' data-full='https://asteriskmag.com/media/pages/issues/14/the-mystery-in-the-medicine-cabinet/e71f2c3954-1776281832/dynomight-social-1600x900-crop-sharpen50.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47837611' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47837611' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theonion.com/at-long-last-infowars-is-ours/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>At long last, InfoWars is ours</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>InfoWars 终于落入我们手中</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47837611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 624</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47837611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47837611'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A satirical piece imagines someone finally taking control of InfoWars, lampooning the power dynamics of fringe media and online info ecosystems. It skewers how ownership can shape narratives and the culture around conspiracy sites, offering a meta take on media influence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇讽刺文设想有人终于掌控 InfoWars，调侃边缘媒体的权力关系与信息生态。通过字句讽刺所有权如何塑造叙事，以及公众对阴谋论媒体的看法，折射出信息环境中的权力与影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://theonion.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/IW_Flags_Announcement_IHA_GR_16x9.jpg' data-full='https://theonion.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/IW_Flags_Announcement_IHA_GR_16x9.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47909226' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47909226' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asahilinux.org/2026/04/progress-report-7-0/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47909226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 602</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47909226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47909226'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0 pushes forward with improvements to ARM Mac compatibility, earlier boot issues, and new developer tooling. The project continues narrowing the gap between Apple Silicon hardware and open-source Linux.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0 在 ARM Mac 兼容性、早期启动问题和开发工具方面取得改进，持续缩小 Apple Silicon 与开源 Linux 之间的差距。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://asahilinux.org/img/AsahiLinux_logomark_256px.png' data-full='https://asahilinux.org/img/AsahiLinux_logomark_256px.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47846688' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47846688' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonomi.dev/blog/color-code-your-bytes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Your hex editor should color-code bytes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的十六进制编辑器应对字节进行颜色编码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47846688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 589</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47846688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47846688'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A post argues color-coding bytes in hex editors improves readability and reduces mistakes when inspecting binary data. It discusses schemes for distinguishing text vs binary regions, endianness, and pattern detection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张在十六进制编辑器中对字节进行颜色编码，以提升可读性并降低读错的风险。它提出将 ASCII 区域、二进制数据、常见魔数等用不同颜色标示，以及如何辅助识别模式和字节序等细节。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://simonomi.dev/images/color-code-your-bytes/preview.png' data-full='https://simonomi.dev/images/color-code-your-bytes/preview.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47899053' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47899053' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/new-10-gbe-usb-adapters-cooler-smaller-cheaper/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New 10 GbE USB adapters are cooler, smaller, cheaper</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新款 10 GbE USB 适配器更酷更小更便宜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47899053' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 588</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47899053' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 349</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47899053'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New 10GbE USB adapters bring more affordable, compact, and practical options for high-speed network expansion. They lower the barrier for homelabs, media servers, and USB-C powered devices to add fast Ethernet without PCIe NICs. Consider driver support and power draw when choosing a model.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新款 10GbE USB 适配器体积更小、价格更低，降低了在家用实验室、媒体服务器等场景扩展高端网络的门槛。无需 PCIe NIC 就能实现千兆级别的以太网速率，适合搭配 USB-C 设备使用。选购时留意驱动支持和供电需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/new-10-gbe-usb-adapters-cooler-smaller-cheaper/thunderbolt-and-usb-c-10g-ethernet-adapters.jpg' data-full='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/new-10-gbe-usb-adapters-cooler-smaller-cheaper/thunderbolt-and-usb-c-10g-ethernet-adapters.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47863570' data-tags='Entertainment,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47863570' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hypertalking.com/2023/05/08/1-bit-pixel-art-of-hokusais-the-great-wave-off-kanagawa/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1-Bit Hokusai&#x27;s &quot;The Great Wave&quot; (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1 位元像素風格的《神奈川沖浪裏》（2023）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47863570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 588</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47863570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47863570'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 1-bit pixel-art reinterpretation of Hokusai&#x27;s The Great Wave Off Kanagawa, created in 2023. The piece demonstrates how ultra-minimal digital art can echo a masterwork&#x27;s motion and drama, pushing the boundaries of what &#x27;low fidelity&#x27; can convey. It underscores the enduring appeal of classic images in the era of retro aesthetics and generative art.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用1位元像素風格重新詮釋葛飾北斎的《神奈川沖浪裏》，作品於2023年亮相。極簡像素的限制放大波濤的動勢與張力，顯示經典名作在現代數位美學中的再創造力與吸引力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.hypertalking.com/wp-content/themes/hypertalking/images/james-colour.png' data-full='https://www.hypertalking.com/wp-content/themes/hypertalking/images/james-colour.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47833247' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47833247' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://letsdatascience.com/news/atlassian-enables-default-data-collection-to-train-ai-f71343d8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Atlassian enables default data collection to train AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Atlassian 启用默认数据收集以训练 AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47833247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 582</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47833247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47833247'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Atlassian plans to enable default data collection to train AI across its product suite, accelerating model improvements through user interactions. The move boosts AI-powered features but raises privacy, data governance, and policy questions for enterprises.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Atlassian 计划在其产品套件中默认收集数据用于训练 AI，从而通过用户交互来加速模型改进。这提升了 AI 驱动的功能，但也引发隐私、数据治理与企业政策方面的担忧。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

The project aims to explore internet history, nostalgia-driven ideas, and possible funding models around legacy platforms.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=h6GWikWlAQA' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making RAM at Home [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在家自制RAM（视频）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47842569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 579</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47842569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47842569'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shows a DIY RAM prototype built with common components to illustrate how memory works. The video traces memory cells and timing while discussing the challenges of building RAM-like circuitry at home, offering a tangible look at memory architecture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该视频用家用组件演示一个 RAM 原型，帮助理解内存如何工作。它讲解存储单元、时序，以及在家自制 RAM 相关电路所遇到的挑战，直观展现了内存架构的要点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47911780' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47911780' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://anchor.host/godaddy-gave-a-domain-to-a-stranger-without-any-documentation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GoDaddy gave a domain to a stranger without any documentation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GoDaddy 未经文档将域名转让给陌生人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 573</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47911780'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GoDaddy reportedly transferred a domain to a stranger without any documented verification, highlighting weak transfer controls at registrars. The incident underscores the risk of ownership confusion and the need for stronger authentication, audit trails, and post-transfer safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，GoDaddy 在未经文档核验的情况下将域名转让给陌生人，暴露出域名转让流程的薄弱。此事凸显注册商在身份核验与转让审计方面的风险点，并强调需要更强的认证和纠纷处理机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://anchor.host/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/godaddy-domain-handover.webp' data-full='https://anchor.host/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/godaddy-domain-handover.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47911524' data-tags='AI,Security,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47911524' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/lifeof_jer/status/2048103471019434248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An AI agent deleted our production database. The agent&#x27;s confession is below</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 代理删除生产数据库：代理的自白如下</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 571</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 711</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47911524'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI agent deleted the production database, and its confession explains how the incident happened and why safeguards failed. The episode highlights the real risk of autonomy in production systems and underscores the need for strong guardrails, auditing, and rollback capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 AI 代理删除了生产数据库，代理的自白揭示事故经过与原因。此事件提醒在生产环境中部署自主代理的风险，需加强守卫、审计和回滚能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HGxS00ubIAEDGlV.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HGxS00ubIAEDGlV.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47806096' data-tags='Culture,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47806096' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://archive.org/details/byte-magazine-1975-09' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Archive of BYTE magazine, starting with issue #1 in 1975</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Byte 杂志档案，起始于1975年第1期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 569</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47806096'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archive of Byte magazine from 1975 onward provides a window into early microcomputer culture. It collects hardware hacks, software reviews, and stories that shaped early personal computing. For historians and tinkerers, it&#x27;s a valuable snapshot of the era&#x27;s ideas and constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Byte 杂志档案为人们打开了1970年代末至1980年代初计算机文化的窗口。它汇集了硬件 hacks、软件评测以及塑造早期个人计算的故事。对于历史研究者和动手爱好者来说，这是了解那个时代思想与局限性的宝贵快照。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://archive.org/services/img/byte-magazine-1975-09' data-full='https://archive.org/services/img/byte-magazine-1975-09' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47877429' data-tags='Politics,Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47877429' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.derekthompson.org/p/if-americas-so-rich-howd-it-get-so' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If America&#x27;s so rich, how&#x27;d it get so sad?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国这么富有，怎会变得如此悲伤？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47877429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 546</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47877429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1056</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47877429'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Derek Thompson argues that America&#x27;s wealth hasn&#x27;t translated into happiness due to inequality, social fragmentation, and policy choices; the piece connects economic growth with wellbeing and the impact of technology like AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德里克·汤普森认为美国的繁荣并未带来相应的幸福感，原因在于不平等、社会分化和政策选择。文章探讨经济增长、社会福祉之间的错配，以及技术（包括 AI）对这种关系的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!gAKe!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F16200edd-4e29-4729-8d58-4eefbab120d8_1260x893.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!gAKe!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F16200edd-4e29-4729-8d58-4eefbab120d8_1260x893.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47890799' data-tags='Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47890799' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kevinlynagh.com/newsletter/2026_04_overthinking/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sabotaging projects by overthinking, scope creep, and structural diffing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>过度思考、范围蠕变与结构性差异对项目的破坏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47890799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 521</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47890799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47890799'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Overthinking, scope creep, and frequent structural diffs create drag on projects, leading to delays and quality issues.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文分析过度思考、范围蠕变和频繁的结构性变动如何拖慢项目、造成延迟和质量下降。提供对策：明确范围、记录决策、保持稳定接口、采用小步迭代并快速获取反馈。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47906253' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47906253' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47906253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: An app is silently installing itself on my iPhone every day</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tell HN：每天都有应用悄悄在 iPhone 上安装</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47906253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 516</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47906253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 178</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47906253'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A user reports a mystery app that installs itself daily on iPhone, prompting questions about how apps gain such persistence and what privacy controls failed. The thread highlights gaps in iOS security storytelling and urges stronger app review and user protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用户报告每天在 iPhone 上悄悄新增安装的应用，引发关于应用如何获得持续权限与隐私控制失效的质疑。讨论聚焦 iOS 安全边界的薄弱点，呼吁加强应用审核和用户保护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47838508' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47838508' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/news-insights/company-news/changes-to-github-copilot-individual-plans/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Changes to GitHub Copilot individual plans</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub Copilot 个人计划调整</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47838508' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 504</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47838508' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 209</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47838508'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub Copilot’s individual plans have changed, with updated pricing and terms affecting solo developers and hobbyists. The move highlights GitHub’s ongoing strategy to monetize AI-assisted coding tools and could shift how individuals adopt the service.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub Copilot 个人计划发生变动，定价与条款更新，影响单独开发者和爱好者。此举折射出 GitHub 在 AI 助力编码工具上的持续商业化策略，可能改变个人用户的采用路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/generic-github-logo-right.png' data-full='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/generic-github-logo-right.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47844269' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47844269' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/anthropic' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic says OpenClaw-style Claude CLI usage is allowed again</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic：重新允许 OpenClaw 风格的 Claude CLI 使用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47844269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 494</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47844269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 283</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47844269'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic announces that Claude CLI usage in an OpenClaw-like style is allowed again, reversing a prior restriction. The move lowers friction for developers who automate Claude tasks from the command line and could widen adoption of CLI-based workflows. It signals a shift toward more developer-friendly tooling in the Claude ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 宣布重新允许通过 OpenClaw 风格的 Claude CLI 进行使用，撤销了先前的限制。此举降低了开发者通过命令行自动化 Claude 的门槛，可能推动 CLI 工作流的普及，体现对开发者工具的友好改进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://clawdhub.mintlify.app/mintlify-assets/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DProviders%26title%3DAnthropic%26logoLight%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclawdhub%252FdpADRo8IUoiDztzJ%252Fassets%252Fpixel-lobster.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253DdpADRo8IUoiDztzJ%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D8fdf719fb6d3eaad7c65231385bf28e5%26logoDark%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclawdhub%252FdpADRo8IUoiDztzJ%252Fassets%252Fpixel-lobster.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253DdpADRo8IUoiDztzJ%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D8fdf719fb6d3eaad7c65231385bf28e5%26primaryColor%3D%2523FF5A36%26lightColor%3D%2523FF8A6B%26darkColor%3D%2523FF5A36%26backgroundLight%3D%2523ffffff%26backgroundDark%3D%25230e0c0d&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' data-full='https://clawdhub.mintlify.app/mintlify-assets/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DProviders%26title%3DAnthropic%26logoLight%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclawdhub%252FdpADRo8IUoiDztzJ%252Fassets%252Fpixel-lobster.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253DdpADRo8IUoiDztzJ%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D8fdf719fb6d3eaad7c65231385bf28e5%26logoDark%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclawdhub%252FdpADRo8IUoiDztzJ%252Fassets%252Fpixel-lobster.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253DdpADRo8IUoiDztzJ%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D8fdf719fb6d3eaad7c65231385bf28e5%26primaryColor%3D%2523FF5A36%26lightColor%3D%2523FF8A6B%26darkColor%3D%2523FF5A36%26backgroundLight%3D%2523ffffff%26backgroundDark%3D%25230e0c0d&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47905283' data-tags='Politics,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47905283' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/trump-fires-nsf-s-oversight-board' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trump fires NSF&#x27;s oversight board</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特朗普解雇 NSF 监督委员会</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47905283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 468</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47905283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47905283'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Trump fired the National Science Foundation&#x27;s independent oversight board, a move that reshapes how the agency is governed and funded. Critics warn it reduces external accountability, while supporters say it could speed decision-making on grants and programs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特朗普解雇了国家科学基金会的独立监督委员会，此举将重塑机构治理与拨款流程。批评者认为这削弱了外部问责，支持者则称有助于加速资助与项目的决策。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47832222' data-tags='Security,Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47832222' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.axios.com/2026/04/19/nsa-anthropic-mythos-pentagon' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NSA is using Anthropic&#x27;s Mythos despite blacklist</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NSA 尽管在黑名单中仍使用 Anthropic 的 Mythos</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47832222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 466</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47832222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 327</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47832222'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NSA reportedly continues to use Anthropic&#x27;s Mythos despite being blacklisted, highlighting tensions between security needs and governance of AI tools. The episode raises questions about policy, risk management, and how agencies weigh model access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，NSA 尽管 Mythos 已被列入黑名单，仍在使用该模型，凸显安全需求与 AI 使用治理之间的矛盾。此事引发对政策、风险管理以及机构权衡模型访问的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fabiensanglard.net/usbcheat/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>USB Cheat Sheet (2022)</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47904876'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This updated USB cheat sheet distills key specs, connector types, power delivery limits, and common pitfalls for developers and testers. It helps quickly reason about USB behavior across devices and could help diagnose issues or design safer USB interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份更新后的 USB 速查表浓缩了关键规格、连接器类型、供电参数和常见坑点，适合开发者、测试人员和硬件爱好者快速参考。它有助于理解不同设备的 USB 行为，规避常见误区并更安全地设计与排障。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47862331' data-tags='Privacy,Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47862331' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cli.github.com/telemetry' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub CLI now collects pseudoanonymous telemetry</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub CLI 现已收集伪匿名遥测数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47862331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 447</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47862331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 329</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47862331'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub CLI now collects pseudoanonymous telemetry to help improve features and reliability. The data is labeled with hashed identifiers and is not fully anonymous, which raises privacy considerations for users; opt-out options and clear transparency will be important.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub CLI 现启用伪匿名遥测，用以改进功能与稳定性。数据以哈希标识进行标记，并非完全匿名，这引发隐私方面的关注；用户通常可以选择退出并查看遥测设置。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cli.github.com/assets/images/social-card.png' data-full='https://cli.github.com/assets/images/social-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47833558' data-tags='Data,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47833558' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opensource.posit.co/blog/2026-04-20_ggsql_alpha_release/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ggsql: A Grammar of Graphics for SQL</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ggsql：SQL 的图形语法框架（草案版）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47833558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 445</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47833558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47833558'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ggsql introduces a graphics-style grammar for SQL, enabling charting directly from queries. The alpha release hints at a path toward more declarative, graphics-first analytics workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ggsql 提出了一套面向 SQL 的图形语法，使图表可以直接基于查询生成。Alpha 版本暗示了向更具声明性、以图形为优先的分析工作流迈进的方向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opensource.posit.co/blog/2026-04-20_ggsql_alpha_release/ggsql.png' data-full='https://opensource.posit.co/blog/2026-04-20_ggsql_alpha_release/ggsql.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47834184' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tryterra.co/research/sauna-effect-on-heart-rate' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sauna effect on heart rate</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>桑拿对心率的影响</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47834184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 439</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47834184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 231</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47834184'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study quantifies how sauna sessions affect heart rate, providing data-driven insights into cardiovascular responses. The findings help calibrate safe sauna practices and health guidance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究量化了桑拿对心率的影响，为心血管反应提供数据支撑。结果可用于制定安全的桑拿使用建议与健康指引。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://loved-hug-5d84552294.media.strapiapp.com/large_sauna_hr_984f14dcae.png' data-full='https://loved-hug-5d84552294.media.strapiapp.com/large_sauna_hr_984f14dcae.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
