<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47862497' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47862497' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/infrastructure-and-cloud/google-cloud/eighth-generation-tpu-agentic-era/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Our eighth generation TPUs: two chips for the agentic era</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们的第八代TPU：双芯片引领代理时代</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47862497' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 437</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47862497' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 214</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47862497'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google unveils eighth-generation TPUs built as dual-chip modules aimed at supporting agentic AI workloads. The design emphasizes higher throughput and efficiency for large language models and autonomous agents, with cloud availability and potential cost implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌发布第八代TPU，采用双芯片模块，面向代理时代的AI工作负载。该设计强调为大模型和自治代理提供更高吞吐与效率，并讨论了云端部署及潜在的成本影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/two_chips_for_the_agentic_era_hero.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/two_chips_for_the_agentic_era_hero.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47793161' data-tags='Gaming,Programming,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47793161' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kotaku.com/video-game-devs-explain-how-pausing-works-and-sometimes-it-gets-weird-2000686339' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Game devs explain the tricks involved with letting you pause a game</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>游戏开发者揭秘暂停游戏背后的技术技巧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47793161' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 424</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47793161' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 233</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47793161'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pausing a game is more than freezing the screen. It requires carefully suspending the game loop, physics, and timers while keeping audio and other subsystems coherent. In multiplayer and lockstep scenarios, pausing becomes far from trivial, so developers use tricks like time scaling, separate render-only states, or input buffering to ensure a consistent experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>暂停游戏不仅仅是将画面停下。需要精确地暂停游戏循环、物理仿真和计时器，同时保持音频等子系统的连贯。在多人或锁步场景下，暂停远非易事，开发者会使用时间缩放、仅渲染状态分离或输入缓冲等技巧来保证体验的一致性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kotaku.com/app/uploads/2026/04/pauseeee-1200x675.jpg' data-full='https://kotaku.com/app/uploads/2026/04/pauseeee-1200x675.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47867048' data-tags='AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47867048' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://flipbook.page/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Website streamed live directly from a model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>网站直接由模型实时输出内容</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47867048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 423</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47867048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47867048'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A site streams output directly from a live AI model, delivering real-time, model-generated content to viewers. It demonstrates low-latency, streaming-ready architecture for on-demand AI experiences and raises questions about cost and content control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>某站点实现了模型实时输出的流式传输，向观众提供即时的模型生成内容。展示了适用于实时 AI 体验的低延迟架构，同时也引发成本和内容治理的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://flipbook.page/flipbook-unfurl.jpg' data-full='https://flipbook.page/flipbook-unfurl.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47827259' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47827259' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ashley.rolfmore.com/stop-trying-to-engineer-your-way-out-of-listening-to-people/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stop trying to engineer your way out of listening to people</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>停止用工程手段回避倾听他人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47827259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 417</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47827259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47827259'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that listening is the core issue, not process design. It emphasizes feedback loops and user-centric work practices to capture voices, warning that automation can’t replace human listening.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为倾听才是关键问题，而不是简单地改造流程。通过强调反馈循环和更贴近用户的工作方式，文章点出技术解决方案无法替代真实的人类倾听。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1453738773917-9c3eff1db985?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDE2fHxsaXN0ZW58ZW58MHx8fHwxNzcwODU1NDg2fDA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1453738773917-9c3eff1db985?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDE2fHxsaXN0ZW58ZW58MHx8fHwxNzcwODU1NDg2fDA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47866913' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47866913' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nrehiew.github.io/blog/minimal_editing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Over-editing refers to a model modifying code beyond what is necessary</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>过度编辑指模型对代码进行超出必要程度的修改</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 415</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 241</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47866913'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Over-editing describes when an AI model makes more changes to code than necessary, introducing instability and feature creep. The piece advocates minimal editing as a discipline to keep patches focused and maintainable, with practical patterns to avoid overzealous modifications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章提出了“过度编辑”的现象，即模型对代码的修改超出必要范围，容易引入不必要的复杂性。作者主张采用“最小编辑”原则，给出避免过度修改的实践方法和启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nrehiew.github.io/blog/minimal_editing/images/overediting.png' data-full='https://nrehiew.github.io/blog/minimal_editing/images/overediting.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47891019' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47891019' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-04-24/norway-wants-kids-to-be-kids-with-social-media-ban-for-under-16s' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Norway set to become latest country to ban social media for under 16s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>挪威拟成为最新禁止16岁以下使用社交媒体的国家</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47891019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 411</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47891019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 476</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47891019'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Norway is poised to ban social media access for anyone under 16, marking another move in youth-protection policy. The plan would push platforms to enforce age gates or restrict under-16 accounts, prompting questions about enforcement and online rights. If enacted, Norway could influence global debates on digital safety and teen privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>挪威拟对16岁以下用户全面禁用社交媒体，成为持续加强青少年保护政策的一环。该举措将促使平台实施年龄限制或禁止未满16岁的账户，带来执行难点与个人使用权的讨论。若落地，或将影响全球关于数字安全与青少年隐私的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47843434' data-tags='Business,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47843434' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/04/20/business/infowars-alex-jones-the-onion.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Onion to Take over InfoWars</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>The Onion 将接管 InfoWars</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47843434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 407</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47843434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 177</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47843434'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Onion is set to take over InfoWars, a move that blends satire with fringe-media economics. The acquisition could reshape audience, advertising, and policy dynamics in an influential, if controversial, media space.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讽刺媒体《The Onion》拟接管 InfoWars，这一举动将幽默与边缘媒体的商业生态结合起来。此类整合可能改变受众、广告以及平台政策在相关领域的格局。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47877366' data-tags='Security,Data,Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47877366' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/french-govt-agency-confirms-breach-as-hacker-offers-to-sell-data/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>French government agency confirms breach as hacker offers to sell data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国政府机构遭遇数据泄露：黑客提出出售数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47877366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 404</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47877366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47877366'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A French government agency confirms a data breach; a hacker is publicly offering to sell the stolen data. The incident highlights the risk to sensitive citizen data and the ongoing commodification of breached information.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国政府机构确认遭遇数据泄露，攻击者公开提出出售被盗数据。事件暴露了对公民敏感信息的风险，同时凸显被盗数据买卖的普遍性及持续威胁。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2026/04/21/Titres.jpg' data-full='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2026/04/21/Titres.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47824945' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47824945' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/weezerOSINT/status/2045849358462222720' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Notion leaks email addresses of all editors of any public page</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Notion 泄露公开页面编辑者的邮箱地址</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 398</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47824945'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Notion reportedly leaked email addresses for editors of any public page, widening phishing and social-engineering risks for those editors. The exposure underscores how collaboration platforms can inadvertently leak contact data, prompting urgent checks on access, password hygiene, and alerting for targeted scams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Notion 被曝公开页面编辑者的邮箱地址，扩大了他们遭遇钓鱼与社工攻击的风险。此类暴露凸显协作工具对外共享联系信息的潜在隐患，需紧急检查权限、提升密码安全并警惕定向诈骗。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HGRQmjla4AEFzw5.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HGRQmjla4AEFzw5.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47897891' data-tags='Privacy,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47897891' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://itsfoss.com/news/firefox-ships-brave-adblock-engine/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firefox Has Integrated Brave&#x27;s Adblock Engine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Firefox 已集成 Brave 的广告拦截引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47897891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 394</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47897891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 231</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47897891'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox now ships with Brave&#x27;s ad-blocking engine, enabling more aggressive blocklists and better tracking protection by default. The move improves privacy and performance but raises questions about licensing and how this affects the broader adtech ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 现已内置 Brave 的广告拦截引擎，默认提供更强的拦截名单和更好的追踪保护。此举提升了隐私和性能，但也引发关于许可和对更广泛广告技术生态的影响的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://itsfoss.com/content/images/2026/04/firefox-brave-adblock-rust-banner.png' data-full='https://itsfoss.com/content/images/2026/04/firefox-brave-adblock-rust-banner.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47905984' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47905984' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://freakonomics.com/podcast/why-has-there-been-so-little-progress-on-alzheimers-disease/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why has there been so little progress on Alzheimer&#x27;s disease?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何阿尔茨海默病研究进展如此缓慢？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47905984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 393</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47905984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 267</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47905984'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Despite large funding, progress remains limited due to disease complexity, late detection, and trial failures. The episode explores structural bottlenecks in research funding, data sharing, and clinical trial design, and suggests priorities like early biomarkers and smarter trial designs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管投入巨资，阿尔茨海默病研究进展仍受疾病复杂性、早期诊断不足以及临床试验设计等结构性瓶颈制约。节目讨论了研究资助、数据共享等体系性问题，并提出通过早期生物标志物和更高效的试验设计等路径来突破。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47874814' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47874814' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/23/surveillance-vendors-caught-abusing-access-to-telcos-to-track-peoples-phone-locations-researchers-say/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Investigation uncovers two sophisticated telecom surveillance campaigns</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>调查揭露两起高级电信监控行动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47874814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 389</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47874814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47874814'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two sophisticated telecom surveillance campaigns were uncovered, showing how vendors abused access to telcos to track people&#x27;s locations. The exposure highlights private surveillance risks and gaps in vendor governance, raising calls for tighter oversight and stricter data-access controls. It underscores why telecom partnerships and supply-chain security matter for user privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调查揭露两起高级电信监控行动，揭示供应商滥用对运营商的访问来追踪手机位置。曝光点出私营监控的风险与治理漏洞，促使各方呼吁加强监督和更严格的数据访问控制。此事凸显了电信合作与供应链安全在保护用户隐私方面的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/06/cityscape-location-data.jpg?resize=1200,675' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/06/cityscape-location-data.jpg?resize=1200,675' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47839951' data-tags='AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47839951' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stephvee.ca/blog/artificial%20intelligence/ai-resistance-is-growing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Resistance: some recent anti-AI stuff that’s worth discussing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 抵抗：几条值得讨论的近期反AI观点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47839951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 380</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47839951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 406</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47839951'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Highlights a wave of anti-AI sentiment and proposals, from policy debates to industry watchdog actions. The piece argues that despite hype around AI, there are substantive concerns about safety, bias, job displacement, and governance worth debating for builders and operators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>概述对 AI 的抵抗思潮及近期相关主张，包括政策辩论与行业监察行动等。文章指出，尽管 AI 热潮高涨，但在安全、偏见、就业影响和治理等方面存在实质性担忧，值得技术从业者讨论权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47824976' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47824976' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://decipher.sc/2026/04/19/vercel-says-internal-systems-hit-in-breach/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vercel says internal systems hit in breach</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vercel 内部系统遭遇入侵</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824976' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 378</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824976' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47824976'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vercel says its internal systems were breached and it is now investigating the incident. The case highlights ongoing risk to cloud providers’ internal networks, even when customer data remains uncertain, prompting questions about vendor-side security hygiene and incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vercel 表示内部系统遭到入侵，正在进行调查。此事凸显云服务商内部网络的持续风险，即便客户数据尚未明确受到影响，也引发对供应商端安全与应急响应的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47851634' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47851634' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.trendmicro.com/en_us/research/26/d/vercel-breach-oauth-supply-chain.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Vercel breach: OAuth attack exposes risk in platform environment variables</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vercel 事件暴露：OAuth 攻击暴露环境变量风险</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 365</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47851634'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An OAuth-related attack exposed sensitive environment variables in Vercel&#x27;s platform, potentially leaking credentials used by customers. This shows how secret leakage in hosting environments can ripple across the supply chain and compromise downstream services. It reinforces the need for strict secret management, token rotation, and least-privilege access in platform environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次与 OAuth 相关的攻击暴露了 Vercel 平台中的敏感环境变量，可能泄露客户使用的凭证。此事揭示了托管环境中密钥泄露带来的连锁风险，以及对严格秘密管理、令牌轮换和最小权限的必要性。企业应加强环境变量保护与密钥治理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.trendmicro.com/content/dam/trendmicro/global/en/research/thumbnails/26/d/vercel-breach.png' data-full='https://www.trendmicro.com/content/dam/trendmicro/global/en/research/thumbnails/26/d/vercel-breach.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.koshyjohn.com/blog/ai-should-elevate-your-thinking-not-replace-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI should elevate your thinking, not replace it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 应提升你的思考，而非替代它</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 360</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 275</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47913650'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thoughtful piece argues that AI should augment human thinking, not replace it, offering practical guidance on using AI as a cognitive partner while preserving critical judgment and accountability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张 AI 应该提升人类思考，而非替代，提供把 AI 当作认知伙伴使用的实用方法，同时保留批判性判断与问责。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fffff.at/free-universal-construction-kit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Free Universal Construction Kit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>免费通用构造套件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47860198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 359</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47860198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47860198'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open hardware project that provides 3D-printable adapters to connect LEGO bricks with other brands, enabling cross-brand construction. The kit includes downloadable files and community-funded components, showcasing practical interoperability and the spirit of open hardware in playful engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个开源的硬件项目，提供将乐高与其他品牌的搭建系统连接的3D打印适配器，使跨品牌拼搭成为可能。套件包含可下载的设计文件与社区资助的组件，展示了实际互操作性在玩具硬件中的应用，以及对封闭品牌生态的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47835928' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47835928' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/20/deezer-says-44-of-songs-uploaded-to-its-platform-daily-are-ai-generated/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Deezer says 44% of songs uploaded to its platform daily are AI-generated</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Deezer 每日上传音乐中有 44% 为 AI 生成</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 359</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47835928'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Deezer reports that a substantial share of daily uploads are AI-generated, highlighting the tension between AI creativity and licensing. The trend raises questions about rights, attribution, and how platforms manage AI-created content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Deezer 报告每日上传中相当比例为 AI 生成，凸显 AI 创作与版权之间的张力。此趋势引发关于授权、署名与平台如何管理 AI 内容的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Copy-of-2000x1000-EN-1240x600-1.jpg?resize=1200,581' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Copy-of-2000x1000-EN-1240x600-1.jpg?resize=1200,581' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47897647' data-tags='Science,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47897647' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/yuvadm/quantumslop/blob/25ad2e76ae58baa96f6219742459407db9dd17f5/URANDOM_DEMO.md' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Replace IBM Quantum back end with /dev/urandom</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 /dev/urandom 替换 IBM 量子后端</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47897647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 358</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47897647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47897647'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful demo replaces the IBM Quantum backend with /dev/urandom, showing that for some workflows, classical randomness can mimic quantum results well enough to test software. The exercise highlights the importance of trustworthy backends and clear expectations around quantum versus classical randomness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个有趣的演示用 /dev/urandom 来替代 IBM 量子后端，表明在某些工作流中，经典随机性也能在测试阶段近似量子结果。此举强调信任的后端与区分量子与经典随机性的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/460e72a8670f397b24aa552afd8d5ead12b14319527c63118f05491147b74f8f/yuvadm/quantumslop' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/460e72a8670f397b24aa552afd8d5ead12b14319527c63118f05491147b74f8f/yuvadm/quantumslop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47888372' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47888372' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nate.leaflet.pub/3mk4xkaxobc2p' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to be anti-social – a guide to incoherent and isolating social experiences</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何成为反社会的人——关于不连贯且孤立社交体验的指南</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47888372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 358</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47888372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 320</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47888372'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how social dynamics can feel incoherent and isolating, offering observations about why some people retreat from conventional social routines. It isn&#x27;t a how-to for harm; rather it frames the psychology of misaligned social signals and the tech culture around it.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨为何某些人会在社交场合中感到错位与孤立，社会互动的信号错配让人难以建立联系。作者从科技工作者的视角分析常见社交模式与心理反应，强调并非倡导不社交，而是理解背后的原因。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://leaflet.pub/lish/did%3Aplc%3Axbtmt2zjwlrfegqvch7fboei/3lxksvuhk3s2j/3mk4xkaxobc2p/opengraph-image?edc23816ae45539e' data-full='https://leaflet.pub/lish/did%3Aplc%3Axbtmt2zjwlrfegqvch7fboei/3lxksvuhk3s2j/3mk4xkaxobc2p/opengraph-image?edc23816ae45539e' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47893779' data-tags='Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47893779' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21691' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There Will Be a Scientific Theory of Deep Learning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将来会有深度学习的科学理论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47893779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 351</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47893779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47893779'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A theoretical paper argues that deep learning will eventually be understood through a rigorous, testable theory that explains generalization and training dynamics. It outlines guiding principles and discusses how such a theory could steer future research and evaluation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇理论论文认为深度学习终将建立一套连贯、可检验的科学理论，解释泛化与训练动力学。文中提出若干原则，并讨论该理论如何引导未来研究与评估。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47851885' data-tags='Open Source,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47851885' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://britannica11.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Britannica11.org – a structured edition of the 1911 Encyclopædia Britannica</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Britannica11.org：1911 年版《大英百科全书》的结构化版本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 343</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47851885'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Britannica11.org hosts a structured edition of the 1911 Encyclopædia Britannica, making the historical text more searchable and navigable. It demonstrates how retro-digitization can unlock research value and educational use with modern tooling. The project shows how open reconstruction of old reference works benefits today’s learners.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Britannica11.org 将1911年版《大英百科全书》的文本以结构化方式呈现，使之更易检索与导航。该项目展示了以现代工具对历史文本进行再数字化的研究与教育价值。开放重建古籍资料有助于当代学习者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://britannica11.org/title_page.jpg' data-full='https://britannica11.org/title_page.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47871519' data-tags='DevOps,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47871519' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://antiz.fr/blog/archlinux-now-has-a-reproducible-docker-image/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Arch Linux Now Has a Bit-for-Bit Reproducible Docker Image</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Arch Linux 现已提供逐位可重现的 Docker 镜像</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47871519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 340</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47871519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47871519'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Arch Linux now ships a bit-for-bit reproducible Docker image, enabling deterministic builds for CI and debugging. This reduces non-determinism in container creation and helps developers reproduce environments exactly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Arch Linux 现已提供逐位可重现的 Docker 镜像，使构建过程在 CI 和调试中具备可重复性。这样可消除镜像创建中的随机差异，帮助开发者精准重现运行环境。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://antiz.fr/images/pfp.jpg' data-full='https://antiz.fr/images/pfp.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47865661' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47865661' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martinfowler.com/fragments/2026-04-02.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Technical, cognitive, and intent debt</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>马丁·福勒：技术债、认知债与意图债</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47865661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 339</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47865661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47865661'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Martin Fowler frames debt beyond code: technical debt, cognitive debt, and intent debt. He outlines how mental load and misaligned intent erode maintainability, and suggests design and documentation practices to curb them.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>马丁·福勒将债务分为技术债、认知债与意图债三类。认知负担和实现意图不一致会侵蚀可维护性，他建议通过设计清晰的接口、更好地文档来降低风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://martinfowler.com/logo-sq.png' data-full='https://martinfowler.com/logo-sq.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47856535' data-tags='Programming,Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47856535' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://luminousmen.substack.com/p/drunk-post-things-ive-learned-as' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Drunk post: Things I&#x27;ve learned as a senior engineer (2021)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>醉笔记：资深工程师学到的事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47856535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 336</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47856535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 243</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47856535'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Late-night reflections from a seasoned engineer distill practical lessons about code quality, mentorship, and prioritization. It also offers candid observations on avoiding burnout while building reliable systems and nurturing healthy teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一位资深工程师的深夜随笔，浓缩了关于代码质量、导师制、工作优先级与抗 Burnout 的经验教训。文中还就如何在构建可靠系统的同时维护健康的团队氛围提出了真实的观察与建议。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47887334' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47887334' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/matz/spinel' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spinel: Ruby AOT Native Compiler</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Spinel：Ruby 的 AOT 原生编译器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47887334' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 334</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47887334' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47887334'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spinel is an ahead-of-time native compiler for Ruby, precompiling Ruby code into optimized machine code. The compiler promises faster startup times and lower memory usage, benefiting server apps, scripting tools, and embedded deployments. If it matures, Spinel could shift Ruby performance characteristics closer to statically compiled languages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Spinel 是 Ruby 的 AOT 原生编译器，将 Ruby 代码预编译为本地机器码。它有望提高启动速度并降低内存占用，受益于服务器应用、脚本工具和嵌入式部署。若发展成熟，Spinel 可能使 Ruby 的性能更接近静态编译语言。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/be51db131737334760a8eb50a4c32a5ebb7dc2c588316e18e1705d27d3925425/matz/spinel' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/be51db131737334760a8eb50a4c32a5ebb7dc2c588316e18e1705d27d3925425/matz/spinel' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47847324' data-tags='Business,AI,Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47847324' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stratechery.com/2026/tim-cooks-impeccable-timing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tim Cook&#x27;s Impeccable Timing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蒂姆·库克的完美时机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47847324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 334</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47847324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 402</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47847324'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues Tim Cook has timed key moves around AI, privacy, and supply chains to optimize Apple’s trajectory. By aligning product cadence with regulatory and market windows, Apple maintains pricing power and investor confidence in a shifting tech landscape.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为蒂姆·库克在 AI、隐私与供应链等关键节点的时机把握，帮助苹果在快速变化的科技格局中维持定价能力与投资者信心。通过与监管与市场窗口对齐的产品节奏，苹果在竞争中保持领先。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stratechery.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/cook-1.png' data-full='https://stratechery.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/cook-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47857461' data-tags='Culture,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47857461' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47857461' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: I&#x27;m sick of AI everything</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>告诉 HN：我厌倦 AI 无处不在</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47857461' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 331</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47857461' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47857461'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Hacker News tell thread expresses fatigue with AI hype across consumer tech, arguing for more emphasis on robust engineering and practical value. It reflects a broader pushback against overhyped AI features and asks for more meaningful AI workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一则 Tell HN 帖子表达了对 AI 无处不在的厌倦，主张将精力放在扎实的工程与实际价值上，而不是追逐热炒的 AI 功能。该讨论反映了对 AI 控制与实用性之间的更广泛质疑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47822978' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47822978' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/i12bp8/TagTinker' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Edit store price tags using Flipper Zero</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Flipper Zero 修改商店价格标签</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 329</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 305</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47822978'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Editing store price tags with Flipper Zero demonstrates potential tampering of NFC/RFID labels in retail. The project highlights security risks in automated tagging and stresses the need for tamper-evident controls. It serves as a reminder to harden retail IoT and labeling systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>使用 Flipper Zero 编辑商店价格标签演示了零售环境下对近场通信/射频标签的潜在篡改风险。该项目凸显了自动标签的安全隐患，并强调防篡改控制的重要性，提醒业界加强零售物联网和标签系统的安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a635064dfad38e00e0b9b93b5565e8edbccc49fe2c62c157a12dbb4724977997/i12bp8/TagTinker' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a635064dfad38e00e0b9b93b5565e8edbccc49fe2c62c157a12dbb4724977997/i12bp8/TagTinker' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47902525' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47902525' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.matthewbrunelle.com/its-ok-to-use-coding-assistance-tools-to-revive-the-projects-you-never-were-going-to-finish/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using coding assistance tools to revive projects you never were going to finish</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用编码辅助工具重启那些你原本不打算完成的项目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47902525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 328</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47902525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 210</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47902525'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues it&#x27;s okay to use coding assistants to revive projects you weren&#x27;t going to finish. The approach is pragmatic: carve out a minimal viable scaffold quickly, establish clear goals, and iterate in small steps. Maintain awareness of long-term maintainability and debt as you revive and ship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为使用编程辅助工具来重启原本打算放弃的项目是可以接受的做法。策略上强调务实：快速搭建最小可行框架、设定清晰目标，并分阶段迭代；在重振项目的同时，也要留意维护性和技术债务。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.matthewbrunelle.com/content/images/2026/04/feishin_play_sub_standard.png' data-full='https://blog.matthewbrunelle.com/content/images/2026/04/feishin_play_sub_standard.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.adriankrebs.ch/blog/design-slop/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scoring Show HN submissions for AI design patterns</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 AI 设计模式对 Show HN 提交打分</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47864393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 328</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47864393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 233</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47864393'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A design-focused critique proposes scoring Show HN submissions using AI design patterns, offering a rubric to evaluate modularity, data flows, and safety. The idea is to give more structured, design-conscious feedback on community demos.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种以设计为导向的评论提出用 AI 设计模式对 Show HN 提交进行评分，提供评估模块化、数据流和安全等要点的标准。旨在为社区展示提供更有结构的反馈。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47822486' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://madhadron.com/programming/seven_ur_languages.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The seven programming ur-languages (2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编程之祖：七种原始语言（2022）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 327</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47822486'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post surveys seven foundational languages that shaped modern programming, tracing how concepts from these roots influence today’s language design and tooling. It also reflects on what we gain by revisiting these roots, and which ideas still matter for building robust, expressive software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文梳理了七种塑造现代编程的基础语言，追溯其思想如何影响当今的语言设计与工具链。回顾根源可以帮助理解当代语言的优缺点，哪些理念在构建健壮且高效的软件时仍然重要。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47836730' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47836730' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vivianvoss.net/blog/why-we-accepted-surveillance' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We accepted surveillance as default</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们已默认接受监控</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47836730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 325</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47836730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47836730'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Traces how everyday technology—phones, apps, smart devices—normalizes surveillance as a cost of convenience. The piece argues for rethinking consent, data minimization, and regulatory safeguards to reclaim privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析日常科技产品如何使监控成为便利成本的一部分。呼吁重新审视用户同意、数据最小化与监管保障，以重获隐私控制权。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vivianvoss.net/assets/images/vivianvossnet-logo-short.png' data-full='https://vivianvoss.net/assets/images/vivianvossnet-logo-short.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47835775' data-tags='Business,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47835775' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.androidauthority.com/amazon-kindle-2026-3657863/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Not buying another Kindle</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不再买下一台 Kindle</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835775' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 323</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835775' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 273</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47835775'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that new Kindle models may not justify upgrade costs amid marginal gains in display tech and features. The piece weighs ecosystem lock-in, price, and privacy considerations, suggesting readers pause and compare alternatives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为新一代 Kindle 的升级成本可能得不偿失，显示技术与功能提升有限。作者分析生态锁定、价格与隐私等因素，建议读者暂停跟进并对比其他选项。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.androidauthority.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/11/Amazon-Kindle-Oasis-with-warmth-and-brightness-slider.jpg' data-full='https://www.androidauthority.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/11/Amazon-Kindle-Oasis-with-warmth-and-brightness-slider.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47894747' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47894747' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hhh.hn/rodecaster-duo-fw/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My audio interface has SSH enabled by default</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的音频接口默认启用 SSH</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47894747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 320</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47894747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47894747'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers found that the audio interface ships with SSH enabled by default, exposing remote access on the same network. This kind of vendor-default service can create attack surfaces; users should disable SSH or update firmware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>音频接口默认开启 SSH，若设备在同一网络中，攻击者可能取得远程访问权限。这类厂商默认开启的服务暴露了攻击面，用户应禁用 SSH 或尽快升级固件。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hhh.hn/images/desk.JPG' data-full='https://hhh.hn/images/desk.JPG' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47833156' data-tags='Business,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47833156' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rts.ch/info/monde/2026/article/tesla-dissimule-des-milliers-d-incidents-de-conduite-autonome-mortels-29214161.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla concealed fatal accidents to continue testing autonomous driving</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉隐瞒致命事故以继续测试自动驾驶</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47833156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 320</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47833156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47833156'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Allegations claim Tesla concealed fatal autonomous-driving incidents to keep testing ongoing. The report raises questions about safety oversight, corporate incentives, and the path to scalable, trustworthy self-driving tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称特斯拉在测试自动驾驶时隐瞒致命事故以维持测试进度。此事引发对安全监管、企业激励以及实现可扩展且可信的自动驾驶技术路径的质疑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://img.rts.ch/articles/2026/image/50gomb-29214558.image?w=1200&amp;h=630' data-full='https://img.rts.ch/articles/2026/image/50gomb-29214558.image?w=1200&amp;h=630' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47885596' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1069399/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ubuntu 26.04</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ubuntu 26.04 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47885596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47885596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 248</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47885596'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ubuntu 26.04 arrives as a long-term release with updated kernels and desktop components, plus enterprise-oriented improvements. It emphasizes stability, security, and hardware support, serving as a reliable base for both desktops and servers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ubuntu 26.04 作为长期版本发布，带来内核与桌面组件的更新以及面向企业的改进。强调稳定性、安全性，并提升对新硬件的支持，适合作为桌面与服务器的长期基线。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47914350' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/sport/athletics/articles/crm1m7e0zwzo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sawe becomes first athlete to run a sub-two-hour marathon in a competitive race</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>萨韦在正式比赛中实现马拉松2小时内的里程碑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47914350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 317</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47914350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 217</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47914350'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sawe reportedly becomes the first athlete to run a sub-two-hour marathon in a competitive race, a landmark that presses the boundaries of human endurance. The feat highlights advances in training, pacing, and data-driven preparation enabled by modern sports tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称萨韦在正式比赛中首次实现马拉松两小时内的里程碑，挑战人类极限。此成就反映出训练、 pacing 与数据驱动的备战在当代体育科技中的进步。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_sport/1200/cpsprodpb/b363/live/5c67a4a0-4163-11f1-8a25-27287a9e092d.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_sport/1200/cpsprodpb/b363/live/5c67a4a0-4163-11f1-8a25-27287a9e092d.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tech.marksblogg.com/american-solar-farms-v2.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>3.4M Solar Panels</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47862386'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new US solar farm project uses 3.4 million panels, underscoring the scale of modern utility-scale renewables.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国一处大型太阳能农场部署了340万块面板，凸显了现代公用事业级可再生能源的规模。文章讨论了面板密度、土地使用和并网对成本、容量与建设周期的影响。</p>
