<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47838703' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kimi.com/blog/kimi-vendor-verifier' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kimi vendor verifier – verify accuracy of inference providers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kimi 厂商验证器：验证推理提供商的准确性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47838703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 308</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47838703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47838703'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kimi launches a vendor verifier to assess the accuracy of AI inference providers, addressing reliability concerns in enterprise AI purchases. The tool likely standardizes benchmarks and governance checks, helping buyers compare model quality across vendors. As enterprises lean on third‑party inference, verifiable performance becomes a strategic gatekeeper.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kimi 推出厂商验证器，用以评估 AI 推理提供商的准确性，解决企业在采购时对可靠性的担忧。该工具可能提供标准化的基准与治理检查，帮助买家对比不同供应商的模型质量。随着企业对推理能力的依赖，具可验证性能的能力变得日益关键。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47907130' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47907130' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://juraj.bednar.io/en/blog-en/2026/04/17/eu-age-control-the-trojan-horse-for-digital-ids/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU Age Control: The trojan horse for digital IDs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟年龄控制：数字身份的特洛伊木马</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47907130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 307</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47907130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47907130'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU age-control rules could become a backdoor into broad digital identity systems, forcing platforms to collect or verify identity beyond age. Privacy and accessibility concerns accompany the push.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟的年龄控制规则可能成为通往广泛数字身份体系的特洛伊木马，推动平台在年龄之外收集或验证身份。此举伴随隐私和可访问性方面的担忧。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47823270' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47823270' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Apr/18/opus-system-prompt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Changes in the system prompt between Claude Opus 4.6 and 4.7</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Opus 4.6 与 4.7 的系统提示变更</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47823270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 307</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47823270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47823270'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes updates to the model&#x27;s system prompt between Claude Opus 4.6 and 4.7, showcasing how tiny prompt design changes can affect behavior, safety, and output style. The note emphasizes the importance of prompt engineering on LLMs and how platform updates can shift capabilities and guardrails.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了 Claude Opus 4.6 与 4.7 的系统提示变更，展示了微小的提示设计差异对模型行为、安全边界和输出风格的影响。强调提示工程在大型语言模型中的重要性，以及平台更新如何改变能力与约束。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47825160' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47825160' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcgamer.com/games/world-of-warcraft/turtle-wow-classic-server-announces-shutdown-after-blizzard-wins-injunction/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turtle WoW classic server announces shutdown after Blizzard wins injunction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在暴雪胜诉禁令后 Turtle WoW 经典服务器宣布关闭</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47825160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 305</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47825160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 280</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47825160'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Turtle WoW, a private World of Warcraft Classic server, announced shutdown after Blizzard won an injunction. The move underscores IP enforcement dynamics in the private-server space and the ongoing tension between fan-run servers and game publishers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在暴雪获得禁令后，Turtle WoW 经典私服宣布关闭。这凸显了私服领域在知识产权执法方面的压力，以及玩家社区与发行商之间的持续博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/FMANT4DGMrKnDjrSVT8sui-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/FMANT4DGMrKnDjrSVT8sui-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47840980' data-tags='AI,Business,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47840980' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.adweek.com/media/exclusive-leaked-deck-reveals-stackadapts-playbook-for-chatgpt-ads/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI ad partner now selling ChatGPT ad placements based on “prompt relevance”</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 广告合作伙伴现依据“提示相关性”销售 ChatGPT 广告位</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47840980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 300</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47840980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47840980'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An OpenAI ad partner is reportedly selling ChatGPT ad placements based on prompt relevance, suggesting a shift to contextualized advertising within AI chat experiences. The leaked deck hints at programmable targeting that could reshape how brands buy AI-native inventory. This raises questions about measurement, privacy, and monetization in AI advertising.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，OpenAI 的广告合作伙伴正基于“提示相关性”销售 ChatGPT 广告位，标志着在 AI 聊天体验中向情境化广告的转变。泄露的演示文稿暗示了可编程的定向策略，可能改变品牌对 AI 原生广告位的购买方式。此举引发对衡量、隐私与 AI 广告营收模式的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static-www.adweek.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/05/open-ai-data-chatgpt-2025.jpg?w=600&amp;h=315&amp;crop=1' data-full='https://static-www.adweek.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/05/open-ai-data-chatgpt-2025.jpg?w=600&amp;h=315&amp;crop=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47836784' data-tags='Security,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47836784' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://words.filippo.io/128-bits/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Quantum Computers Are Not a Threat to 128-Bit Symmetric Keys</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>量子计算对128位对称密钥并非威胁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47836784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 299</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47836784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47836784'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece explains that quantum computers are not an immediate risk to 128-bit symmetric keys; Grover&#x27;s algorithm would require impractical resources to break them soon. It reassures cryptographers about near-term security margins.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章解释量子计算并非对128位对称密钥的直接威胁；利用 Grover 算法在短期内要实现破解需要巨大的资源。为近期香港的安全裕度提供安抚作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/4de912cf-5147-4672-9800-4510d734d5ec.jpeg?w=960&amp;fit=max' data-full='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/4de912cf-5147-4672-9800-4510d734d5ec.jpeg?w=960&amp;fit=max' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47829486' data-tags='Politics,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47829486' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cge0grppe3po' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The insider trading suspicions looming over Trump&#x27;s presidency</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特朗普任期内的内幕交易嫌疑笼罩</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47829486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 299</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47829486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 151</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47829486'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Regulators are scrutinizing potential insider trading during Trump&#x27;s presidency, examining stock trades around major announcements. The focus reflects tensions between political activity and securities laws, with implications for accountability and market integrity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>监管机构正在审查特朗普任期内是否存在内幕交易，重点关注重大公告前后的股票交易。此事反映政治活动与证券法之间的合规风险，以及对市场公信力的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/974f/live/6a5a6a20-3c95-11f1-9d5c-8ba507d7dbde.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/974f/live/6a5a6a20-3c95-11f1-9d5c-8ba507d7dbde.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47897681' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47897681' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unsung.aresluna.org/plain-text-has-been-around-for-decades-and-its-here-to-stay/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Plain text has been around for decades and it’s here to stay</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纯文本已存在数十年且将继续存在</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47897681' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 297</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47897681' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47897681'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Plain text remains the most portable, durable format for long-term data and interoperability. Even as rich formats proliferate, plain text underpins archival resilience, tooling, and cross-system compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纯文本仍是最便携、最耐久的数据格式，适用于长期存档与互操作性。尽管涌现出丰富的格式，纯文本在可存档性、工具兼容性等方面依然占据核心地位。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://unsung.aresluna.org/_media/plain-text-has-been-around-for-decades-and-its-here-to-stay/ogimage.png' data-full='https://unsung.aresluna.org/_media/plain-text-has-been-around-for-decades-and-its-here-to-stay/ogimage.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47831437' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47831437' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.flyingpenguin.com/build-an-openclaw-free-secure-always-on-local-ai-agent/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenClaw isn&#x27;t fooling me. I remember MS-DOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenClaw 骗不了我，我记得 MS-DOS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47831437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 296</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47831437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 316</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47831437'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece casts doubt on OpenClaw, arguing for the virtues of simpler, offline computing reminiscent of MS-DOS. It also stresses the need for rigorous security when deploying local AI agents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章对 OpenClaw 持保留态度，强调简洁离线计算的优点，类似于 MS-DOS 的思路。同时强调在本地部署 AI 代理时需要强有力的安全设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.flyingpenguin.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/OpenClaw-Shell-500x273.png' data-full='https://www.flyingpenguin.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/OpenClaw-Shell-500x273.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47839835' data-tags='Politics,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47839835' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://warontherocks.com/cogs-of-war/the-f-35-is-a-masterpiece-built-for-the-wrong-war/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>F-35 is built for the wrong war</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>F-35：为错误的战争打造的杰作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47839835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 294</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47839835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 675</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47839835'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique argues the F-35 program is optimized for a hypothetical war that doesn’t reflect current threat realities, focusing on cost, interoperability, and doctrine alignment. The piece invites reevaluation of defense procurement and emphasizes the need for open, adaptable systems. For tech readers, it’s a case study in aligning hardware programs with real-world use cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 F-35 项目为一种与现实威胁不符的假设战争进行了优化，批评成本、互操作性与作战理念的错配。该观点推动对国防采购的再评估，强调需要更开放、可适应的系统。对于科技读者而言，这是一个将硬件计划与真实应用对齐的案例研究。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://warontherocks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/9513271.jpg?v=1776697753' data-full='https://warontherocks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/9513271.jpg?v=1776697753' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47834689' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47834689' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://mnt.stanleylieber.com/reform/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MNT Reform is an open hardware laptop, designed and assembled in Germany</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MNT Reform：在德国设计并组装的开放硬件笔记本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47834689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 294</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47834689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47834689'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MNT Reform is an open hardware laptop with full public documentation, allowing users to build, customize, or repair the device. Made and assembled in Germany, the project emphasizes repairability, transparency, and user freedom in hardware beyond sealed consumer devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这款开放硬件笔记本提供完整的公开文档、材料清单与装配指导，用户可自行组装、定制或维修。德国制造的该项目强调在供应链日益复杂的背景下，硬件的可修复性与透明度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://mnt.stanleylieber.com/reform/img/mnt_reform.png' data-full='http://mnt.stanleylieber.com/reform/img/mnt_reform.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47872306' data-tags='Hardware,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47872306' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://craigmod.com/essays/ipad_neo/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MacBook Neo and how the iPad should be</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MacBook Neo 与 iPad 的未来方向</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47872306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 293</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47872306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 172</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47872306'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Craig Mod&#x27;s essay argues about how the MacBook Neo and iPad lines should converge or diverge, examining hardware and software decisions to balance portability, productivity, and ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨 MacBook Neo 与 iPad 的未来定位，分析硬件与软件取舍，以在便携性、生产力与生态系统之间寻求平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://craigmod.com/images/about/craigmod-headshot-2025-small-01.jpg' data-full='https://craigmod.com/images/about/craigmod-headshot-2025-small-01.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47882697' data-tags='Show HN,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47882697' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/refactoringhq/tolaria' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Tolaria – Open-source macOS app to manage Markdown knowledge bases</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tolaria：用于管理 Markdown 知识库的开源 macOS 应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47882697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 292</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47882697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 132</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47882697'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tolaria is an open-source macOS app for managing Markdown knowledge bases. It offers local-first storage, markdown editing, and linking between notes to create a searchable knowledge graph. For researchers and developers who want offline, portable knowledge management, it&#x27;s a compelling option.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tolaria 是一个开源的 macOS 应用，用来管理 Markdown 知识库。它支持本地存储、Markdown 编辑，以及笔记之间的连结，方便构建可搜索的知识体系。对需要离线、可移植笔记管理的开发者和研究者很有吸引力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ec96e31f399a44083038d5fe932890fd0783ef728a7e9016e1be2131e0b19572/refactoringhq/tolaria' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ec96e31f399a44083038d5fe932890fd0783ef728a7e9016e1be2131e0b19572/refactoringhq/tolaria' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47874647' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47874647' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/russellromney/honker' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Honker – Postgres NOTIFY/LISTEN Semantics for SQLite</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Honker：SQLite 实现 Postgres NOTIFY/LISTEN 语义</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47874647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 288</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47874647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47874647'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Honker brings Postgres NOTIFY/LISTEN semantics to SQLite, enabling real-time event signaling without a full Postgres server. It demonstrates how lightweight, embedded databases can emulate cross-database messaging patterns, useful for event-driven UIs and local apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Honker 将 PostgreSQL 的 LISTEN/NOTIFY 实时通知语义带到 SQLite，使嵌入式应用能够在不依赖完整 Postgres 的情况下实现事件驱动通信。它为本地应用提供了轻量级的事件总线，适合实现实时 UI 更新等场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/06f24fe7bae4ecc67429ed8fdda67108db8902c2722ac84353ff9b411f871053/russellromney/honker' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/06f24fe7bae4ecc67429ed8fdda67108db8902c2722ac84353ff9b411f871053/russellromney/honker' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47870901' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47870901' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/introducing-openai-privacy-filter/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI Privacy Filter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 隐私过滤器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47870901' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 285</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47870901' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47870901'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI introduces a privacy filter designed to scrub personal data from prompts and responses, helping users keep sensitive information out of training data and shared logs. The feature aligns with increasing regulatory and user demand for privacy-preserving AI interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 推出隐私过滤器，旨在从提示和回复中去除个人敏感信息，帮助用户避免将数据用于训练或公开日志。该功能回应日益增长的隐私合规与用户对隐私保护的需求。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47884768' data-tags='Politics,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47884768' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.orwellfoundation.com/the-orwell-foundation/orwell/essays-and-other-works/why-i-write/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why I Write (1946)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我为何写作（1946）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47884768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 284</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47884768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47884768'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Orwell explains that writers are driven by four motives—ego, aesthetic pleasure, historical impulse, and political purpose—and argues that writing should aim to illuminate truth and resist tyranny. The piece remains a candid meditation on why serious writing matters in a world of rising propaganda.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>奥威尔在文中提出写作源于四种动机：自我、审美、历史冲动和政治目的，并主张写作应以揭示真相、抵制暴政为目标。此篇对在极权与宣传日益高涨的时代，写作为何仍然重要给出直率的思考。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47847558' data-tags='Show HN,Web,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47847558' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vidstudio.app/video-editor' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: VidStudio, a browser based video editor that doesn&#x27;t upload your files</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>VidStudio：基于浏览器、在本地处理的视频编辑器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47847558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 284</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47847558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47847558'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>VidStudio offers browser-based video editing with client-side processing, so your files never leave your device. It demonstrates that privacy-focused web apps can rival traditional editors, though real-world performance and feature parity depend on ongoing Web platform progress. Early demos suggest strong potential for offline work and rapid iteration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>VidStudio 提供基于浏览器的视频编辑，文件全部在本地处理，不会上传云端。此类隐私友好网络应用正逐步接近传统编辑器的功能，实际性能与功能仍取决于网页平台的持续进展。早期演示显示在离线工作和快速迭代方面具有潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vidstudio.app/og-image.png' data-full='https://vidstudio.app/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47892291' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47892291' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/libsdl-org/SDL/pull/15377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SDL Now Supports DOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SDL 现已支持 DOS 环境</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47892291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 283</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47892291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47892291'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SDL now supports DOS environments, enabling legacy DOS apps to run with modern SDL features. This broadens cross-platform reach for retro games and tooling built on SDL, and could simplify porting older multimedia software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SDL 现已支持 DOS 环境，使基于 SDL 的旧 DOS 应用能够在现代系统上运行。此举拓展了向后兼容性，有助于复兴复古游戏和相关工具的移植与开发。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/130a8ef78cb07547e2b2b35f1d127906aa493471d2a583e2c0e4352eb2f1006e/libsdl-org/SDL/pull/15377' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/130a8ef78cb07547e2b2b35f1d127906aa493471d2a583e2c0e4352eb2f1006e/libsdl-org/SDL/pull/15377' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本宫古以东约100公里发生7.4级地震</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47832248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 283</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47832248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47832248'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An earthquake of magnitude 7.4 occurred roughly 100 km ENE of Miyako, Japan. Seismic monitoring and aftershock planning are ongoing, with authorities advising preparedness for potential tremors and damage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本宫古以东约100公里发生7.4级地震。地震监测与余震预警正在进行，相关部门提醒公众保持警惕并做好防灾准备。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47844431' data-tags='DevOps,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47844431' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://webmatrices.com/post/how-a-roblox-cheat-and-one-ai-tool-brought-down-vercel-s-entire-platform' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Roblox cheat and one AI tool brought down Vercel&#x27;s platform</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一起 Roblox 作弊和一个 AI 工具导致 Vercel 平台宕机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47844431' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 281</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47844431' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47844431'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Roblox cheat and an AI tool reportedly caused a cascading outage on Vercel’s platform, illustrating how misuse and automation tools can ripple through a cloud service. The incident underscores the fragility of complex, automated deployments and the importance of robust abuse controls and mitigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一起 Roblox 作弊脚本和一个 AI 工具事件据称导致 Vercel 平台发生大范围崩溃，暴露了滥用与自动化工具对云服务的连锁影响。此事凸显了复杂自动部署的脆弱性，以及加强滥用防护与缓解措施的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47822414' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47822414' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/ai-artificial-intelligence/914672/the-ram-shortage-could-last-years' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The RAM shortage could last years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RAM短缺可能持续数年</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 280</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 313</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47822414'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RAM shortages driven by surging AI workloads and ongoing supply-chain constraints may persist for years, keeping memory prices volatile. Consumers could see higher prices and tighter availability for PCs and consumer devices, while data centers face tougher procurement as demand stays elevated. The situation underscores how AI demand can reshape even core hardware markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RAM 短缺可能持续多年，AI、云计算对内存的需求持续高位，叠加产能瓶颈。对消费电子和服务器市场将带来价格与库存的持续压力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/12/STKS523_RAM_SHORTAGE_B.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/12/STKS523_RAM_SHORTAGE_B.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47831742' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47831742' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.politico.eu/article/eu-brussels-launched-age-checking-app-hackers-say-took-them-2-minutes-break-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Brussels launched an age checking app. Hackers took 2 minutes to break it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>布鲁塞尔推出年龄验证应用，黑客两分钟就破解了</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47831742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 279</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47831742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 179</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47831742'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hackers reportedly cracked Brussels’ age-check app within two minutes, exposing vulnerabilities in a government digital tool. The breach underscores the difficulty of designing robust privacy- and safety-focused checks and raises questions about deployment practices and responsible disclosure. It serves as a cautionary tale for public‑sector app rollouts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称黑客在两分钟内破解了布鲁塞尔的年龄验证应用，暴露政府数字工具的脆弱性。此事件凸显在隐私与安全检查方面设计的挑战，也引发对部署实践与披露责任的讨论。对公共部门应用上线具有警示作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.politico.eu/cdn-cgi/image/width=1200,height=630,fit=crop,quality=80,onerror=redirect/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/17/GettyImages-1829285149-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://www.politico.eu/cdn-cgi/image/width=1200,height=630,fit=crop,quality=80,onerror=redirect/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/17/GettyImages-1829285149-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47866750' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zed.dev/blog/parallel-agents' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Parallel agents in Zed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zed 的并行代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47866750'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zed unveils parallel agents architecture that lets multiple AI agents run concurrently within pipelines. This boosts throughput and enables more complex collaboration, while raising questions about coordination and resource management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zed 公布并行代理架构，允许多个 AI 代理在工作流中同时并发运行。提升吞吐与协作复杂性，但也带来协作、状态与资源管理等挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://zed.dev/img/post/parallel-agents/thumbnail.webp' data-full='https://zed.dev/img/post/parallel-agents/thumbnail.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://petapixel.com/2026/04/22/flickr-the-first-and-last-great-photo-platform/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flickr: The first and last great photo platform</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flickr：第一代也是最后一代伟大的照片平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47867473' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 276</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47867473' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47867473'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on Flickr&#x27;s rise as a community-driven photo platform and its decline, drawing lessons about openness, data portability, and the trade-offs between openness and monetization in online communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 Flickr 在社区驱动的照片平台时代的崛起及其衰落，揭示开放性、数据可移植性，以及开放性与商业化之间在在线社区中的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47878117' data-tags='AI,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47878117' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.meshcore.io/2026/04/23/the-split' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MeshCore development team splits over trademark dispute and AI-generated code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MeshCore 开发团队因商标纠纷与 AI 生成代码分裂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47878117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47878117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 175</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47878117'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MeshCore&#x27;s dev team splits amid a trademark dispute and debates over AI-generated code, highlighting tensions between IP ownership and the governance of machine-generated outputs. This quarrel signals how legal questions around branding and code provenance can reshape internal teams and project direction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MeshCore 的开发团队因商标争议与对 AI 生成代码的治理分歧而分裂，反映出知识产权归属与机器生成内容管理之间的紧张关系。此事或将影响团队组织与项目走向，揭示企业需要尽早就品牌与代码来源达成共识。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.meshcore.io/assets/images/icon.png' data-full='https://blog.meshcore.io/assets/images/icon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47848276' data-tags='Business,Startups,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47848276' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/20/anthropic-takes-5b-from-amazon-and-pledges-100b-in-cloud-spending-in-return/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic takes $5B from Amazon and pledges $100B in cloud spending in return</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 获 Amazon 投资50亿美元，承诺云端支出1000亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47848276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 274</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47848276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 289</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47848276'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic raises $5B from Amazon in a deal tied to cloud usage, with Anthropic pledging to spend up to $100B on Amazon cloud. The arrangement signals deep reliance on one cloud provider and could shape AI workloads&#x27; deployment, pricing, and reliability considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 获得亚马逊 50 亿美元投资，并承诺在亚马逊云端支出最多 1000 亿美元。该交易显示双方在云和 AI 服务上的深度绑定，可能影响未来 AI 工作负载的部署、定价与可靠性考量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/11/GettyImages-1445867611.jpg?w=1024' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/11/GettyImages-1445867611.jpg?w=1024' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47910388' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47910388' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/why-we-no-longer-evaluate-swe-bench-verified/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SWE-bench Verified no longer measures frontier coding capabilities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SWE-bench Verified 不再衡量前沿编码能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47910388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 272</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47910388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47910388'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SWE-bench Verified no longer measures frontier coding capabilities because the metric diverges from real-world coding tasks and can be gamed. The change signals a shift toward more robust, meaningful benchmarks for AI-assisted coding tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SWE-bench Verified 不再用于衡量前沿编码能力，因为该指标与实际编码任务脱节且易被操控。此举推动评测向更稳健、具备实际意义的基准转变，影响 AI 辅助编码工具的评估方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47877644' data-tags='DevOps,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47877644' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.githubstatus.com/incidents/myrbk7jvvs6p' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Incident with multple GitHub services</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 多项服务故障事件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47877644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 267</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47877644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47877644'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub reports an incident affecting multiple services, impacting developers&#x27; workflows. The incident underscores the fragility of cloud‑hosted DevOps tools and the importance of robust incident response and contingency planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 因多项服务出现故障，影响开发者工作流。这起事件凸显云端开发工具的脆弱性以及健全的应急响应和备份计划的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47908833' data-tags='Design,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47908833' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://statecharts.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Statecharts: hierarchical state machines</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>状态图：分层状态机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47908833' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 265</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47908833' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47908833'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Statecharts extend traditional state machines with hierarchy and parallel composition, enabling modeling of complex systems with reusable components. They improve readability and verification of software behavior in UI, robotics, and protocol design; widely used in design of embedded systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>状态图在传统状态机之上引入了层次结构和并行组合，便于用可重用的组件来建模复杂系统。它提升了对软件行为的可读性与验证性，广泛用于嵌入式、UI、机器人和协议设计等场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://statecharts.dev/on-off.svg' data-full='https://statecharts.dev/on-off.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47855565' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47855565' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bsky.app/profile/edzitron.com/post/3mjzxwfx3qs2a' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code to be removed from Pro Tier?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 将从 Pro 版移除？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47855565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 265</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47855565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47855565'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post hints that Claude Code may be removed from the Pro tier, signaling a shift in access and pricing for coding-focused AI features. If true, it could affect developers who rely on higher-tier AI-assisted code generation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>帖子暗示 Claude Code 可能会从 Pro 版移除，标志着对代码生成功能的获取权限与定价的调整。若属实，将影响依赖这类功能的开发者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.bsky.app/img/feed_thumbnail/plain/did:plc:qc6xzgctorfsm35w6i3vdebx/bafkreiddpkqpxwsi42rgzrktvrzurcwjcmwbegqouwd5u3qsz5c5c4drte' data-full='https://cdn.bsky.app/img/feed_thumbnail/plain/did:plc:qc6xzgctorfsm35w6i3vdebx/bafkreiddpkqpxwsi42rgzrktvrzurcwjcmwbegqouwd5u3qsz5c5c4drte' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47894435' data-tags='Culture,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47894435' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blogs.loc.gov/picturethis/2026/04/the-classic-american-diner/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Classic American Diner</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>经典美式餐馆</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47894435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 262</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47894435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47894435'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Library of Congress piece examines the iconography and history of the classic American diner, from stainless interiors to neon signage, and its role in 20th-century American life.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾经典美式餐馆的历史与标志性设计，如不锈钢内饰和霓虹招牌，以及它们在20世纪美国生活中的文化地位。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogs.loc.gov/picturethis/files/2026/04/service-pnp-mrg-00100-00102v.jpg' data-full='https://blogs.loc.gov/picturethis/files/2026/04/service-pnp-mrg-00100-00102v.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47858939' data-tags='Science,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47858939' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paraloid_B-72' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Paraloid B-72</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Paraloid B-72树脂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47858939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 262</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47858939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47858939'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paraloid B-72 is a widely used acrylic resin in art conservation, serving as a consolidant and varnish due to its clarity, reversibility, and chemical stability. It remains a topic of ongoing discussions around aging, solvent compatibility, and long-term stability in museum environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Paraloid B-72 是艺术品保护中广泛使用的丙烯酸树脂，用作固色剂和清漆，因其清晰、可逆性和化学稳定性而备受推崇。但关于其老化、溶剂兼容性及长期稳定性的问题仍在研究和讨论中。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/b6/Paraloid_B-72.png/1280px-Paraloid_B-72.png' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/b6/Paraloid_B-72.png/1280px-Paraloid_B-72.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47885377' data-tags='Science,Health,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47885377' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/s41467-026-71264-8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Habitual coffee intake shapes the microbiome, modifies physiology and cognition</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日常咖啡摄入改变微生物群、影响生理与认知</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47885377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 261</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47885377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 252</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47885377'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Habitual coffee intake reshapes the gut microbiome and alters physiological markers and cognitive function. The study links daily caffeine consumption to specific microbial shifts and metabolic changes, with potential implications for energy, mood, and brain health.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>规律性咖啡摄入会重塑肠道微生物群、改变生理指标并影响认知功能。研究将日常咖啡因摄入与特定微生物变化及代谢改变联系起来，可能对能量、情绪和大脑健康产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41467-026-71264-8/MediaObjects/41467_2026_71264_Fig1_HTML.png' data-full='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41467-026-71264-8/MediaObjects/41467_2026_71264_Fig1_HTML.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47841129' data-tags='Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47841129' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://isaaccorbrey.com/notes/jujutsu-megamerges-for-fun-and-profit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jujutsu megamerges for fun and profit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>咒术大并购：为了乐趣与利润</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47841129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 261</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47841129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 163</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47841129'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post uses a Jungian metaphor of jujutsu megamergers to examine large-scale M&amp;A activity, weighing potential synergies against integration risk and regulatory friction. It argues that while consolidation can unlock value, misaligned deals often destroy more than they create. For readers, it’s a lens on strategic playbooks in tech markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章以‘咒术’隐喻探讨大型并购活动，权衡潜在协同效应与整合风险及监管摩擦。作者认为，尽管并购能创造价值，但若目标错配往往适得其反。读者可通过该视角理解科技市场中的战略博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://isaaccorbrey.com/profile.png' data-full='https://isaaccorbrey.com/profile.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47878208' data-tags='Security,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47878208' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.telegraph.co.uk/business/2026/04/23/hairdryer-used-trick-weather-sensor-34000-polymarket-bet/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Hairdryer used to trick weather sensor&#x27; to win Polymarket bet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用吹风机欺骗天气传感器以赢取 Polymarket 赌注</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47878208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 260</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47878208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 248</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47878208'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hairdryer was used to trick a weather sensor to win a Polymarket bet, illustrating how simple physical tampering can influence data-driven markets. The incident exposes vulnerabilities in sensor-based predictions and underscores the need for tamper-evident validation and robust data sources. It serves as a warning for operators of decentralized betting platforms to vet input data more rigorously.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有人用吹风机欺骗天气传感器以在 Polymarket 赌注中获胜，说明简单的物理篡改也能影响数据驱动的市场。事件暴露了基于传感器的预测脆弱性，强调需要具备防篡改的校验与可靠的数据源。对去中心化博彩平台的运营者来说，这是一个更严格审查输入数据的警示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.telegraph.co.uk/content/dam/briefs/2026/04/23/TELEMMGLPICT000449701987_17769343097120_trans_NvBQzQNjv4BqqVzuuqpFlyLIwiB6NTmJwSX5rhseiWKOo9p9OQ-ymek.jpeg?impolicy=OG-Standard' data-full='https://www.telegraph.co.uk/content/dam/briefs/2026/04/23/TELEMMGLPICT000449701987_17769343097120_trans_NvBQzQNjv4BqqVzuuqpFlyLIwiB6NTmJwSX5rhseiWKOo9p9OQ-ymek.jpeg?impolicy=OG-Standard' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47849854' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/d/34369-original-grapheneos-responses-to-wired-fact-checker' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Original GrapheneOS responses to WIRED fact checker</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GrapheneOS 对 WIRED 事实核查的原始回应</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47849854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 260</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47849854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 235</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47849854'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS posted its initial responses to WIRED&#x27;s fact-check, pushing back on asserted privacy and security claims. The exchange illustrates how security-focused projects engage with media scrutiny and the importance of precise claims in reporting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 发布了对 WIRED 事实核查的初步回应，辩护其隐私与安全主张并澄清被错误表述之处。此事展示了以安全为导向的项目如何应对媒体审查，以及报道中对主张精确性的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/opengraph.png' data-full='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/opengraph.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47894000' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/changelog' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI releases GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.5 Pro in the API</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 发布 GPT-5.5 与 GPT-5.5 Pro API</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47894000' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 255</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47894000' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47894000'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI expands its API with GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.5 Pro, bringing improved capabilities and enterprise features to developers. The update promises better reasoning, safety controls, and more deployment options for applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 在 API 中引入 GPT-5.5 与 Pro 版本，带来更强的推理能力、更完善的安全控制以及更多部署选项，面向开发者。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47852155' data-tags='Open Source,Web,Privacy,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47852155' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/calcom/cal.diy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cal.diy: open-source community edition of cal.com</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cal.diy：cal.com 的开源社区版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47852155' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 255</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47852155' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47852155'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cal.diy is a community edition of cal.com that you can self-host, enabling scheduling on your own infrastructure with privacy controls. It demonstrates how open-source forks empower teams to customize and avoid vendor lock-in. The project broadens choices for privacy-conscious deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cal.diy 是 cal.com 的开源社区版，支持自托管，让团队在自有基础设施上进行排程并掌控隐私。它展示了开源分叉如何赋能定制化并减少对厂商的依赖，为注重隐私的部署提供了更多选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0cfc70d12f0f81367c3ec1b35cd1b92529aff6e1f2328561789955b8bc86e48e/calcom/cal.diy' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0cfc70d12f0f81367c3ec1b35cd1b92529aff6e1f2328561789955b8bc86e48e/calcom/cal.diy' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47863324' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47863324' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.psu.edu/news/earth-and-mineral-sciences/story/treetops-glowing-during-storms-captured-film-first-time' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ultraviolet corona discharges on treetops during storms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>树梢出现紫外电晕放电</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47863324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 253</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47863324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47863324'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Storms produce faint ultraviolet corona discharges visible on treetops, captured on film for the first time. The observation helps scientists study atmospheric electricity and could refine models of lightning initiation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>暴风雨中树梢出现可见的紫外电晕放电，首次被记录成像。这一现象有助于研究大气电现象，并可能改进雷电起始的模型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://psu-gatsby-files-prod.s3.amazonaws.com/s3fs-public/styles/16_9_1000w/public/2026/04/corona-news-1.jpg?h=84071268&amp;itok=bocHWT_2' data-full='https://psu-gatsby-files-prod.s3.amazonaws.com/s3fs-public/styles/16_9_1000w/public/2026/04/corona-news-1.jpg?h=84071268&amp;itok=bocHWT_2' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47832969' data-tags='Web,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47832969' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ArcaneNibble/awawausb' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WebUSB Extension for Firefox</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Firefox 的 WebUSB 扩展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47832969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 253</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47832969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 229</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47832969'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Firefox WebUSB extension enables web apps to access USB devices directly, expanding browser hardware access while raising security considerations. This work illustrates ongoing efforts to bring hardware interoperability to the web.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 的 WebUSB 扩展使网页应用能够直接访问 USB 设备，在扩展浏览器对硬件的接入能力的同时，也带来安全方面的考量，体现了将硬件互操作性带到网页的持续努力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/26a0dccb0e485322529ab2e1e764d09ca37e42411b5a4b42516e23fa2973ce99/ArcaneNibble/awawausb' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/26a0dccb0e485322529ab2e1e764d09ca37e42411b5a4b42516e23fa2973ce99/ArcaneNibble/awawausb' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47883108' data-tags='Security,Legal,Politics,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47883108' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.justice.gov/usao-sdny/pr/us-soldier-charged-using-classified-information-profit-prediction-market-bets?bm-verify=AAQAAAAN_____y6To7sZYZ502biZwIHXlr-7zXZUqV4H0xLTfW__wDA3SjNLqifXRaQwsikyuz6IJknyuL8xfVYRkesxcDk5V10m-HoXl2K93f17rygBphL77WVFoQ_XvlmUo922IwM_DQ66137X6wWMtpdHslcEjpJG7KbBmUw9Su4kDENpDt_yv2spThQZehgv-X1Adk5U2VHfp41co2s_QJGjRj4y0KmL1mhSCyVaE7MC1LHG0mtP-xYmD0xBOQwn6PlHkPiP5Nt46h5ZIjLGXgCZDEVD42i7rIEM379DKLPUPX0PDNmOAwFSuTqcTDVnT_UUV8vxpHLRMb7rUxPxKUPcIB23iZTRJddWDDtPHMXadpwv67xr-f1sKDLZT9NgHCO4iuC2EthmAt0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. soldier charged with using classified info to profit from prediction market</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美军士兵被控利用机密信息在预测市场获利</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47883108' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 250</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47883108' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47883108'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A U.S. soldier has been charged with using classified information to profit from prediction-market bets. The case illustrates how insider access to sensitive data can spill into civilian markets and tests the enforcement of ethics rules for service members. If convicted, it could tighten scrutiny on handling classified material in and out of government channels.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名美国士兵被控利用机密信息在预测市场下注获利。此案凸显对敏感信息的内部访问可能扩展至民间市场，并检验对军人遵守伦理规定的执法力度。若被定罪，可能促使对处理机密材料的监管进一步收紧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.justice.gov/themes/custom/usdoj_uswds/images/metatag-image--press-release.png' data-full='https://www.justice.gov/themes/custom/usdoj_uswds/images/metatag-image--press-release.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47866127' data-tags='Design,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47866127' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.korny.info/2026/04/19/the-joy-of-folding-bikes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Joy of Folding Bikes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>折叠自行车的乐趣</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 249</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 170</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47866127'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Folding bikes excel at portability and urban practicality, letting riders fold, carry, and store bikes easily without sacrificing ride quality. The post celebrates design variety and the growing popularity among city riders.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>折叠自行车在便携性与骑行体验之间取得平衡，便于多模态通勤与存放。文章赞美其设计变体与在城市骑行中的日益普及。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.korny.info/assets/images/korny-face.png' data-full='https://blog.korny.info/assets/images/korny-face.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47824403' data-tags='Business,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47824403' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/913765/adobe-rivals-free-creative-software-app-updates' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The creative software industry has declared war on Adobe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>创意软件行业对 Adobe 的挑战</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824403' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 248</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824403' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47824403'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Industry players are intensifying competition with more affordable, capable tools that threaten Adobe&#x27;s monopoly on creative workflows. The piece surveys notable alternatives and product strategies aimed at teams and individual creators, signaling a shift toward open formats, better pricing, and interoperability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>众多厂商通过更实惠、功能完善的工具挑战 Adobe 在创作工作流中的垄断地位。文章梳理了值得关注的替代品和产品策略，强调开源/开放格式、更友好的定价与互操作性正在成为潮流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/04/adobe-war_c7d8c4.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.742559278735%2C100%2C78.514881442529&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/04/adobe-war_c7d8c4.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.742559278735%2C100%2C78.514881442529&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47904568' data-tags='AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47904568' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newrepublic.com/article/209163/ai-industry-discovering-public-backlash' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The AI industry is discovering that the public hates it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人工智能行业才意识到公众对其存有反感</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47904568' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 245</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47904568' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 331</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47904568'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Public sentiment toward AI is turning more negative as concerns about safety, bias, and hype intensify. The industry faces mounting scrutiny from policymakers and skeptics, making trust and responsible innovation top priorities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公众对人工智能的情绪正变得更为负面，安全、偏见与炒作等担忧加剧。行业正面临来自政策制定者和公众的日益密集的监督，信任与负责任的创新成为首要议题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.newrepublic.com/7619b4e952a635df61a0a59005e2f476c20f36bc.jpeg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;crop=faces&amp;fit=crop&amp;fm=jpg' data-full='https://images.newrepublic.com/7619b4e952a635df61a0a59005e2f476c20f36bc.jpeg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;crop=faces&amp;fit=crop&amp;fm=jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://joshblais.com/blog/using-the-internet-like-its-1999/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using the internet like it&#x27;s 1999</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>像在1999年一样上网</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47881198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 245</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47881198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47881198'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look at surfacing the web with retro, dial-up aesthetics and minimal modern features, challenging today’s feature-heavy UX.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以1999年的互联网方式上网，回归简约、低带宽体验，挑战当下功能繁多的用户体验。文章讨论简化界面、离线优先与低广告干扰的体验对性能、隐私与可访问性的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://joshblais.com/og-images/netscape.jpeg' data-full='https://joshblais.com/og-images/netscape.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
