</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47843194' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47843194' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(138) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zef-lang.dev/implementation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to make a fast dynamic language interpreter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何打造高效的动态语言解释器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47843194' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 245</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47843194' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47843194'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece surveys architecture choices for a fast dynamic language interpreter, weighing the trade-offs between interpretation, compilation, and JIT techniques. It highlights practical optimizations such as inline caches, bytecode representations, tracing JIT, and data-layout choices that push performance without sacrificing dynamism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文讨论快速动态语言解释器的设计取舍，权衡解释、编译和JIT等技术。介绍字节码表示、内联缓存、跟踪编译等实际优化，以及在不牺牲动态性的前提下提升性能的数据结构选择。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47864056' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thehistoryblog.com/archives/75877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iliad fragment found in Roman-era mummy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>罗马时期木乃伊中发现伊利亚特碎片</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47864056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 242</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47864056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47864056'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An Iliad fragment was found inside a Roman-era mummy, indicating Greek literature circulated in Egypt during Roman rule and offering clues about textual transmission across cultures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在罗马时期木乃伊中发现《伊利亚特》碎片，表明希腊文学在罗马统治下的埃及仍在流传，并为跨文化文本传抄提供线索。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ynarwal.github.io/how-llms-work/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: How LLMs Work – Interactive visual guide based on Karpathy&#x27;s lecture</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：基于 Karpathy 演讲的互动式 LLM 工作原理可视化指南</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47886517' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 241</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47886517' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

It demystifies core concepts like tokens, attention, prompts, and inference, making advanced AI theory accessible to developers and students.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个基于 Karpathy 演讲的互动式可视化指南，分步展示大型语言模型的工作原理。通过直观的令牌、注意力、提示与推理等要素，帮助开发者和学习者更好地理解模型运作。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47899844' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/nex-crm/wuphf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: A Karpathy-style LLM wiki your agents maintain (Markdown and Git)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：由代理维护的 Karpathy 风格的 LLM 维基（Markdown 与 Git）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47899844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 239</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47899844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47899844'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: A wiki for large language models that agents maintain, using Markdown and Git, inspired by Karpathy&#x27;s approach. It enables auditable knowledge sharing and versioned reasoning traces for LLM-driven workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN：一个由代理维护、以 Markdown 与 Git 为版本控制的 LLM 维基，灵感来自 Karpathy 的做法。实现可审计的知识共享与对 LL M 推理过程的版本化追踪。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Niri 26.04 release introduces scrollable tiling in its Wayland compositor, enabling more flexible window tiling with scrollable frames. The update improves multitasking for users who prefer tiling workflows and dynamic navigation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Niri 26.04 版本在 Wayland 合成器中加入可滚动的平铺布局，使窗口排布更加灵活，支持滚动的窗口区域。此更新提升了偏好平铺工作流的用户在多任务和导航上的体验。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47861087'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece breaks down how satellites, receivers, and precise timing combine to determine a location. It covers signal structure, accuracy, and common vulnerabilities like jamming or spoofing, with implications for devices and privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文分解了卫星、接收器和精确时间如何共同确定位置，介绍信号结构、精度，以及常见的干扰与欺骗等脆弱点，并讨论对设备和隐私的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://causality.blog/essays/what-async-promised/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What async promised and what it delivered</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>异步编程：承诺与现实</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47859442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 232</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47859442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 267</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47859442'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Async promised simpler concurrency and scalability but delivered complexity in practice. The piece argues for better mental models and language/runtime design to align promises with real-world trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于异步编程的承诺与现实存在差距。文章认为异步更多地解决了 I/O 的扩展性问题，但带来了新的复杂性，并呼吁改进心智模型与语言/运行时设计，以更好地把承诺落地。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://causality.blog/og-essay-3.png' data-full='https://causality.blog/og-essay-3.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47815462'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hobbyist experiment shows a Minecraft server and more running on a 1960s UNIVAC computer, illustrating how software can push beyond hardware era boundaries. It underscores the enduring value of abstraction and the quirky edge cases of retro computing.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47887683' data-tags='AI,Security,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47887683' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c4gx1n0dl9no' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>South Korea police arrest man for posting AI photo of runaway wolf</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>韩国警方因发布 AI 生成的逃逸狼照片逮捕男子</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47887683' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 231</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47887683' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 151</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47887683'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A man in South Korea was arrested after authorities say he posted an AI-generated image of a runaway wolf. The incident highlights the legal and safety risks of synthetic imagery, and how governments are responding to AI-created misinformation. It underscores why platform moderation and digital-literacy are increasingly important.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>韩国警方就一名男子发布AI生成的逃逸狼照片进行逮捕。这起事件凸显了合成图像带来的法律与公共安全风险，以及各国对AI生成内容的监管回应。也提醒人们提升数字素养并加强平台监管的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/4940/live/f8c31140-3fb5-11f1-b61b-1d0ccf7b6022.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/4940/live/f8c31140-3fb5-11f1-b61b-1d0ccf7b6022.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47814874' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47814874' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thinkygames.com/features/10-years-of-grilling-stephens-sausage-roll-remains-one-of-the-most-influential-puzzle-games-ever-created/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stephen&#x27;s Sausage Roll remains one of the most influential puzzle games</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>斯蒂芬的香肠卷仍是最具影响力的解谜游戏之一</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47814874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 230</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47814874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 129</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47814874'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stephen&#x27;s Sausage Roll, a minimalist puzzle game, marks a decade of influence on the puzzle genre. Praised for its elegant yet punishing design, it helped shape indie puzzle design and inspired later titles that emphasize precise rules and open-ended exploration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《斯蒂芬的香肠卷》这款极简解谜游戏迎来十周年，因其优雅却高难度的谜题设计被广泛认可。它对独立解谜游戏的发展产生深远影响，启发了后续注重精确定义规则与开放探索的作品。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47849221' data-tags='Business,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47849221' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.worseonpurpose.com/p/your-favorite-brands-got-worse-on-purpose' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Brands got worse on purpose</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>品牌被故意变坏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47849221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 230</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47849221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47849221'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that some brands deliberately degrade product quality or user experience to boost monetization, citing examples from consumer apps and services. It explores the economics of the &#x27;worse is better&#x27; strategy and why users end up paying more for features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章声称部分品牌有意降低产品质量或用户体验以提升盈利，结合应用与服务的实例进行剖析。探讨这种“更糟即更好”的商业策略背后的经济逻辑，以及用户为何为功能付出更高成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://beehiiv-images-production.s3.amazonaws.com/uploads/asset/file/3b24affa-eff7-4a2a-a557-ed9df33e98eb/Worse_on_Purpose_-_Thumbnail_Preview.png?t=1774308955' data-full='https://beehiiv-images-production.s3.amazonaws.com/uploads/asset/file/3b24affa-eff7-4a2a-a557-ed9df33e98eb/Worse_on_Purpose_-_Thumbnail_Preview.png?t=1774308955' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47865622' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47865622' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/04/22/science/988-youth-suicides-decline.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Youth Suicides Declined After Creation of National Hotline</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>国家热线成立后青少年自杀率下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47865622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 228</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47865622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47865622'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds youth suicide rates declined after launching a nationwide crisis hotline, suggesting hotlines can have a measurable public health impact. The finding underscores the importance of accessible mental health resources, though ongoing efforts are needed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现国家热线推出后，青少年自杀率下降，表明热线对公共卫生有显著影响。该发现强调可及心理健康资源的重要性，但需要持续努力以巩固效果。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47847124' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47847124' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fsfe.org/news/2026/news-20260420-01.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple ignores DMA interoperability requests and contradicts own documentation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果无视 DMA 互操作性请求，并与自家文档矛盾</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47847124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 228</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47847124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47847124'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple reportedly ignored interoperability requests under the Digital Markets Act and contradicted its own documentation on device access or compatibility. The incident underscores regulatory pressure on platform openness and the friction between policy mandates and how products are documented.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，苹果无视 DMA（数字市场法）的互操作性请求，并与自身文档存在矛盾。这凸显了监管机构对大型平台强制开放的压力，以及政策要求与产品设计之间的持续张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pics.fsfe.org/uploads/original/ee/3d/407431339a047caf4fc5f1ea05db.png' data-full='https://pics.fsfe.org/uploads/original/ee/3d/407431339a047caf4fc5f1ea05db.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47873323' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47873323' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://buttondown.com/blog/x400-vs-smtp-email' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Email could have been X.400 times better</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>邮件本可以比 X.400 更好</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47873323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47873323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47873323'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration argues that X.400 offered architectural benefits over SMTP—such as stronger routing and security—yet failed to gain traction; the post reflects on why SMTP won and what current email protocols could learn.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 X.400 相较于 SMTP 在路由和安全性等方面具备潜在优势，但未被广泛采用。回顾 SMTP 的胜出原因，并讨论当前邮件协议可以从中学习的经验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://marketing.buttondown.com/og/generic?title=Email+could+have+been+X.400+times+better&amp;subtitle=Matthew+Guay&amp;date=April+17%2C+2026' data-full='https://marketing.buttondown.com/og/generic?title=Email+could+have+been+X.400+times+better&amp;subtitle=Matthew+Guay&amp;date=April+17%2C+2026' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47826100' data-tags='Business,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47826100' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://warontherocks.com/cogs-of-war/the-bromine-chokepoint-how-strife-in-the-middle-east-could-halt-production-of-the-worlds-memory-chips/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Bromine Chokepoint</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>溴资源成为全球记忆芯片生产的瓶颈</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47826100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47826100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 136</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47826100'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bromine is a key input in several semiconductor processes, and ongoing strife in the Middle East could disrupt its supply, potentially bottlenecking memory-chip production. The result could be higher costs and tighter supply for DRAM and flash devices, underscoring geopolitical risk in chemical supply chains. Diversification and alternative manufacturing paths become strategic levers for the industry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>中东局势可能打断全球记忆体芯片生产对溴供应的依赖，造成产量受限与价格波动。溴在芯片制造中的关键作用使其成为脆弱的供应瓶颈。这凸显了供应多元化和替代工艺的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://warontherocks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/The_Dead_Sea_ASTER.jpg?v=1775831012' data-full='https://warontherocks.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/The_Dead_Sea_ASTER.jpg?v=1775831012' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47860742' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47860742' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/2026/04/22/meta_employee_surveillance_software/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Irony as Meta staff unhappy about running surveillance software on work PCs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 员工对在工作电脑上运行监控软件感到讽刺</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47860742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 225</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47860742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 172</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47860742'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta employees reportedly dislike the surveillance software installed on company devices, despite the company&#x27;s own stance on privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，Meta 员工对工作机器上运行的监控软件感到不满，尽管公司自述强调隐私保护。这反映出科技巨头在内部安保工具与员工隐私之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://regmedia.co.uk/2025/08/28/shutterstock_surprised_man.jpg' data-full='https://regmedia.co.uk/2025/08/28/shutterstock_surprised_man.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47844797' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/apr/21/israeli-soldiers-using-sexual-assault-to-force-palestinians-out-of-west-bank-report-says' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Israeli soldiers using sexual assault to force Palestinians out of West Bank</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以色列士兵利用性侵迫使巴勒斯坦人离开西岸</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47844797' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 225</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47844797' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47844797'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Independent report alleges that Israeli soldiers used sexual violence as a tactic to displace Palestinians in the West Bank. The findings add to ongoing accountability debates about military conduct and human rights in the conflict.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>独立报道指控以色列士兵将性暴力作为迫使巴勒斯坦人离开西岸的手段之一。这一发现加剧了人们对冲突中军事行为和人权问责的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/06b45e9329dda673ddb529c4e99fefc8b5551593/570_0_4561_3648/master/4561.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=daaadee6e882f25a0868e6b96e5995be' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/06b45e9329dda673ddb529c4e99fefc8b5551593/570_0_4561_3648/master/4561.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=daaadee6e882f25a0868e6b96e5995be' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47880189' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47880189' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c9d4zgnqpqeo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Girl, 10, finds rare Mexican axolotl under Welsh bridge</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>10岁女孩在威尔士的一座桥下发现罕见的墨西哥钝口螈</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47880189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 224</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47880189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 208</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47880189'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 10-year-old girl in Wales found a rare Mexican axolotl under a bridge, a remarkable sighting outside its native range. The story spotlights conservation and citizen science dynamics when unusual species show up in unexpected places. It also underscores how outreach and curiosity connect people to wildlife.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>威尔士一名10岁女孩在一座桥下发现罕见的墨西哥钝口螈，这一发现颇为罕见且引人注目。此事凸显了在非原产地也会出现独特物种的保护与公民科学的互动，同时也体现了公众对野生动物的关注与好奇心。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/bb4f/live/4211fe40-3f13-11f1-9aab-c976736c58b1.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/bb4f/live/4211fe40-3f13-11f1-9aab-c976736c58b1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47858246' data-tags='Space,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47858246' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fortune.com/2026/04/21/scientists-disappear-die-nasa-space-blue-origin-spacex/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FBI looks into dead or missing scientists tied to NASA, Blue Origin, SpaceX</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FBI 调查与 NASA/Blue Origin/SpaceX 相关的死者或失踪科学家</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47858246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 224</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47858246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47858246'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FBI is investigating dead or missing scientists linked to major space organizations. The case underscores potential security or safety concerns in high-profile aerospace projects and may prompt calls for enhanced oversight and vetting of contractor work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FBI 正在调查与 NASA、Blue Origin、SpaceX 相关的已故或失踪科学家，引发对安全和合规的关注。此类调查或促使对高风险航天项目承包商的审查及风险管理加强。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47874854' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47874854' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/raysan5/raylib/releases/tag/6.0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Raylib v6.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Raylib 6.0 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47874854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 221</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47874854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47874854'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Raylib 6.0 arrives as a major update to the lightweight game development library, offering API improvements, better portability, and streamlined tooling for cross-platform projects. The release makes it easier for indie developers and educators to build and teach with graphics and audio capabilities. It could accelerate rapid prototyping and learning in game development communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Raylib 6.0 作为轻量级游戏开发库的重大更新，带来 API 改善、可移植性提升与跨平台工具链的简化。该版本让独立开发者与教育者更容易构建并教学，强化图形、音频等功能。预计将推动快速原型设计和游戏开发社区的学习进程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0ced67c6358522b6db8a616b36e431bf8c9aa30cab10aeadbce505ac56079579/raysan5/raylib/releases/tag/6.0' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0ced67c6358522b6db8a616b36e431bf8c9aa30cab10aeadbce505ac56079579/raysan5/raylib/releases/tag/6.0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47829178' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47829178' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Apr/20/claude-token-counts/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Token Counter, now with model comparisons</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Token Counter，现在支持模型对比</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47829178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 220</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47829178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47829178'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Token Counter now supports cross-model comparisons, helping developers estimate token costs and compare efficiency across Claude models. This aids model selection and budgeting for NLP projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Token Counter 增加了跨模型的对比功能，便于开发者对不同模型的代币消耗进行比对与成本估算。新功能有助于选型与预算控制，优化提示设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.simonwillison.net/static/2026/claude-token-card.jpg' data-full='https://static.simonwillison.net/static/2026/claude-token-card.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47826839' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reason.com/2026/04/20/this-big-tech-firm-wants-to-reinstate-the-draft/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Palantir Wants to Reinstate the Draft</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Palantir 想要重启征兵</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47836463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 219</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47836463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 236</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47836463'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports that Palantir lobbies or advocates for reinstating conscription, linking tech firms to national service in a way that could reshape the talent pipeline and civil-liberties considerations. The piece prompts readers to consider the governance questions around private sector influence over public policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道 Palantir 倡议重新征兵，或游说推动国家征兵制度，暗示科技企业与公共政策之间的影响力重组以及对公民自由的潜在考量。引发对私企对公共政策影响力的治理问题的思考。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47859861' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47859861' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/20260421-00/?p=112247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>XOR&#x27;ing a register with itself is the idiom for zeroing it out. Why not sub?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把寄存器与自身进行异或以清零是经典写法，为什么不用减法？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47859861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 218</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47859861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 212</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47859861'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>XORing a register with itself is the classic zeroing trick in low-level code. It avoids data dependencies and typically maps to the cleanest, fastest zeroing instruction across architectures. The piece argues that subtraction is a slower or less portable alternative, making XOR the preferred technique in practice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>把寄存器与自身做异或来清零是经典技巧。它避免数据依赖且在大多数架构上实现简单、效率高；而直接用减法清零往往性能较差且可移植性较低，因此不太被推荐。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' data-full='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47910546' data-tags='Web,Design,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47910546' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/orgs/community/discussions/192666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub unwanted UX change: issue links now open in a popup</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 的 UX 变动：问题链接现在在弹窗中打开</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47910546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47910546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47910546'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Users report that clicking issue links opens a popup window instead of navigating, disrupting workflow and increasing context-switching. The change showcases how UI decisions in developer platforms can impact productivity and friction, and suggests better ways to surface issue details.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用户反馈称点击问题链接会弹出一个对话框，导致无法直接跳转，提高了切换成本。此 UX 改动暴露了开发者平台在功能暴露上的取舍，也提醒改动应更好地兼顾工作流连续性和信息可用性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a0667e319725fafeb67f9181edbf8d89c109b4778b020c7487b0df0708910788/orgs/community/discussions/192666' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a0667e319725fafeb67f9181edbf8d89c109b4778b020c7487b0df0708910788/orgs/community/discussions/192666' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47857477' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47857477' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kpbs.org/news/economy/2026/03/27/san-diego-rents-declined-more-than-19-of-nations-top-20-markets-following-surge-in-supply' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>San Diego rents declined following surge in supply</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>圣地亚哥租金在供应激增后下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47857477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47857477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47857477'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>San Diego rents declined more than 19% following a surge in housing supply, placing it among markets with notable rent falls. The data suggests a cooling demand and rising vacancy as new units hit the market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>圣地亚哥在住房供应激增后，租金下降超过 19%，成为租金降幅显著的市场之一。数据表明需求放缓与空置率上升，随着新单位投放市场。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.kpbs.org/dims4/default/2703f12/2147483647/strip/true/crop/1772x930+0+30/resize/1200x630!/quality/90/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fkpbs-brightspot.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com%2Fd7%2F24%2F6a9a09974694a11c4b201e448da0%2Fnewapt.png' data-full='https://cdn.kpbs.org/dims4/default/2703f12/2147483647/strip/true/crop/1772x930+0+30/resize/1200x630!/quality/90/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fkpbs-brightspot.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com%2Fd7%2F24%2F6a9a09974694a11c4b201e448da0%2Fnewapt.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47812749' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47812749' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prismml.com/news/ternary-bonsai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ternary Bonsai: Top Intelligence at 1.58 Bits</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>三值盆景：以1.58比特实现的顶级智能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47812749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 216</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47812749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47812749'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ternary Bonsai showcases an ultra-efficient AI model that operates with only 1.58 bits of information per decision, challenging assumptions about model size and performance. The approach combines ternary quantization and sparsity with principled optimization to enable smaller, cheaper models that still perform well on real tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>三值盆景展示一种极致高效的AI模型，利用仅1.58比特的信息量来做出决策，挑战对模型大小与性能的传统认知。通过三值量化和稀疏化等技巧实现更小、更省资源的模型，同时保持在真实任务上的表现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/699604cc2b9dd89bdbda0608/69cc089669ab0df26e921aac_prism-og-img%20(2).png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/699604cc2b9dd89bdbda0608/69cc089669ab0df26e921aac_prism-og-img%20(2).png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hackster.io/news/a-diy-watch-you-can-actually-wear-8f91c2dac682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A programmable watch you can actually wear</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可穿戴的 DIY 手表</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47846307' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47846307' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 120</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47846307'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A DIY wearable watch project demonstrates turning off-the-shelf parts and open firmware into a functional wearable. It showcases accessible hardware hacking and could inspire others to build their own wearables.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这个可穿戴 DIY 手表项目展示了如何用现成组件和开源固件组装出一块可佩戴的设备。它体现了硬件黑客的门槛正在降低，或将激发更多人尝试自制可穿戴设备。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47911350' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://feministhackerspaces.cargo.site/Clay-PCB-Tutorial' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Clay PCB Tutorial</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Clay PCB 教程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 211</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47911350'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Clay PCB Tutorial offers a DIY approach to making printed circuit boards using clay-based materials, outlining steps, tools, and safety considerations. It demonstrates how hobbyists can prototype circuits on a budget, while noting trade-offs in precision and durability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Clay PCB Tutorial 提供一种用黏土材料制作简易 PCB 的实操指南，涵盖所需工具、步骤与安全注意事项。为爱好者以低成本原型电路提供途径，同时也提醒在精度和耐久性之间的权衡。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47895080' data-tags='Business,Startups,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47895080' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/24/google-to-invest-up-to-40b-in-anthropic-in-cash-and-compute/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google to invest up to $40B in Anthropic in cash and compute</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌将向 Anthropic 投资最多400亿美元，含现金与算力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47895080' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 209</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47895080' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47895080'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google plans to invest up to $40 billion in Anthropic, combining cash with compute credits. The move deepens a strategic AI partnership and could give Google earlier access to Anthropic’s models and tools, strengthening its cloud AI capabilities amid the race for next-gen AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这笔交易加深了双方的战略合作，可能让谷歌更早获得Anthropic模型和工具的使用权，强化云端AI能力，推动对新一代AI的竞争格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/GettyImages-2252871842.jpg?w=1024' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/GettyImages-2252871842.jpg?w=1024' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47876071' data-tags='Show HN,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47876071' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.playcanvas.com/turning-a-gaussian-splat-into-a-videogame/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Turning a Gaussian Splat into a videogame</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：将高斯斑点变成一款电子游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47876071' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 209</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47876071' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47876071'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical exploration turning 2D Gaussian splats—simple, soft-blurred particles—into a full game aesthetic. The approach highlights how lightweight rendering primitives can yield rich visuals and performance, useful for indie devs and rapid prototyping.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过将简单的高斯斑块用于游戏美术，展示了如何用轻量渲染基元实现丰富的视觉效果与高性能。此做法对独立游戏开发者和快速原型设计具有实用价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.playcanvas.com/img/playcanvas-social-card.jpg' data-full='https://blog.playcanvas.com/img/playcanvas-social-card.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47844539' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47844539' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://longwoodgardens.org/blog/2023-05-17/beauty-bonsai-styles' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Beauty of Bonsai Styles</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>盆栽风格之美</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47844539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 209</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47844539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47844539'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Beauty of Bonsai Styles explores the diverse aesthetics and techniques of bonsai, from formal upright to windswept and cascade forms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《盆栽风格的美》介绍了盆栽美学的多样性与技法，从直立式到风吹式等形式。文章解释修剪、盆器选择与容器几何如何共同塑造微型树木的气质。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://longwoodgardens.org/sites/default/files/styles/social_share_cropped/public/2023-05/639649_Bonsai%20Forms_Gross_%20Carol.jpg?h=fcf25457&amp;itok=q3WFNJtG' data-full='https://longwoodgardens.org/sites/default/files/styles/social_share_cropped/public/2023-05/639649_Bonsai%20Forms_Gross_%20Carol.jpg?h=fcf25457&amp;itok=q3WFNJtG' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bodegacatsofnewyork.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bodega cats of New York</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47812649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47812649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47812649'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project catalogs the famous cats living in New York bodegas, offering a portrait of a quirky urban ritual. Beyond cute photos, it reveals how these feline mascots become local icons and online personalities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个项目记录纽约杂货店里的著名店猫，呈现一种独特的城市文化。除了可爱照片，它们也成为当地的偶像与网络明星，讲述社区故事。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(176) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nationsreportcard.gov/highlights/ltt/2023/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scores decline again for 13-year-old students in reading and mathematics (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2023年13岁学生阅读和数学成绩再次下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47867755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 207</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47867755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 291</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47867755'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NAEP 2023 highlights show a continued decline in reading and math scores for 13-year-olds, widening gaps and raising concerns about the long-term effects of disrupted schooling. The trend adds urgency to targeted interventions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2023年全国评估显示，13岁学生在阅读和数学上的分数再次下滑，拉大了一些教育差距并引发对长期影响的担忧。此趋势凸显了对有针对性干预措施的紧迫需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nationsreportcard.gov/adminonly/themes/redesign_2015/images/nationsreportcard-logo.png' data-full='https://www.nationsreportcard.gov/adminonly/themes/redesign_2015/images/nationsreportcard-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47876647' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nyc.streetsblog.org/2026/04/23/to-protect-and-swerve-nypd-cop-has-527-speeding-tickets-yet-remains-on-the-force' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>To Protect and Swerve: NYPD Cop Has 547 Speeding Tickets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为保护与规避：NYPD 警员累积 547 张超速罚单</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47876647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47876647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47876647'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A New York Police Department officer has racked up 547 speeding tickets while on duty, yet remains on the force. The case raises questions about accountability, supervisory oversight, and how fleet safety policies are enforced. It highlights tensions between public safety duties and personal driving behavior within law enforcement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名纽约警察在在职期间累积了547张超速罚单，仍留任于警队。此事引发关于问责、监督以及车队安全规定执行的质疑，凸显执法部门在公共安全职责与个人驾驶行为之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nyc.streetsblog.org/wp-content/uploads/sites/9/2026/04/GIOVANSANTI-Greg-Mango-with-filter2.jpg' data-full='https://nyc.streetsblog.org/wp-content/uploads/sites/9/2026/04/GIOVANSANTI-Greg-Mango-with-filter2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47822734' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.legalnomads.com/fish-sauce/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Brief History of Fish Sauce</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>鱼露的简史</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47822734'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise tour of fish sauce&#x27;s history, from ancient fermentation to modern industrial production. Regional methods and long aging times built a globally distributed condiment that underpins Southeast Asian cuisine and today’s flavor economics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>鱼露从古代发酵到现代工业化生产的简要演变。文章梳理了区域做法和漫长熟成如何让这一调味品走向全球，并影响了亚洲菜肴的风味与贸易格局。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47885239'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A visual guide that explains Mahjong basics—tiles, hands, and common winning patterns—through diagrams and annotated illustrations. It serves as a practical quick-start for beginners and a handy reference for players brushing up on rules.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份可视化指南用图解介绍麻将的牌型、和牌模式及常见役种，直观易懂。适合初学者快速入门，也为玩家复习规则提供便捷参考。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/what-physical-life-force-turns-biologys-wheels-20260420/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What physical ‘life force’ turns biology’s wheels?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>什么样的物理‘生命力’让生物学运转？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47846718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 202</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47846718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47846718'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Quanta Magazine delves into the physics behind biology, examining what fundamental energy and information flows drive living systems. The piece connects thermodynamics, metabolism, and information processing to explain how life sustains order amid chaos.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>量子杂志探讨生物学背后的物理学，研究驱动生命系统的基本能量和信息流。文章将热力学、代谢与信息处理联系起来，解释生命在紊乱中维持有序的机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/FlagellarMotors-crKristinaArmitage-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/FlagellarMotors-crKristinaArmitage-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47849097' data-tags='Show HN,Open Source,AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47849097' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ENTERPILOT/GOModel/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: GoModel – an open-source AI gateway in Go</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: GoModel——用 Go 实现的开源 AI 网关；比 LiteLLM 轻量 44 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47849097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 202</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47849097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47849097'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GoModel is a lightweight open-source AI gateway written in Go, boasting a footprint far smaller than competing LLM gateways. It targets easier deployment of small-to-midscale AI services, especially for edge or on-prem setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GoModel 是用 Go 编写的轻量级开源 AI 网关，声称体积要比 LiteLLM 等实现低几十倍。它面向在边缘或本地部署的小到中等规模的 AI 服务，降低门槛与运维成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/81314de10a9232ccb08fe24794f0b3fa5c5e767c7c7ffe5b01953298e06eb5c5/ENTERPILOT/GoModel' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/81314de10a9232ccb08fe24794f0b3fa5c5e767c7c7ffe5b01953298e06eb5c5/ENTERPILOT/GoModel' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47891801' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47891801' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kalzumeus.com/2009/09/05/desktop-aps-versus-web-apps/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m done making desktop applications (2009)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不再做桌面应用（2009）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47891801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47891801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47891801'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In his 2009 essay, Patrick McKenzie argues that the era of desktop applications is fading in favor of web apps due to distribution, maintenance, and reach advantages. He outlines forces shaping software delivery and monetization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 2009 年的博文中，作者认为桌面应用的时代正在消退，网页应用因更易分发、维护与覆盖玩家更广而成为主流。他分析了塑造软件交付与盈利模式的驱动因素。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.kalzumeus.com/blog-images/piracy-is-dying.png' data-full='https://media.kalzumeus.com/blog-images/piracy-is-dying.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47888465' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47888465' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/AndrewVos/endless-toil' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hear your agent suffer through your code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让你的智能体在你的代码里痛苦地折腾</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47888465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47888465' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47888465'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An experimental setup where an AI agent repeatedly runs and debugs code, exposing how brittle software and ambiguous interfaces force costly problem-framing. It underscores the need for reliable tooling, better error messages, and structured prompts to reduce toil when AI-assisted coding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个让人工智能代理不断执行、调试代码的实验场景，揭示了脆弱代码和模糊接口会带来高成本的问题。文章强调需要更可靠的工具、清晰的错误信息，以及结构化的提示以降低 AI 辅助编码时的工作量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/37ec4f89ae41abdd2a79ef2e139d1e8bfc4428a087543a12f64e18a789e3b664/AndrewVos/endless-toil' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/37ec4f89ae41abdd2a79ef2e139d1e8bfc4428a087543a12f64e18a789e3b664/AndrewVos/endless-toil' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47872452' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47872452' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/staff/2026/04/our-newsroom-ai-policy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Our newsroom AI policy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ars Technica：新闻编辑部的 AI 政策</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47872452' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 200</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47872452' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47872452'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ars Technica lays out rules for how the newsroom uses AI, including disclosure, verification, and limits on relying on AI for reporting. The policy signals a cautious, transparency-first approach to AI in journalism that could influence newsroom standards industry-wide.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Ars Technica》的新闻编辑部在 AI 使用方面提出了规则与底线，包括披露、核实和对报道的限制。该政策体现了在新闻业中以透明和谨慎为先的态度，可能影响行业的AI使用标准。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/ars-logo-dark-background-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/ars-logo-dark-background-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47858294' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47858294' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260416-the-humpback-super-groups-swarming-the-seas' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Humpback whales are forming super-groups</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>座头鲸正形成超群群体</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47858294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47858294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47858294'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Humpback whales are forming larger &#x27;super-groups&#x27; on feeding grounds, aggregating multiple pods into long-lasting social networks. These groups may boost foraging efficiency and info exchange about prey patches, challenging the view of whales as solitary; reveals new social complexity in large marine mammals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>座头鲸在某些觅食场所聚成更大的“超群”，由多个群体组成并持续一段时间。这些社交聚集提升了觅食效率和信息交流，揭示了大型海洋哺乳动物中复杂的社会结构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0ndswqp.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0ndswqp.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47913855' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47913855' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sentinelone.com/labs/fast16-mystery-shadowbrokers-reference-reveals-high-precision-software-sabotage-5-years-before-stuxnet/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fast16: High-precision software sabotage 5 years before Stuxnet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fast16：比 Stuxnet 早5年的高精度软件破坏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913855' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 197</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913855' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47913855'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New evidence ties Fast16 to a high-precision sabotage capability years before Stuxnet, suggesting sophisticated cyber-physical attack capabilities existed well ahead of that famous incident. The finding underscores long-running threats to industrial control systems and the need for deeper defense in supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新证据将 Fast16 与比 Stuxnet 早现的高精度破坏能力联系起来，表明对工业控制系统的高端攻击能力早已存在。此发现凸显对供应链与控制网络的长期威胁，以及更深层防御的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sentinelone.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/fast16_3.jpg' data-full='https://www.sentinelone.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/fast16_3.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47881786' data-tags='AI,Hardware,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47881786' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developers.googleblog.com/torchtpu-running-pytorch-natively-on-tpus-at-google-scale/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TorchTPU: Running PyTorch Natively on TPUs at Google Scale</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TorchTPU：在 Google 规模的 TPUs 上原生运行 PyTorch</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47881786' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 197</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47881786' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47881786'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TorchTPU enables PyTorch to run natively on Google-scale TPUs, bringing PyTorch’s dynamic features to TPU hardware. This unlocks large-scale training with the ease of PyTorch APIs while leveraging the efficiency of TPUs in cloud environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TorchTPU 让 PyTorch 能在 Google 规模的 TPU 上原生运行，将 PyTorch 的动态图特性带到 TPU 硬件。借助云端 TPU 的高效，开发者可以在大规模训练中继续使用 PyTorch APIs。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-developer-goog-blog-assets/images/Meta-A.2e16d0ba.fill-1200x600.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-developer-goog-blog-assets/images/Meta-A.2e16d0ba.fill-1200x600.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47828896' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47828896' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/shivampkumar/trellis-mac' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Run TRELLIS.2 Image-to-3D generation natively on Apple Silicon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：在 Apple Silicon 上原生运行 TRELLIS.2 的图像转3D 生成</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47828896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 197</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47828896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47828896'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TRELLIS.2 enables image-to-3D generation, and this build runs natively on Apple Silicon, suggesting optimized performance for Macs with M-series chips. This lowers hardware barriers for offline, on-device AI content creation and could influence Mac-focused AI tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TRELLIS.2 实现图像到3D 的生成，且在 Apple Silicon 上实现原生运行，表现在对 M1/M2 的优化能显著提升速度与离线工作能力。此举降低了对高端云算力的依赖，利于本地创作和跨平台工具链的开发。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9df788af0e0e73c7eb48d555052259ce148857a6898808376dbb7631ba95b70c/shivampkumar/trellis-mac' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9df788af0e0e73c7eb48d555052259ce148857a6898808376dbb7631ba95b70c/shivampkumar/trellis-mac' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47898597' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47898597' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/magiblot/tvision' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turbo Vision 2.0 – a modern port</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Turbo Vision 2.0：现代移植</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47898597' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47898597' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47898597'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Turbo Vision 2.0 is a modern port that keeps the classic terminal UI library alive with updated builds and cross-platform support. It enables lightweight, text-based interfaces for tools and utilities, appealing to retro enthusiasts and systems programmers. The project demonstrates how legacy tooling can evolve without abandoning its roots.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Turbo Vision 2.0 是对经典终端 UI 库的现代移植，更新构建并扩展跨平台支持。它为轻量级工具提供文本界面，吸引复古爱好者和系统开发者。该项目显示出传统工具在不放弃根基的前提下也能持续进化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/184303095/5badcd00-9313-11ea-8554-4d4949084174' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/184303095/5badcd00-9313-11ea-8554-4d4949084174' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47875843' data-tags='Privacy,Data,Programming,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47875843' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://biobank.rocher.lc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UK Biobank health data keeps ending up on GitHub</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>UK Biobank 健康数据持续被上传至 GitHub</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47875843' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47875843' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47875843'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Health data from the UK Biobank continues to appear on GitHub, raising concerns about data governance, privacy, and access controls in open repositories. The incident spotlights the tension between reproducible science and safeguarding sensitive health information. It also underscores the need for robust data-sharing policies and tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国生物库的健康数据持续出现在 GitHub 上，引发对数据治理、隐私和开放仓库访问控制的担忧。此事凸显了可重复科学与保护敏感健康信息之间的矛盾，也提醒加强数据共享政策和工具的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://biobank.rocher.lc/og.png' data-full='https://biobank.rocher.lc/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47888557' data-tags='Privacy,Data,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47888557' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bmj.com/content/393/bmj.s781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UK Biobank leak: Health details of 500k people offered for sale on Alibaba</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>UK Biobank 数据泄露：约50万名个人健康信息在阿里巴巴平台上架出售</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47888557' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47888557' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47888557'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A leak of the UK Biobank dataset exposed health details for about 500,000 people and were offered for sale on Alibaba. The incident raises urgent questions about consent, data governance, and the risks of commodifying sensitive medical information. It underscores the need for stricter data controls and oversight in large biobank-like datasets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国 Biobank 数据集约有50万人的健康信息被泄露并在阿里巴巴平台上架出售。事件暴露出同意、数据治理以及将敏感健康信息商品化的风险等问题，凸显加强大型生物样本库数据规范和监管的必要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47849315' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47849315' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fusionenergybase.com/fusion-power-plant-simulator' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fusion Power Plant Simulator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>聚变电厂仿真器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47849315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47849315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47849315'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A fusion power plant simulator lets researchers explore dynamics, control, and design trade-offs without building a real reactor. By modeling plasma physics, heat exhaust, and economics, it clarifies the engineering challenges of scaling fusion to commercial power.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>聚变电厂仿真器让研究者在不建造真实反应堆的情况下，探索动力学、控制策略与设计权衡。通过对等离子体物理、热排放与经济性的建模，揭示了将聚变推向商业规模所需面对的工程挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.fusionenergybase.com/images/fusion-simulator-preview.png' data-full='https://www.fusionenergybase.com/images/fusion-simulator-preview.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
