<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47912645' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://road.cc/news/driverless-taxis-veering-into-cycle-lanes-normal-practice-says-waymo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Waymo says can&#x27;t avoid bike lanes because riders want to be dropped off in them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Waymo 表示无法完全避开自行车道，因为乘客希望在自行车道附近下车</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47912645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 185</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47912645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 255</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47912645'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Waymo notes that rider expectations and infrastructure realities may lead AVs to occasionally use bike lanes, highlighting safety, policy, and road design tensions in autonomous taxi deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Waymo 指出乘客期望与基础设施现实可能导致自动驾驶车在下车点接近自行车道，引发关于安全、政策及道路设计的矛盾。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.road.cc/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Waymo-London.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.road.cc/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Waymo-London.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47864785' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/sports/ping-pong-robot-ace-makes-history-by-beating-top-level-human-players-2026-04-22/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ping-pong robot beats top-level human players</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>乒乓机器人击败顶尖人类选手</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47864785' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 185</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47864785' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 259</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47864785'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A ping-pong robot defeated top-level human players, marking a milestone for robotics and AI-enabled sports. The result demonstrates rapid adaptation and precision in robotic control, with implications for research and future competition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一台乒乓球机器人击败了顶尖人类选手，标志着体育领域机器人与AI的一项重要里程碑。该成就展示了机器人对高速对打的控制与学习能力，对机器人研究、人与机器人的竞争以及运动训练系统具有启发意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47877715' data-tags='Hardware,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47877715' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://drobinin.com/posts/my-phone-replaced-a-brass-plug/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My phone replaced a brass plug</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的手机取代了一枚黄铜塞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47877715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 182</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47877715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47877715'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal tech anecdote about how a smartphone interrupted a hardware repair workflow by substituting for a brass plug, illustrating how modern devices increasingly blur between tools and components. The piece reflects on hardware minimalism, repair culture, and the limits of gadget-driven tinkering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一则个人科技趣闻，讲述智能手机在硬件维修中替代一枚黄铜塞的场景，显示现代设备让工具与部件的界线变得模糊。文章思考硬件极简、修理文化以及小工具时代的局限性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://drobinin.com/assets/previews/my-phone-replaced-a-brass-plug.png' data-full='https://drobinin.com/assets/previews/my-phone-replaced-a-brass-plug.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47902987' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47902987' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.happyfellow.dev/simulacrum-of-knowledge-work/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Simulacrum of Knowledge Work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>知识工作的拟像</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47902987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 181</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47902987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47902987'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Knowledge-work simulacrum explores how modern work can feel like rote, dashboard-driven automation rather than meaningful contribution. It argues for rethinking work around authentic impact, human judgment, and more transparent metrics in a world increasingly augmented by AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>知识工作的拟像批评当代工作往往被仪表板和自动化流程所支配，而非真正的有意义产出。作者主张在人工智能时代重新思考工作，将重点放在人类判断和真实影响上，而非空洞的指标。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.happyfellow.dev/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://blog.happyfellow.dev/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在车库门敞开时工作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851613' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 181</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851613' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 125</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47851613'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay argues for transparency in work by keeping the &#x27;garage door up&#x27;—sharing progress publicly to invite feedback, with trade-offs on privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章主张在工作中保持透明，通过公开进展来获取反馈，探讨开放与隐私之间的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47821801' data-tags='Hardware,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47821801' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jasoneckert.github.io/myblog/server-2025-arm64/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Windows Server 2025 Runs Better on ARM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows Server 2025 在 ARM 上运行更佳</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47821801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47821801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47821801'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Windows Server 2025 shows improved performance on ARM64 hardware, including better virtualization density and energy efficiency. This signals growing momentum for ARM in data centers and could influence future cloud architecture and licensing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Windows Server 2025 在 ARM64 硬件上性能提升，虚拟化密度和能效也有所改善。这标志着 ARM 在数据中心的应用日益增多，或将影响未来的云架构与许可策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jasoneckert.github.io/myblog/server-2025-arm64/windows-admin-center-snapdragon.png#center' data-full='https://jasoneckert.github.io/myblog/server-2025-arm64/windows-admin-center-snapdragon.png#center' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47823179' data-tags='Business,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47823179' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/04/18/in-the-uk-evs-are-cheaper-than-petrol-cars-thanks-to-chinese-competition/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>In the UK, EVs are cheaper than petrol cars, thanks to Chinese competition</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在英国，电动汽车因中国竞争而比汽油车更便宜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47823179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 178</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47823179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 172</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47823179'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UK EVs are cheaper than petrol cars, driven by intense Chinese competition, lower battery costs, and policy incentives. The shift reshapes consumer choice and tightens the race to accelerate the UK’s electrification and industrial strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国电动汽车因中国厂商的强力竞争、更低的电池成本与政策激励而已成为更便宜的选择。这一变化改变了消费者偏好，也为英国的新能源转型与产业战略带来新的压力点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2023/03/electric-Cab-London-LEVC.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2023/03/electric-Cab-London-LEVC.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47780743' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sheets.works/data-viz/keyboard-sounds' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mechanical Keyboard Sounds – A listening Museum</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>机械键盘声音——一个聆听博物馆</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47780743' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 178</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47780743' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47780743'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A data-driven collection of acoustic profiles for different keyboard switches and keycaps, exploring how hardware design shapes sound. The project invites readers to explore the sonic textures of typing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目以数据可视化方式收集不同机械键盘的声音特征，展示按键声与触感之间的关系。通过声音档案，读者可以直观比较不同开关、结构对音色的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sheets.works/og-keyboard-sounds.png' data-full='https://sheets.works/og-keyboard-sounds.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47900398' data-tags='Open Source,Gaming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47900398' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(201) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/MartinGalway/C64_music' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Martin Galway&#x27;s music source files from 1980&#x27;s Commodore 64 games</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Martin Galway 的1980年代 Commodore 64 遊戲音樂源檔</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47900398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47900398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47900398'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archive release of Martin Galway&#x27;s music source files from 1980s Commodore 64 games. The materials offer a rare glimpse into early chiptune composition, revealing how Galway layered simple waveforms and timing tricks to craft catchy tunes within severe hardware constraints. For vintage-game enthusiasts and reproducible retro sound design, it&#x27;s a goldmine.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公開了 Martin Galway 在1980年代 Commodore 64 遊戲的音樂源檔，提供珍貴的早期像素音樂創作見解。這些檔案揭示了在有限硬體下，如何運用簡單波形與節奏技巧創作出上癮的音樂。對復古遊戲音效與重現者而言，這是寶貴資源。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fc713b44b5222fe7600701dffddc8270c3cc27751e1433c38512814f27f206c2/MartinGalway/C64_music' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fc713b44b5222fe7600701dffddc8270c3cc27751e1433c38512814f27f206c2/MartinGalway/C64_music' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://interblah.net/self-updating-screenshots' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Self-updating screenshots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自更新截图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47908051' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47908051' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47908051'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores techniques for keeping screenshots in sync with live apps, enabling true-time demonstrations without manual re-captures. The piece weighs automation, determinism, and integration with recording tooling to streamline documentation and demos. This matters for faster, more reliable product showcases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍让截图自动跟随实时应用变化的技术与工具，避免人工重复截屏以展示最新界面。讨论自动化、确定性以及与录制工具的集成对加速文档与演示的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47822805' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Hardware,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47822805' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(204) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.usenix.org/system/files/conference/woot17/woot17-paper-guri.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SPEAKE(a)R: Turn Speakers to Microphones for Fun and Profit [pdf] (2017)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SPEAKE(a)R：将扬声器变成麦克风以实现乐趣与盈利（2017）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47822805'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates that loudspeakers can be repurposed as microphones, enabling covert data capture via acoustic channels. The paper analyzes the hardware and signal-processing tricks that make this possible and discusses privacy and security implications for consumer devices and embedded systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了扬声器可以被逆向用作麦克风，从而通过声学信道实现隐蔽数据获取。论文解析了实现这一方法的硬件与信号处理要点，并讨论了对消费电子和嵌入式系统的隐私与安全隐患及应对思路。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47897790' data-tags='Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47897790' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alash3al.github.io/stash?_v01' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open source memory layer so any AI agent can do what Claude.ai and ChatGPT do</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源记忆层：让任意 AI 代理具备长期记忆，达到 Claude/ChatGPT 的能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47897790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47897790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47897790'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-source memory layer aims to give AI agents long-term context and recall, bridging the gap to proprietary chat models. By persisting knowledge across sessions, agents can deliver more coherent and capable interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个开源的记忆层旨在让 AI 代理具备长期上下文与记忆能力，填补与专有聊天模型之间的差距。跨会话持久化知识使代理能提供更连贯、更强的交互能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stash.alash3al.com/og-image.png' data-full='https://stash.alash3al.com/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47914677' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47914677' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(206) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/science-nature/butterflies-are-in-dramatic-decline-across-north-america-a-close-look-at-the-western-monarch-shows-why-180988582/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Butterflies are in decline across North America, a look at the Western Monarch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>北美蝴蝶数量下降：以西部君蝶为例</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47914677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47914677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47914677'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Butterflies are in decline across North America, with Western Monarchs emblematic of broader pollinator losses. Habitat loss, pesticide exposure, and climate shifts are central pressures, signaling ecosystem-wide consequences that extend beyond butterflies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>北美蝴蝶数量持续下降，西部君蝶成为更广泛传粉者损失的缩影。栖息地消失、农药暴露和气候变化是主要压力，预示着对生态系统的广泛影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/-pUy-tpdhYEeFuT3-STWXN5xIIc=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(2784x1856:2785x1857)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/f0/cc/f0cccc48-0697-4243-89b0-1e93aa195dc2/dsc_0152.jpg' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/-pUy-tpdhYEeFuT3-STWXN5xIIc=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(2784x1856:2785x1857)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/f0/cc/f0cccc48-0697-4243-89b0-1e93aa195dc2/dsc_0152.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47893060' data-tags='Politics,Finance,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47893060' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/04/24/us/politics/companies-consumers-tariff-refunds.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tariffs Raised Consumers&#x27; Prices, but the Refunds Go Only to Businesses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关税提高了消费者价格，退税却主要发放给企业</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47893060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47893060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47893060'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tariffs raised the price of goods for consumers, but refunds appear to go mainly to businesses rather than individuals. The outcome highlights how policy levers can transfer costs away from consumers and prompts questions about who truly benefits from tariff relief and how to improve equity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关税抬高了消费者的购买成本，退税却主要发放给企业而非个人。此现象凸显贸易政策对普通消费者的实际影响及其公平性，引发对改进退税机制的讨论。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47822549'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Modern rendering relies on advanced culling to skip unseen geometry, boosting frame rates. The piece reviews techniques from occlusion to frustum and mesh-level pruning, with tradeoffs for real-time graphics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现代渲染通过先进的裁剪技术跳过不可见几何体，提升帧率。文章回顾从遮挡剔除到视锥裁剪、网格级别剔除等方法及其在实时图形中的权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>墓园地下网络中的蜂群：多达800万只蜜蜂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47810101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47810101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47810101'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scientists have mapped an underground bee network under a cemetery housing up to eight million bees, revealing a surprisingly complex subterranean ecosystem. The find informs urban pollinator habitats and resilience of city ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科学家在墓园下方发现一个地下蜂群网络，数量高达800万只，揭示了一个相当复杂的地下生态系统。此发现为城市授粉者栖息地及城市生态的韧性提供新视角。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47891872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47891872' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 215</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece argues for patients to refuse let doctors record certain parts of their medical visits, citing privacy risks and potential misuse of recorded data. It offers practical steps for asserting control over one’s medical information, including consent choices and data retention limits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章提出在就诊时应拒绝让医生对部分内容进行记录，以降低隐私风险与数据滥用的可能。作者给出实用的做法，帮助患者掌控自己的就医信息，包括明确同意、限制数据保留等措施。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates impressive inference speed with a 27B parameter model using optimization on consumer GPUs, hitting 207k tokens per second. <p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IEA：全球首次太阳能超越所有能源来源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47831272' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47831272' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 123</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47831272'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The IEA reports solar overtook all energy sources as the largest source of global electricity, a historic first.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>国际能源署宣布太阳能成为全球最大的电力来源，首次超越所有能源。文章讨论对电网、储能和政策的影响，随着太阳能份额持续增长。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47900478' data-tags='Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47900478' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(218) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fabiensanglard.net/discret11/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Discret 11, the French TV encryption of the 80s (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Discret 11：80年代法国电视加密</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47900478' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47900478' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47900478'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discret 11 is a vintage French TV encryption scheme from the 1980s examined in depth. The write-up analyzes how the system worked, including its key management and hardware implementation, and why it ultimately failed to stop piracy, highlighting the era&#x27;s cryptographic limits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Discret 11 是上世纪80年代法国电视的加密方案，文中对其工作原理、密钥管理和实现方式进行了分析。并解释了为何该系统未能有效阻止盗版，反映了当时加密技术的局限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fabiensanglard.net/discret11/cplus_anim.gif' data-full='https://fabiensanglard.net/discret11/cplus_anim.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47864454'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DuckDB 1.5.2 expands cross-environment support, including browser WASM execution, enabling analytics without a backend. This release makes it easier to ship data tooling offline or in client-side apps, aligning with portable analytics trends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DuckDB 1.5.2 扩展跨环境支持，新增在浏览器中通过 WASM 运行的能力，使在无后端环境下也能进行分析成为现实。该版本让数据工具更易离线或在客户端应用中使用，符合便携式分析的趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cdxd0xxp0jko' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Department of Justice has officially reclassified cannabis as less dangerous</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国司法部正式将大麻降级为风险较低</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47875629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47875629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 203</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47875629'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The U.S. Department of Justice has officially reclassified cannabis as less dangerous, signaling a shift in federal stance on a substance long treated as high-risk. The move could affect research access, banking, and state-federal policy alignment, while inviting continued policy debates. Expect downstream changes in enforcement priorities and public health programs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国司法部正式将大麻降级为风险较低，标志着联邦层面对该物质的态度发生转变。此举可能影响研究许可、银行业务以及州与联邦政策的协调，同时引发持续的政策辩论。未来在执法重点和公共卫生规划方面可能出现变化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/ca67/live/348f0210-3e71-11f1-8f78-2b3776d2d1d0.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/ca67/live/348f0210-3e71-11f1-8f78-2b3776d2d1d0.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(222) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ky.fyi/posts/ai-burnout' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Do I belong in tech anymore?</a></p>

<p class='hn-title'>(223) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lists.gnupg.org/pipermail/gnupg-announce/2026q2/000504.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GnuPG – post-quantum crypto landing in mainline</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GnuPG——后量子加密进入主线</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47907018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47907018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47907018'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GnuPG is incorporating post-quantum cryptography into its mainline, signaling real-world readiness for quantum-era security. This move strengthens open-source crypto and could influence how people securely exchange emails and sign data at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GnuPG 的主线代码现已接纳后量子加密算法，标志着量子时代安全的实际落地。此举提升了开源密码学的能力，可能影响大规模邮件加密与数据签名的实际应用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47851077'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful data visualization maps cheeses onto a periodic-table-like grid, sorting varieties by flavor notes, texture, and origin. The project showcases how familiar scientific visuals can make culinary diversity approachable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个有趣的数据可视化项目，将奶酪按风味、质地和产地等特征排成类似元素周期表的网格。这样的展示让美食世界以科学化的方式变得更易理解，也展示了数据可视化在食品领域的潜力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://huggingface.co/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V4-Pro' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek-V4: Towards Highly Efficient Million-Token Context Intelligence</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek-V4：迈向高效百万令牌上下文智能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47885014' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47885014' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47885014'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek-V4 demonstrates an approach to highly efficient million-token context intelligence, enabling long-context reasoning at scale. By optimizing memory usage and retrieval, it supports richer document understanding and long-horizon dialogue. This advancement could empower enterprise search, AI assistants, and research tools that must reference extensive histories.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek-V4 展示了在百万级上下文下实现高效推理的思路，通过优化内存和检索能力，支持对大规模文档的理解与长时对话。此进展可能推动企业检索、AI 助手和需要大量历史信息参考的研究工具的发展。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这款基于 CRDT 的图数据库实现了类型安全的实时协作，能够在离线场景下实现冲突自由的并发编辑。对于复杂图结构的应用场景，提供更鲁棒的协作体验。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/legal/government/ex-ceo-ex-cfo-bankrupt-ai-company-charged-with-fraud-2026-04-17/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ex-CEO, ex-CFO of iLearningEngines charged with fraud</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47828225' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47828225' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47828225'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Former CEO and CFO of iLearningEngines have been charged with fraud tied to the bankrupt AI company, signaling ongoing scrutiny of leadership and finances in AI startups. The case underscores governance challenges in the sector.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>iLearningEngines 的前任CEO与CFO因涉及欺诈而被起诉，聚焦于这家AI公司的领导与财务治理问题。此案凸显人工智能初创领域的治理挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47840618' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47840618' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://slate.com/business/2026/04/florida-state-orange-food-houses-real-estate.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What killed the Florida orange?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>佛州橙子死因何在？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47840618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47840618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47840618'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece traces how Florida oranges fell from prominence as diseases and drought, pests, and real estate-driven land-use squeezed growers; it connects agricultural decline with housing booms and farmland value shifts. It highlights complex ecosystems of farming, property markets, and policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章梳理了佛州橙子产业衰落的原因——柑橘黄化病、虫害、气候变化与土地使用等因素交织，以及其对地产和劳动市场的连锁影响，揭示农业、房地产与政策之间的复杂关系。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47810531'>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>定向微波技术使3D打印机能够在广泛材料基底上融合电路，显著提升快速原型设计和定制化外形的潜力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(232) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://oilprice.com/Alternative-Energy/Geothermal-Energy/Americas-Geothermal-Breakthrough-Could-Unlock-a-150-Gigawatt-Energy-Revolution.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>America&#x27;s Geothermal Breakthrough</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国地热领域的突破或引发150吉瓦能源革命</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47903945'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A geothermal breakthrough could unlock up to 150 GW of clean, baseload power if deployed at scale. The advancement highlights geothermal as a reliable complement to solar and wind, with implications for grid reliability and energy policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>地热技术实现突破，若大规模部署，理论上可提供多达约150GW的清洁基荷发电。此进展显示地热能源在稳定电网、与风光互补方面的潜力，以及对能源政策的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d32r1sh890xpii.cloudfront.net/article/1200x675/2026-04-23_qw906tcvvx.jpg' data-full='https://d32r1sh890xpii.cloudfront.net/article/1200x675/2026-04-23_qw906tcvvx.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(233) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/introducing-workspace-agents-in-chatgpt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Workspace Agents in ChatGPT</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ChatGPT 的工作区代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866860' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866860' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47866860'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ChatGPT adds workspace agents to orchestrate tasks across apps from within the chat interface. This enables cross-tool automation and collaboration, with governance and privacy considerations for enterprise use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ChatGPT 增加工作区代理，可在对话界面内跨应用编排任务，推动跨工具的自动化与协作，同时需关注企业级治理与隐私。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47790041' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://darkounity.com/blog/how-i-learned-unity-the-wrong-way' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I learned Unity the wrong way</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我用错法学习 Unity</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47790041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47790041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47790041'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author reflects on learning Unity the hard way, sharing rookie pitfalls like skipping fundamentals, overreliance on templates, and neglecting performance profiling. The piece advocates a steadier approach: start small, understand the component model, and prioritize performance and cross-platform considerations. Practical tips help newcomers build robust, scalable Unity projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者分享在学习 Unity 时走的弯路，提醒初学者避免对组件模型、场景层级和性能分析的忽视。文章给出更稳妥的学习路径与开发实践要点。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(236) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://skyandtelescope.org/astronomy-news/astronomers-find-the-edge-of-the-milky-way/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Astronomers find the edge of the Milky Way</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>天文学家发现银河系边缘</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47879239' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47879239' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47879239'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astronomers have traced the edge of the Milky Way, refining our understanding of the galaxy&#x27;s size and structure. The finding informs models of mass distribution and the Milky Way’s interaction with its surroundings, with implications for dark matter estimates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>天文学家确认了银河系的边界，进一步明确了银河的大小与结构。该发现有助于改进对质量分布的模型，以及银河与周边天体的相互作用，从而影响对暗物质分布的推断。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(237) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/04/17/climate/japan-cherry-blossom-database-scientist.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Japan&#x27;s cherry blossom database, 1,200 years old, has a new keeper</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47811668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47811668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47811668'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 1,200-year cherry blossom phenology database in Japan gets a new custodian, signaling continuity for climate research and cultural records. The transfer underscores how long-running datasets enable trend analysis in bloom timing amid changing seasons.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本跨越1200年的樱花开花观测数据库迎来新管理员，确保长期数据的连续性。该数据对研究极端气候影响与季节性趋势具有重要价值，同时也是日本文化记忆的一部分。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47901734'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI launches a GPT-5.5 bio bug bounty to surface vulnerabilities in how the model handles biological content and bio prompts. The move signals growing emphasis on bio-safety and responsible disclosure in AI development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 推出 GPT‑5.5 生物领域漏洞赏金计划，鼓励研究者发现模型在处理生物内容与提示时的风险点。此举凸显在 AI 发展中对生物安全与负责任披露的日益重视。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>旧金山国际机场 daily 噪音减少约90分钟，提升了旅客舒适度和工作效率。这一改动反映了旨在创造更安静、可预测旅行体验的机场设计取向。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47826834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47826834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47826834'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discusses pervasive quality issues in scientific datasets due to copy-paste errors, undermining reproducibility and ML training. Calls for stronger data curation, automated checks, and richer metadata practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出科学数据集中普遍存在拷贝粘贴等质量问题，严重影响结果的可复现性和机器学习模型的训练效果。提出通过数据清洗、自动化验证与元数据治理等手段提高数据质量。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47904224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 147</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47904224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 123</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47904224'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Serious Eats tests practical methods to reduce bean-related gas, including soaking, cooking times, and enzyme considerations. The results offer actionable tips for smoother digestion when eating legumes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Serious Eats 测试了多种减少豆类胀气的方法，包括浸泡、烹煮时间和酶处理等，并给出可操作的建议，帮助更顺畅地消化豆类。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(245) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nial.se/blog/less-human-ai-agents-please/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Less human AI agents, please</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>请减少人类化 AI 代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47845429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 143</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47845429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47845429'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As AI systems grow more capable, the author argues against stacking too many human-like AI agents. Simpler, more auditable autonomous agents reduce risk and confusion. The post advocates practical design principles for trustworthy AI that avoids unnecessary human-on-the-loop complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着 AI 系统能力的提升，作者主张减少使用拟人化的 AI 代理。更简单、可审计的自主代理能降低风险与复杂性，为构建可信的 AI 系统提供实用思路。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(246) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://miod.online.fr/software/openbsd/stories/vaxfp.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Math Is Hard – OpenBSD Stories</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47876930'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenBSD anecdotes reveal the challenges of correctness and reliability in systems software, with math and numeric mishaps illustrating why formal correctness matters. The stories blend humor and depth about low-level programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenBSD 故事揭示了在系统软件中追求正确性与可靠性的挑战，涉及数学与数值错误，凸显形式正确性的重要性。这些故事将幽默与对底层编程的深刻理解结合起来。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47835411' data-tags='Show HN,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47835411' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(248) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mediator.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Mediator.ai – Using Nash bargaining and LLMs to systematize fairness</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Mediator.ai — 用 Nash 讨价与大语言模型来系统化公平性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47835411'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mediator.ai combines Nash bargaining with large-language models to formalize notions of fairness in negotiation and decision-making. The project aims to systematize fair outcomes by encoding bargaining principles into AI-assisted workflows, potentially improving governance of collaborative systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mediator.ai 将 Nash 讨价原理与大语言模型结合起来，将公平性概念形式化，用于谈判与决策。通过把博弈论原理编码进 AI 驱动的工作流，旨在提升协作系统的公平性与可解释性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mediator.ai/assets/og-image.png' data-full='https://mediator.ai/assets/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47823365' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47823365' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(249) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://clojure.org/reference/transducers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Clojure: Transducers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Clojure：Transducers（转换器）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47823365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47823365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47823365'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Clojure&#x27;s transducers are composable data transformation abstractions that decouple processing from input and output collections. They enable efficient, reusable pipelines and reduce boilerplate. This concept is central for functional programming enthusiasts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Clojure 的 Transducers（转换器）是一组可组合的数据转换抽象，能够将处理与输入/输出集合解耦，提升效率并实现可复用的流水线。对函数式编程爱好者而言，这是核心概念。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://clojure.org/images/clojure-logo-120b.png' data-full='https://clojure.org/images/clojure-logo-120b.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47839645' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47839645' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/gizmo64k/soulplayer-c64' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Soul Player C64 – A real transformer running on a 1 MHz Commodore 64</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Soul Player C64：在1MHz Commodore 64上运行的真实 Transformer</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47839645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 140</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47839645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47839645'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retro AI project demonstrates a Transformer model running on a 1 MHz Commodore 64, pushing the boundaries of what’s possible on vintage hardware. The effort highlights optimization tricks, memory management, and low-power inference strategies that make AI feasible on extreme constraints. It’s a playful yet instructive reminder that edge AI can start with hardware you already own.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项复古 AI 项目在 1MHz 的 Commodore 64 上实现了 Transformer 模型，挑战了这类老旧硬件的边界。该工作展示了内存管理、模型优化和低功耗推理等技巧，使 AI 推理在极限约束下成为可能。这个案例既有趣又具启发性，提醒我们边缘 AI 的起点可以是手边的旧设备。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/216ab9fd97ac007517db0a98bfdf84fb578780a7bb2678b5265d6d689bff2d0f/gizmo64k/soulplayer-c64' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/216ab9fd97ac007517db0a98bfdf84fb578780a7bb2678b5265d6d689bff2d0f/gizmo64k/soulplayer-c64' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47825423' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(253) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ctvnews.ca/politics/article/pm-carney-declares-us-ties-now-a-weakness-in-address-to-canadians/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PM Carney declares U.S. ties now a &#x27;weakness&#x27; in address to Canadians</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>卡尼总理在对加拿大人的讲话中称美方关系为‘弱点’</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47825423'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canadian Prime Minister Carney characterizes U.S. ties as a weakness in a speech to Canadians, signaling a shift in tone toward the U.S. relationship. The remark could reshape tech policy, cross-border collaboration, and security considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>卡尼总理在对加拿大人的讲话中将美方关系称为“弱点”，释放出对美加关系重新评估的信号。这一表态可能影响科技政策、跨境合作与安全议题的优先级。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一封发给游戏出版商的引人注目的冷邮件揭示了编辑偏好与潜在合作机会。文章分析了语气、结构和时机等要素，为独立开发者和工作室提供实用的 outreach 指南。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47842021' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47842021' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://morgin.ai/articles/even-uncensored-models-cant-say-what-they-want.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Even &#x27;uncensored&#x27; models can&#x27;t say what they want</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>即使是‘不受审查’的模型也说不出它们想说的内容</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47842021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47842021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47842021'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that even uncensored AI models operate under hard constraints—through training data, alignment efforts, and policy defaults—limiting what they can output. It discusses implications for freedom of expression and the design of future language models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>即便是“未审查”的模型，也受到训练数据、对齐目标和安全策略等硬性约束的影响，导致它们无法随心所欲输出内容。文章讨论对未来语言模型设计的影响与取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://morgin.ai/assets/covers/euphemismbench-flinch-profile.jpeg' data-full='https://morgin.ai/assets/covers/euphemismbench-flinch-profile.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47900506'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A benchmark suite using lambda calculus tasks to stress test AI reasoning and symbolic processing. It provides a lens into how models handle functional abstractions and compositional reasoning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以 λ 演算任务为基础的 AI 基准测试集合，用于评估模型在符号推理与函数式抽象上的表现。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47861703' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47861703' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(257) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/cars/2026/04/catls-new-lfp-battery-can-charge-from-10-to-98-in-less-than-7-minutes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CATL&#x27;s new LFP battery can charge from 10 to 98% in less than 7 minutes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>宁德时代新型 LFP 电池7分钟内完成从10%到98%充电</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47861703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 137</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47861703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47861703'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CATL unveils a lithium iron phosphate battery that can recharge from 10% to 98% in under seven minutes, signaling a potential leap for fast-charging EVs. Real-world performance will depend on thermal management and charger availability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>宁德时代宣布一种新型 LFP 电池，能够在不到七分钟的时间内完成10%到98%的充电，或将推动电动车快充技术的突破。实际表现受热管理与充电基础设施等因素影响，需要在真实场景中验证。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47844890' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47844890' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(258) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/cocaine-pollution-gives-salmon-wanderlust' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Salmon exposed to cocaine and its main byproduct roam more widely</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>暴露于可卡因及其副产物的鲑鱼迁徙范围扩展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47844890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 136</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47844890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47844890'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research finds salmon exposed to cocaine and its main byproduct exhibit increased wandering, altering migratory routes. The study highlights how trace contaminants affect aquatic behavior and ecosystem dynamics, with implications for pollution management and wildlife conservation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示暴露于可卡因及其副产物的鲑鱼会增加游荡，迁徙路径因此改变。这项研究揭示微量污染物对水生动物行为和生态系统的影响，对污染治理和野生动物保护具有意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WireGuard for Windows hits version 1.0, delivering a native kernel-backed VPN experience on Windows with improved performance and simpler configuration. The milestone matters for enterprise and personal security, enabling faster, leaner VPN deployments across Windows devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Windows 版 WireGuard 达到1.0 版本，带来原生内核支持的 VPN 体验，性能更好、配置更简洁。这一里程碑对企业与个人的安全通信尤为重要，便于在 Windows 设备上更高效地部署 VPN。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(261) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://orchidfiles.com/stigma-is-a-tax-on-every-operational-decision/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The operating cost of adult and gambling startups</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>成人与赌博初创企业的运营成本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47889279' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47889279' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 212</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47889279'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stigma adds a real operating tax on adult and gambling startups, influencing risk appetites, pricing, and partnerships. The piece argues that social stigma translates into tangible costs across customer acquisition, financing, and compliance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>污名化成为成人及赌博领域初创企业的真实运营成本，影响风险偏好、定价与合作关系等多方面。文章认为社会污名会在获客、融资与合规等环节转化为具体成本。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47865822' data-tags='Show HN,Open Source,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47865822' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Infisical/agent-vault' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Agent Vault – Open-source credential proxy and vault for agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Agent Vault – 面向代理的开源凭据代理与保管库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47865822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47865822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47865822'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Agent Vault is an open-source credential proxy and vault designed for agents. It centralizes secret management, rotates credentials, and provides auditing for tool usage, aiming to simplify secure automation workflows. If you operate AI or software agents, this could reduce credential sprawl and improve security posture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Agent Vault 是一个为代理设计的开源凭据代理与保管库。它集中管理机密、轮换凭证并提供审计，旨在简化安全自动化工作流。对于运行 AI 或软件代理的团队，可以减少凭证碎片并提升安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1194056092/a7ca50e4-a2c1-4561-922e-5f16faf868c6' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1194056092/a7ca50e4-a2c1-4561-922e-5f16faf868c6' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47860682' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47860682' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(263) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/orsonscottcard/status/2046702294406680751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You don&#x27;t need advice from editors on rejected manuscripts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>被拒稿的稿件不需要编辑的建议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47860682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47860682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47860682'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take arguing editors aren’t the fix for every rejected manuscript; writers should trust their own judgment, revise independently, or start anew rather than wait for editorial salvage. It highlights tensions between author autonomy and editorial gatekeeping in the publishing process.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这条推文提出“被拒稿的稿件不需要编辑的建议”这一观点，主张作者应独立自我修订或直接重新起草，而不依赖编辑来挽救被拒稿的作品，凸显作者自主与编辑指导之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/355846335/OrsonScottCard_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/355846335/OrsonScottCard_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47777715' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47777715' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nanopass.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nanopass Framework: Clean Compiler Creation Language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nanopass 框架：干净的编译器创建语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47777715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47777715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47777715'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nanopass provides a modular, low-friction approach to writing compilers by composing passes. It emphasizes separation of concerns, enabling easier reasoning about correctness and transformations, and has influenced language tooling design by making compiler pipelines more approachable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nanopass 通过将编译过程拆分为可组合的阶段，提供模块化、低耦合的编译器构建方式。它强调职责分离，便于验证正确性并简化变换逻辑，对语言工具链设计产生了持续影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nanopass.org/banner.png' data-full='https://nanopass.org/banner.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47902993' data-tags='Culture,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47902993' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/1899550/1/11/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hokusai and Tesselations</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>北斎与镶嵌图案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47902993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47902993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47902993'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This collection explores how Hokusai&#x27;s prints intersect with tessellation ideas—repeating motifs, grids, and spatial tiling in Edo-era art. It reveals a dialogue between Japanese aesthetics and early geometric thinking that informs both art history and mathematics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本集藏品探讨北斎作品与镶嵌图案之间的关系——重复的几何风格、网格与平铺在他的版画中的应用。通过展现日本美学与早期几何思维之间的对话，带来艺术史与数学的交叉视角。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47849592' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47849592' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(266) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://e360.yale.edu/digest/great-white-sharks-climate' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>As oceans warm, great white sharks are overheating</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>海洋变暖使大白鲨过热</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47849592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47849592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47849592'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Global ocean warming is pushing great white sharks toward higher body temperatures and stressed metabolism, with shifts in distribution and hunting behavior. The trend could reshape predator-prey dynamics and shark habitats, with broader ecosystem implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球海洋变暖使大白鲨体温升高、代谢受压，导致分布和捕猎行为出现变化。此趋势可能改变捕食者与猎物的动态以及鲨鱼栖息地，对生态系统产生更广泛的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/Great-White_Wikipedia.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1776762631&amp;s=9449d79f7186d96a82d0ca990473e42b' data-full='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/Great-White_Wikipedia.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1776762631&amp;s=9449d79f7186d96a82d0ca990473e42b' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47885739' data-tags='Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47885739' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(267) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mceglowski.substack.com/p/why-not-venus' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Not Venus?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何不是金星？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47885739' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 128</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47885739' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47885739'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative argument that Venus deserves serious consideration as a space frontier, challenging common assumptions about Mars as the default destination.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章挑衅性地主张金星应被认真考虑作为太空前沿，挑战将火星视为默认目的地的常规观点。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47834213' data-tags='Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47834213' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(268) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47834213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: How to solve the cold start problem for a two-sided marketplace?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：如何解决双边市场的冷启动问题？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47834213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 128</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47834213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47834213'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A discussion on strategies to tackle cold-start for two-sided marketplaces, including demand seeding, onboarding incentives, and growth loops to kickstart network effects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论应对双边市场冷启动的策略，涵盖需求引导、入驻激励和增长循环等，推动早期网络效应的建立。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47900440' data-tags='Web,Programming,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47900440' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(269) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/nakagami/grdpwasm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A web-based RDP client built with Go WebAssembly and grdp</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于 Go WebAssembly 和 grdp 的网页 RDP 客户端</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47900440' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 127</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47900440' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47900440'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates a web-based RDP client built with Go WebAssembly and the grdp library, enabling remote desktop access directly in the browser. The project illustrates how WASM can bring near-native UI responsiveness and performance to browser-based remote sessions, with considerations for security and user experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示一個以 Go WebAssembly 與 grdp 驅動的網頁 RDP 客戶端，實現在瀏覽器中進行遠端桌面連線的可能性。這類實作顯示 WASM 在網頁端的性能與回應性，並需關注安全性與使用者體驗的取捨。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c143f3880e6d7dc2853828cdf33ed65bc154abbbe4f9c5ea27c5f0c849946d93/nakagami/grdpwasm' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c143f3880e6d7dc2853828cdf33ed65bc154abbbe4f9c5ea27c5f0c849946d93/nakagami/grdpwasm' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47823460' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47823460' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(270) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://teamchong.github.io/turboquant-wasm/draw.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Prompt-to-Excalidraw demo with Gemma 4 E2B in the browser (3.1GB)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Prompt 转 Excalidraw 的浏览器演示（Gemma 4 E2B，3.1GB）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47823460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 127</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47823460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47823460'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates a browser-based pipeline that turns natural language prompts into Excalidraw sketches using Gemma 4 E2B models loaded in WASM. With a hefty 3.1GB model, it showcases the feasibility of offline, in-browser creative generation and the practical limits of client-side AI tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了一个在浏览器中运行的管线，能将自然语言提示转化为 Excalidraw 草图，使用 Gemma 4 E2B 模型以 WASM 形式加载。模型体积达到 3.1GB，体现了离线、浏览器端创作生成的可行性，同时也揭示了客户端 AI 工具在资源和性能上的现实限制。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47902746' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(271) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nbcnews.com/world/iran/iran-caused-extensive-damage-us-military-bases-publicly-known-rcna331853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iran caused more extensive damage to U.S. military bases than publicly known</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊朗对美军基地造成的损害比公开信息更广泛</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47902746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47902746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 169</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47902746'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New assessments indicate Iran caused more extensive damage to U.S. bases than officially disclosed, affecting deterrence calculations and regional security dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新评估显示，伊朗对美军基地造成的损害程度比官方披露的更广泛，影响威慑评估和区域安全格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2026-03/260312-shahed-drones-jg-a64c75.jpg' data-full='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2026-03/260312-shahed-drones-jg-a64c75.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
