<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47913406' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47913406' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(272) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.barwypowstania.pl/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The 1944 Warsaw Uprising, in Color</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1944 年华沙起义：彩色影像</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47913406'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Colorized footage of the 1944 Warsaw Uprising provides a visceral view of a pivotal World War II event, helping audiences comprehend scale, emotion, and human experience beyond monochrome archives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>彩色还原的1944年华沙起义影像为观众带来更直观的战争关键场景、情感与人类经历，超越单色档案的观感。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/638cbcb09d4abbe84e0cd95d/63a07436caead82db7c2c86e_Rolling-4s-64px.svg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/638cbcb09d4abbe84e0cd95d/63a07436caead82db7c2c86e_Rolling-4s-64px.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47903541' data-tags='Design,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47903541' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(273) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jordanlord.co.uk/blog/3-constraints/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Three constraints before I build anything</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在动手前的三个约束</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47903541'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocates three non-negotiables before starting any build: define the core problem, constrain scope, and set measurable success criteria. The framework helps prevent scope creep and accelerates delivery by focusing on what truly matters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在动手前，作者提出三个不可谈判的约束：明确核心问题、限定范围、设定可衡量的成功标准。通过聚焦要点，这一框架有助于防止范围蔓延并加速落地。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47862541' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47862541' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(274) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://clad.you/blog/posts/questionnaire-mlp/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 3D Body from Eight Questions – No Photo, No GPU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无需照片、无 GPU 的八问就能生成3D人体</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47862541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47862541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47862541'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 3D body can be generated from eight questions without needing photos or a GPU. This approach highlights privacy-preserving, computation-light pathways for personal avatar creation, with potential use in games and metaverses. It also raises questions about accuracy and bias in de-identified data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过八个问题就能生成3D人体模型，无需照片或显卡驱动。这种方法为个人化头像的隐私友好、低算力实现提供了可行路径，适合游戏与元宇宙场景。也需关注生成结果的准确性与偏差问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://clad.you/blog/images/questionnaire-mlp/og-image_hu13524579386116829050.jpg' data-full='https://clad.you/blog/images/questionnaire-mlp/og-image_hu13524579386116829050.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47890841' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Open Source,AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47890841' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(275) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/browser-use/browser-harness' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Browser Harness – Gives LLM freedom to complete any browser task</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Browser Harness 让 LLM 自由完成任意浏览器任务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47890841' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47890841' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47890841'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Browser Harness provides a framework for LLMs to control a browser directly, enabling a wide range of automated tasks without brittle wrappers. It lowers friction for building end-to-end browser automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Browser Harness 提供一个直接让 LLM 控制浏览器的框架，使自动化任务覆盖面更广且不易出错。降低了构建端到端浏览器自动化的门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5c4d5b26c93a34c06fcab288d01fb02d346b37e2862a853e35742a3775c6bb5c/browser-use/browser-harness' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5c4d5b26c93a34c06fcab288d01fb02d346b37e2862a853e35742a3775c6bb5c/browser-use/browser-harness' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47850212' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47850212' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(276) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.brex.com/crabtrap' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CrabTrap: An LLM-as-a-judge HTTP proxy to secure agents in production</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CrabTrap：将 LLM 作为裁判的 HTTP 代理，用于在生产环境中保护代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47850212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47850212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47850212'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CrabTrap is an HTTP proxy that uses an LLM as a judge to enforce safety policies for production agents. It can veto actions, provide explainable decisions, and help secure deployments, though it may introduce latency and reliance on the model.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CrabTrap 是一个将 LLM 作为裁判的软件代理，通过 HTTP 代理实现对生产环境中代理行为的安全策略强制执行。它能够拒绝不安全操作、提供可解释的决策，并提升部署安全，但可能带来延迟与对模型的依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.brex.com/api/proxy-image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fbrand.brex.com%2Ftransform%2Fd355afa8-98bc-4424-be0c-236bb6bf77ed%2FCrabTrap-landing-page-preview%3Fio%3Dtransform%3Afit%2Cwidth%3A1200' data-full='https://www.brex.com/api/proxy-image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fbrand.brex.com%2Ftransform%2Fd355afa8-98bc-4424-be0c-236bb6bf77ed%2FCrabTrap-landing-page-preview%3Fio%3Dtransform%3Afit%2Cwidth%3A1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(277) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thermodynamicsbook.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Free textbook on engineering thermodynamics</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47911013'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comprehensive, open-access textbook on engineering thermodynamics is freely available online, spanning fundamentals, cycles, and practical applications. It can save students money and serve as a reliable reference for courses, labs, and self-study.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一部工程热力学的免费电子教材现已在线开放获取，涵盖基础、循环与实际应用等内容。为学生、教师和自学者提供了省钱且可靠的参考资料。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(278) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://icv2.com/articles/news/view/62176/r-i-p-louis-zocchi-the-godfather-dice' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Louis Zocchi, games industry pioneer, has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>游戏业先驱路易斯·佐基奇去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47845231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47845231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47845231'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Louis Zocchi, a cornerstone in dice design and game accessibility, has passed away. His contributions helped shape the hobby and industry for decades.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>路易斯·佐基奇，游戏行业的里程碑人物之一，去世。他在骰子设计和让游戏更易获得方面的贡献影响了数十年的业界与爱好者。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(279) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eblong.com/infocom/visi/zork1/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Visible Zorker: Zork 1</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可视化 Zorker：Zork I</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 119</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47911644'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Visible Zorker showcases Zork I in a visible/interactive format that reveals the game&#x27;s state and parser workings as you play. It serves as a folk-history glimpse into interactive fiction and makes classic text adventures approachable for newcomers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>‘可视化 Zorker’ 将 Zork I 以可视化/交互的形式呈现，揭示玩家操作时解析器和游戏状态的运作。它为交互式小说的历史提供直观展示，也让新玩家更易接触这类文本冒险游戏。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47888124' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47888124' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(280) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jeroen.github.io/notes/webassembly-tar/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mounting tar archives as a filesystem in WebAssembly</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 WebAssembly 中将 tar 存档挂载为文件系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47888124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 118</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47888124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47888124'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A WebAssembly project mounts tar archives as a filesystem, letting WASM apps access archive contents as if they were on disk. This enables streaming access, random I/O, and offline packaging for web apps without server round-trips. It demonstrates WASM filesystem interoperability and could simplify data packaging for browser apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 WebAssembly 项目把 tar 存档挂载成一个文件系统，WASM 应用可以像访问真实磁盘一样访问归档内容。这样的实现支持流式访问、随机 I/O，以及网页应用的离线打包，减少服务器往返。也展示了 WASM 的文件系统互操作性，可能简化浏览器端的数据打包与访问。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jeroen.github.io/notes/webassembly-tar/preview.png' data-full='https://jeroen.github.io/notes/webassembly-tar/preview.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47857563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47857563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47857563'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to implementing an FPS counter, covering time delta measurement, smoothing, and integration with render loops. It weighs accuracy against overhead and shows simple code patterns you can adapt in game or graphics apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供實作帧率計數器的實用指南，說明如何計算時間增量、進行平滑處理，以及與渲染循環的整合。討論在準確性、開銷與響應性之間的取捨，並給出可直接套用的範例程式碼。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(282) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medium.com/@iainmcgin/zero-copy-protobuf-and-connectrpc-for-rust-69bda8ac0f02' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zero-copy protobuf and ConnectRPC for Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 的零拷贝 Protobuf 与 ConnectRPC</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47801200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47801200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47801200'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A technical overview of zero-copy protobuf patterns in Rust using ConnectRPC, showing how to minimize allocations and copies in RPC workloads. It highlights practical gains in latency and memory safety, and discusses tradeoffs when adopting zero-copy patterns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍在 Rust 中通过零拷贝 Protobuf 与 ConnectRPC 来减少 RPC 场景中的拷贝和分配，展示对延迟与内存安全的实际提升，并讨论采用零拷贝模式的取舍。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47841141' data-tags='Culture,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47841141' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(283) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c51y797v200o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Desmond Morris has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Desmond Morris 去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47841141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47841141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47841141'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Desmond Morris, the influential anthropologist and author of The Naked Ape, has died at 92. His interdisciplinary work connected biology, psychology, and culture, shaping how popular audiences think about human nature.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Desmond Morris，这位影响深远的人类学家及《裸体猿》的作者，已去世，享年92岁。他将生物学、心理学与文化研究相融合，深刻影响了公众对人性的理解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/317a/live/6b90c220-70e7-11ef-bb5b-c361f7cd4381.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/317a/live/6b90c220-70e7-11ef-bb5b-c361f7cd4381.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(284) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.millersbookreview.com/p/no-books-are-not-remotely-too-expensive' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Books are not too expensive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>书并非太贵</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47835647'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that books aren’t prohibitively expensive when you consider value, digital access, and libraries. It emphasizes that a healthy reading habit pays dividends and that price alone shouldn’t deter readers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者辩称就价值、数字化获取和图书馆资源等因素来看，书籍并非昂贵。阅读的长期收益远超成本，价格不应成为读者的主要障碍。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47848162' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(285) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/how-a-renaissance-gambling-dispute-spawned-probability-theory/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Renaissance gambling dispute spawned probability theory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>文艺复兴赌博纠纷催生概率论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47848162' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47848162' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47848162'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A historical vignette shows how a dispute over gambling odds among Renaissance mathematicians contributed to the development of probability theory. The piece connects early games of chance to foundational ideas of statistics and risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一场文艺复兴时期关于赌博赔率的争论促进了概率论的发展。该文将早期赌博中的不确定性与统计与风险的基础思想联系起来。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47886773' data-tags='Design,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47886773' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(286) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cambra.dev/blog/announcement/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Composition shouldn&#x27;t be this hard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>组合不应如此困难</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47886773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47886773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47886773'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An announcement about simplifying software composition with a new approach or tool that reduces boilerplate and makes assembling components easier. It argues current practices force brittle, ad-hoc architectures and offers a path toward more modular, scalable design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该帖公布了一种简化软件组成的方案，提出通过新工具或方法降低样板代码并提升组件拼接的易用性。作者批评现有做法容易让架构变得脆弱，提出更模块化、可扩展的设计方向。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(288) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.vincentgregoire.com/faceoff/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Faceoff – A terminal UI for following NHL games</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47826104' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47826104' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<p class='hn-title'>(290) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lpeproject.org/blog/surveillance-pricing-exploiting-information-asymmetries/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Surveillance Pricing: Exploiting Information Asymmetries</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>监控定价：利用信息不对称</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47866395'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article explains how surveillance-based pricing exploits information asymmetry to extract more value from users through personalized pricing. It discusses implications for platform fairness, transparency, and protections against data misuse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章阐述监控定价如何利用信息不对称对用户实施个性化定价，从而榨取更多价值。讨论其对平台公平性、透明度和数据滥用防护的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47862230' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(291) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1068928/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kernel code removals driven by LLM-created security reports</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>内核代码移除由LLM创建的安全报告推动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47862230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47862230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47862230'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kernel maintainers are removing code in response to security reports generated with or by LLMs, reflecting a shift toward AI-assisted vulnerability discovery influencing core code. The trend raises questions about trust, automation in security review, and risk of overcorrection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>内核维护者基于由LLM创建的安全报告，移除了部分代码，体现了AI 辅助的漏洞发现对核心代码的影响。这一趋势引发对信任、自动化安全审查以及过度修正风险的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47832720' data-tags='AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47832720' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(292) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zknill.io/posts/all-your-agents-are-going-async/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>All your agents are going async</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>所有代理都在走向异步化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47832720' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47832720' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47832720'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI agents are transitioning to asynchronous architectures to improve throughput, concurrency, and fault tolerance in orchestration systems. The shift affects how developers design agent flows, handle promises, and coordinate between components.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 代理正向异步架构过渡，以提升吞吐量、并发性和编排系统的容错性。这一转变将影响开发者在设计代理流程、处理异步调用以及跨组件协作时的方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://zknill.io/img/header.png' data-full='https://zknill.io/img/header.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47886272' data-tags='Show HN,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47886272' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(293) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/NV404/gova' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Gova – The declarative GUI framework for Go</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Gova — Go 的声明式 GUI 框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47886272' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47886272' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47886272'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gova is a declarative GUI framework for Go that aims to simplify building user interfaces by expressing UI as declarative trees bound to Go data. It enables rapid UI development with less boilerplate and a more predictable rendering model.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gova 是一个供 Go 使用的声明式 GUI 框架，旨在通过将界面表达为与 Go 数据绑定的声明性树来简化界面开发。它提供更少的样板代码和更可预测的渲染模型，提升开发效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e0c339e7abf04d41ce4b55bce23a5607a1e4f38146de1a8feb0d185c6e8f32d0/NV404/gova' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e0c339e7abf04d41ce4b55bce23a5607a1e4f38146de1a8feb0d185c6e8f32d0/NV404/gova' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47862626' data-tags='Data,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47862626' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(294) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://buttondown.com/jaffray/archive/columnar-storage-is-normalization/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Columnar Storage Is Normalization</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>列式存储就是规范化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47862626' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 110</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47862626' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47862626'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that columnar storage achieves normalization-like benefits by organizing data for columnar access rather than row-oriented normalization alone. It explains why columnar layouts improve compression, IO efficiency, and analytics workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章提出列式存储在面向列访问的数据组织上实现了类似规范化的效果，相对于传统行存的规范化。它解释了列式布局如何提升压缩率、IO 效率以及分析型工作负载的性能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/ade45dbb-1818-4db8-8d05-2a35d8c321cc.png' data-full='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/ade45dbb-1818-4db8-8d05-2a35d8c321cc.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(295) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/articles/cn08jy6w0l5o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Smoking ban for people born after 2008 in the UK agreed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国同意对2008年以后出生的人实施禁烟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47847240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 110</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47847240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47847240'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Britain agrees to a policy that effectively creates a tobacco-free generation by restricting smoking for cohorts born after 2008, reflecting public health goals and potential long-term societal impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国同意通过限制2008年以后出生人群的吸烟权利来实现准禁烟代际的目标，体现公共卫生愿景及对社会长期影响的考量。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47797457' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(296) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.onesteppower.com/post/subsea-cable-repair' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How a subsea cable is repaired (2021)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>海底光缆修复</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47797457' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 110</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47797457' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47797457'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Subsea cable repair is a high-stakes, specialized operation involving ship-based spooling, careful trenching, and remotely operated vehicles. The piece outlines the logistical choreography, risk management, and the criticality of underwater fiber connectivity to global networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>海底光缆修复属于高风险的专业作业，涉及船载铺设、谨慎开沟和遥控水下机器人等技术。文章概述了这一作业的物流调度、风险控制，以及海底光纤连接对全球网络的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.wixstatic.com/media/9d72ca_4ae3f2bdca234a79abf5be7172f88877~mv2.jpg/v1/fill/w_768,h_552,al_c,q_85/9d72ca_4ae3f2bdca234a79abf5be7172f88877~mv2.jpg' data-full='https://static.wixstatic.com/media/9d72ca_4ae3f2bdca234a79abf5be7172f88877~mv2.jpg/v1/fill/w_768,h_552,al_c,q_85/9d72ca_4ae3f2bdca234a79abf5be7172f88877~mv2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47848195' data-tags='Hardware,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47848195' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(297) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tindie.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tindie store under &quot;scheduled maintenance&quot; for days</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tindie 商店长期处于计划维护状态</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47848195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 109</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47848195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47848195'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Tindie store has stayed in scheduled maintenance mode for days, affecting visibility and sales for makers. The outage highlights the fragility of niche hardware marketplaces and the need for clearer status indicators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 Tindie 商店多日处于计划维护状态，影响创客的曝光和销售。此事凸显了小众硬件市场的脆弱性以及对透明状态更新的需求。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47898660' data-tags='Health,Education'>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍人体解剖中的罕见变异与有趣特征，挑战标准教材的叙述。强调这些例外对医学理解与教育的启示。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47832366' data-tags='Design,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47832366' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(299) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martinalderson.com/posts/figmas-woes-compound-with-claude-design/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Figma&#x27;s woes compound with Claude Design</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Figma 的困境因 Claude Design 而加剧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47832366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47832366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47832366'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that Figma’s challenges are compounded by Claude Design’s entry, intensifying competition in design tooling and AI-assisted workflows. It discusses implications for pricing, feature parity, and platform strategy as AI features blur tool boundaries. Readers should watch how Figma adapts to this evolving AI-enabled design landscape.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 Figma 的挑战因 Claude Design 的进入而加剧，扩大了设计工具市场的竞争与 AI 辅助工作流的压力。讨论定价、功能对等与平台战略的影响，因为 AI 特性正在模糊工具边界。读者应关注 Figma 如何应对日益 AI 化的设计生态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://martinalderson.com/img/og/figma-s-woes-compound-with-claude-design.png' data-full='https://martinalderson.com/img/og/figma-s-woes-compound-with-claude-design.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47821853' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47821853' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(300) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fitzgen.com/2024/02/06/safe-gc.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Garbage Collection Without Unsafe Code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在不使用不安全代码的情况下实现垃圾回收</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47821853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47821853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47821853'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rust&#x27;s safe garbage collector demonstrates you can implement memory management without unsafe blocks, by using safe abstractions and tracing/GC techniques. This approach improves safety guarantees for systems programming while showing practical performance parity with unsafe implementations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在纯安全 Rust 中实现垃圾回收，证明无需 unsafe 代码也能完成内存管理。通过安全抽象实现跟踪/回收等技术，为系统编程提供更安全的内存方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://licensebuttons.net/l/by-sa/4.0/88x31.png' data-full='https://licensebuttons.net/l/by-sa/4.0/88x31.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47903173' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(301) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://coalton-lang.github.io/mine/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mine, an IDE for Coalton and Common Lisp</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mine：为 Coalton 与 Common Lisp 打造的 IDE</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903173' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903173' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47903173'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mine is an IDE tailored for Coalton and Common Lisp, offering integrated editing, a REPL, and tooling designed for Lisp workflows. It showcases ongoing work in Coalton and Lisp toolchains, aiming to improve productivity for power users. This matters for developers who want modern tooling without leaving Lisp ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mine 是专为 Coalton 与 Common Lisp 打造的集成开发环境，提供编辑、REPL 与工具链，旨在提升 Lisp 开发的效率。它体现了 Lisp 生态的活跃与 Coalton 与 CL 的互操作性。对想要保留 Lisp 生态同时追求现代工作流的开发者尤为有用。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47820791' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(302) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MuJoCo – Advanced Physics Simulation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MuJoCo：高级物理仿真</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47820791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47820791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47820791'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MuJoCo is a high-fidelity physics simulator used in robotics and AI research, enabling fast, accurate dynamics for articulated agents and environments. The project continues to evolve to support more complex models and integration with ML workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MuJoCo 是用于机器人与人工智能研究的高保真物理仿真器，支持对关节式代理和环境的快速、准确动力学计算。该项目持续发展，进一步支持更复杂的模型及与机器学习工作流的整合。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/562fa83f179c00a18f464148db00232cd3e932498da9e127e3a6982882305a4c/google-deepmind/mujoco' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/562fa83f179c00a18f464148db00232cd3e932498da9e127e3a6982882305a4c/google-deepmind/mujoco' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical look at how prompts to multiple AI models appear in Nginx logs, revealing patterns in AI traffic versus referrals. It highlights implications for measuring AI usage and mitigating bot traffic.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(306) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cssence.com/2024/six-levels-of-dark-mode/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Six Levels of Dark Mode (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>深色模式的六个层级</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47826502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47826502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47826502'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Six levels of dark mode provide a practical framework that ranges from basic contrast tweaks to adaptive, accessible, and brand-aligned themes. Each level guides decisions about contrast, color temperature, readability, and motion to balance usability with consistency across platforms. The framework helps teams implement thoughtful, scalable theming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>六个层级的深色模式为界面美学与可用性提供了一个实用分级框架，从对比度、色温到无障碍性与品牌一致性都给出设计路线。帮助团队在多平台间保持一致性，同时提升可用性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(307) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tempest.homemade.systems' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tempest vs. Tempest: The Making and Remaking of Atari&#x27;s Iconic Video Game</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tempest 与 Tempest：雅达利经典电子游戏的诞生与再造</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47871195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47871195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47871195'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on the making of Atari&#x27;s Tempest and its later remakes or reinterpretations. It examines how the game&#x27;s design, hardware, and legacy have shaped modern arcade design and preservation culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾雅达利经典射击游戏 Tempest 的诞生与再造，分析其设计、硬件与遗产如何影响现代街机设计与游戏保存文化。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47868063' data-tags='Energy,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47868063' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(308) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropocenemagazine.org/2025/04/new-study-compares-growing-corn-for-energy-to-solar-production-its-no-contest/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New study compares growing corn for energy to solar production</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新研究：以玉米生物能源对比太阳能发电，结论显而易见：太阳能更具优势</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47868063' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47868063' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47868063'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new study finds solar power outperforms using corn for energy in terms of land use and efficiency, challenging the idea that crop-based energy is competitive with solar. It underscores photovoltaics as a scalable, low-carbon solution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究显示，从玉米生物能源转化的土地利用与效率方面，太阳能发电远优于玉米生物能源，挑战以作物生产能源的观点，强调光伏在大规模低碳能源中的优势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.anthropocenemagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/corn-ethanol-and-solar.jpg' data-full='https://www.anthropocenemagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/corn-ethanol-and-solar.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47897706' data-tags='Science,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47897706' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.blender.org/user-stories/cosmology-with-geometry-nodes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cosmology with Geometry Nodes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Blender：用几何节点构建的宇宙学场景</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47897706' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47897706' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47897706'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cosmology with Geometry Nodes demonstrates using Blender&#x27;s geometry nodes to procedurally generate cosmic structures. It showcases artists and scientists leveraging procedural tools to visualize galaxies, nebulae, and spacetime phenomena, enabling repeatable experiments and educational visuals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>演示如何用 Blender 的几何节点来流程化生成宇宙结构，如星系、星云和时空现象。示例表明艺术家与科学家都能利用流程化工具实现可重复的天文视觉效果，适合教育与科学可视化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.blender.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/CosmologyWithGN-480x270.png' data-full='https://www.blender.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/CosmologyWithGN-480x270.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(312) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arpitbhayani.me/blogs/defensive-databases/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agentic AI systems violate the implicit assumptions of database design</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代理性 AI 系统违反数据库设计的隐性假设</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47897140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47897140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47897140'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Agentic AI systems challenge implicit database design assumptions, raising concerns about data integrity, governance, and accountability as autonomous agents access and modify data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>代理性 AI 系统挑战数据库设计中的隐性假设，引发对数据完整性、治理与问责的关注，尤其在自治代理访问和修改数据时。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47830332' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(313) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://neugierig.org/software/blog/2026/04/theseus.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Theseus, a Static Windows Emulator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Theseus：一个静态的 Windows 模拟器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47830332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 97</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47830332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47830332'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Theseus is a static Windows emulator exploring lightweight paths to Windows compatibility without dynamic binaries. The project highlights research directions in OS emulation, safety, and portability. It appeals to systems researchers and enthusiasts exploring alternative compatibility models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Theseus 是一个静态的 Windows 模拟器，探索用轻量化方式实现 Windows 兼容性的可能性，无需动态二进制。该项目指向操作系统仿真、安全性与可移植性的研究方向，吸引系统研究者与对另类兼容模型感兴趣的爱好者。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47781950' data-tags='AI,Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47781950' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(314) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://browser-use.com/posts/prove-you-are-a-robot' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Prove you are a robot: CAPTCHAs for agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>证明你是机器人：为代理设计的验证码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47781950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 97</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47781950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47781950'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CAPTCHAs are being contemplated as a gatekeeping tool for AI agents, raising questions about how to distinguish automated agents from humans in dynamic environments. The concept highlights security, privacy, and governance considerations for AI-enabled systems. Practical implications include how to design robust tests that don’t hinder legitimate use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 CAPTCHA 用作区分人类与代理的认证机制，反映出对AI代理安全与信任框架的探索。该思路将影响人工智能代理的设计、隐私与可用性，并对系统治理提出挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://browser-use.com/api/og?title=Prove+You+Are+a+Robot%3A+CAPTCHAs+for+Agents&amp;date=Apr+13%2C+2026&amp;author=Luka+Secilmis' data-full='https://browser-use.com/api/og?title=Prove+You+Are+a+Robot%3A+CAPTCHAs+for+Agents&amp;date=Apr+13%2C+2026&amp;author=Luka+Secilmis' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(316) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dystroy.org/blog/box-to-save-memory/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Box to save memory in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 中的 Box：节省内存的思路</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47878231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47878231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47878231'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines how Box&lt;T&gt; can reduce memory usage in Rust in certain patterns, weighing heap allocation costs against layout improvements and potential performance gains. The guidance helps readers decide when boxing is beneficial or避免不必要的装箱.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨在特定模式下，Box&lt;T&gt; 如何降低 Rust 的内存占用，权衡堆分配成本与布局优化及潜在性能收益，帮助读者判断何时值得使用 Box，何时应避免不必要的装箱。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47846861' data-tags='Science,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47846861' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(317) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.courthousenews.com/preserved-for-billions-of-years-organic-compounds-found-on-mars/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Diverse organic molecules on Mars revealed by the first SAM TMAH experiment</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>火星上首次 SAM TMAH 实验揭示多样有机分子</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47846861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47846861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47846861'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mars&#x27; SAM TMAH experiment revealed a diverse suite of organic molecules, expanding what we know about organics on the planet&#x27;s surface. While this supports ongoing questions about past habitability, interpretation must carefully account for Martian chemistry and potential contamination.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过首个 SAM TMAH 实验，在火星发现了多种有机分子。这一发现拓展了对火星表面有机物的认识，表明曾经存在能够保持有机物的环境，但解读需谨慎，因为表面氧化剂和风化过程可能混淆信号。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://courthousenews.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/mars-rover-800x600.jpg' data-full='https://courthousenews.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/mars-rover-800x600.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47905304' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(318) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fosstodon.org/@carlrichell/116460505717380644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Colorado Adds Open-Source Exemption to Age-Verification Bill</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科罗拉多州在年龄验证法案中加入开源豁免</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47905304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47905304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47905304'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Colorado adds an open-source exemption to its age-verification bill, letting open-source tools satisfy age checks and avoiding vendor lock-in. The change helps privacy-preserving, auditable identity workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科罗拉多州在年龄验证法案中加入开源豁免，允许使用开源工具完成年龄检查，减少对单一供应商的依赖，并支持更可审计的隐私身份工作流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fosstodon.org/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://fosstodon.org/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47890873' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47890873' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(319) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Different language models learn similar number representations</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不同语言模型学习到相似的数字表示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47890873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47890873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47890873'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Different language models converge on similar numerical representations despite architectural differences, indicating shared inductive biases in how they process numbers. This alignment may enable cross-model transfer of number-related reasoning and suggests robust numeric grounding across models. The result informs how we probe, compare, and compose AI systems for math-heavy tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管架构不同，语言模型在数字表示上趋于一致，显示出对数字处理的共性偏置。这种对齐或为跨模型的数值推理迁移带来机会，表明模型在数值理解方面具有更稳定的基础。研究也为在数学相关任务中对AI系统进行比较与组合提供了参考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47823654' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47823654' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(320) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/smart-contact-lens-glaucoma-microfluidics' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Contact Lens Uses Microfluidics to Monitor and Treat Glaucoma</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>隐形眼镜利用微流控监测并治疗青光眼</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47823654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 92</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47823654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47823654'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Prototype smart contact lens uses microfluidics to monitor glaucoma biomarkers in tears and can deliver drug treatment through microchannels. The technology aims to enable continuous monitoring of biomarkers and on-demand treatment, potentially improving adherence and disease control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>原型智能隐形眼镜集成微流控系统，能够从泪液中监测青光眼相关生物标志物，并通过微通道分发药物治疗。此类设计旨在实现持续监测与按需治疗，提升依从性与疾病控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=65561020&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C625%2C0%2C625' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=65561020&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C625%2C0%2C625' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(321) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://felixbarbalet.com/familiarity-is-the-enemy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Familiarity is the enemy: On why Enterprise systems have failed for 60 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>熟悉感是敌人：企业系统为何60年来屡次失败</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47885668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47885668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47885668'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Longstanding familiarity with legacy architectures is blamed for persistent enterprise-system failures. The piece calls for abandoning dogmatic habits in favor of pragmatic observability, composable services, and fresh mental models to modernize.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张长期以来对现有架构的依赖和熟悉感是企业系统长期失败的根源。文章呼吁抛弃守旧思维，转向以可观测性、可组合服务和新思维模式为核心的现代化路径。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(322) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-04-monumental-ship-burial-beneath-ancient.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Monumental ship burial beneath ancient Norwegian mound predates the Viking Age</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>挪威古墩下的巨型船葬早于维京时代</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47804178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47804178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47804178'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archaeologists have uncovered a monumental ship burial beneath an ancient mound in Norway, dating to before the Viking Age. The discovery shifts the timeline of Norse burial practices and maritime capabilities, offering new clues about prehistoric social structure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>考古学家在挪威一座古墩下发现一座大型船葬，年代早于维京时代。这一发现将挪威早期葬俗与海上工艺的时间线向前推进，提供关于史前社会结构的新线索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/2026/monumental-ship-burial-1.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/2026/monumental-ship-burial-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

The response highlights ongoing concerns about supply-chain and third-party tooling in AI workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 针对涉及 Axios 的开发者工具安全事件给出应对措施，强调风险缓释、密钥轮换与滥用监控等关键步骤，反映出在 AI 工作流中对供应链与第三方工具的持续关注。</p>

The strategy signals a shift toward hybrid cloud and on-device intelligence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道指出谷歌正在押注 AI 边缘计算以缩短与 AWS、Microsoft 的差距，目标是降低延迟、提升隐私保护推理能力，并在边缘释放新工作负载。这一策略预示云端与边缘融合、以及设备内智能的新趋势。</p>

Scarcity and prestige drive rising prices and a blend of professional gear with collectible value.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>二手 La Marzocco 意式咖啡机在咖啡馆经营者与收藏家之间需求强劲，二手市场因此繁荣，品牌的耐用性与稳定性是关键因素。供给稀缺和声望效应推高了价格，使专业设备逐渐具备收藏价值。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47835366' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47835366' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(331) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://perthirtysix.com/how-the-heck-does-shazam-work' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How the Heck does Shazam work?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Shazam到底是如何工作的？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47835366'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shazam uses audio fingerprinting to identify songs from short samples, matching against a vast database with robust hashing that tolerates noise and pitch changes. The article breaks down the pipeline from capture to fingerprinting, database lookup, and confident results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Shazam 通过音频指纹对短样本进行识别，与庞大数据库进行哈希比对，能在嘈杂环境和音高偏移中保持鲁棒性。文章梳理了从录制、指纹提取、到数据库检索和给出高置信度结果的全过程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://perthirtysix.com/img/shazam-cover.png' data-full='https://perthirtysix.com/img/shazam-cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47837105' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47837105' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(332) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.princeton.edu/~pkrugman/interstellar.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Theory of Interstellar Trade [pdf] (1978)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>星际贸易理论（1978）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47837105' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47837105' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47837105'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A classic 1978 thought experiment that extends trade theory to interstellar commerce, showing how transport costs and economies of scale shape exchanges between worlds. It serves as a playful yet instructive lens on macro modeling and international trade logic.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是1978年的经典思考实验，将贸易理论扩展到星际贸易，展示运输成本与规模经济如何影响星际间的交换。它以玩笑但具启发性的方式，帮助理解宏观模型和国际贸易逻辑。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47817639' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(333) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.johndcook.com/blog/2026/04/17/fp4/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>4-bit floating point FP4</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47817639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47817639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47817639'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introducing FP4, a compact 4-bit floating-point format for resource-constrained tasks, examining its precision, performance, and suitability for ML or embedded workloads. Discusses trade-offs between hardware simplicity and numerical accuracy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 FP4，这是一种用于资源受限任务的四位浮点数格式，分析其精度、性能及在 ML 与嵌入式场景中的适用性，并探讨硬件简化与数值精度之间的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47908139' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47908139' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(334) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ciechanow.ski/exposing-floating-point/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Exposing Floating Point – Bartosz Ciechanowski (2019)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>揭示浮点数的局限——Bartosz Ciechanowski</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47908139' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47908139' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47908139'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into how floating-point representation leads to surprising rounding errors and precision pitfalls in everyday code. It offers pragmatic tips for writing numerically robust algorithms and understanding when to use integers, fixed-point, or higher precision.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入分析浮点数表示带来的舍入误差与精度陷阱，以及在日常编码中如何避免它们。提供写出数值鲁棒算法的实用建议，讲解何时使用整数、定点或更高精度的方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ciechanow.ski/images/anchor.png' data-full='https://ciechanow.ski/images/anchor.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(335) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Arc1011/KernelUNO' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ibuilt a tiny Unix‑like &#x27;OS&#x27; with shell and filesystem for Arduino UNO (2KB RAM)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Arduino UNO（仅 2KB RAM）构建的微型 Unix‑like 操作系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47851630'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>I built a tiny Unix-like OS with a shell and filesystem for Arduino UNO, constrained to 2KB RAM. It pushes the boundaries of what small microcontrollers can run and demonstrates minimalist OS design. A provocative example for embedded systems enthusiasts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在仅有 2KB RAM 的 Arduino UNO 上构建了一个微型的类 Unix 操作系统，包含 shell 与文件系统。此举挑战了微控制器的极限，展示了极简操作系统设计的可能性，适合嵌入式系统爱好者参考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0c1a780ca6fbea5cc1b3cd4260b86ee058de4a3e629087c7ffddbd7c221716a1/Arc1011/KernelUNO' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/0c1a780ca6fbea5cc1b3cd4260b86ee058de4a3e629087c7ffddbd7c221716a1/Arc1011/KernelUNO' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47870245'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lute provides a standalone runtime for Luau, enabling Luau scripting outside Roblox. It broadens the ecosystem for Luau developers building tools and games beyond Roblox.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lute 提供 Luau 的独立运行时，使 Luau 脚本语言能够在 Roblox 之外使用。它提高了 Luau 的可移植性与性能，为在 Roblox 之外构建工具和游戏的开发者带来新选项。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Show HN project called YourMemory experiments with AI memory that decays over time, achieving about 52% recall. The approach prompts questions about long-term context management and memory-aligned AI behavior.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(340) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/microsoft/sudo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sudo for Windows (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 的 sudo</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47828853'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft introduces a sudo-like tool for Windows, enabling non-root commands with elevated privileges and unifying developer workflows with Linux environments. It signals a shift toward more Linux-style privilege management on Windows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软推出 Windows 的 sudo 工具，让在 Windows 上的命令提升权限更加类似 Linux 的 sudo 行为。该功能有助于统一开发工作流、提升权限管理的一致性，同时提升安全性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(341) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blisscast.wordpress.com/2026/04/21/windows-2-gui-wonderland-12a/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Only one side will be the true successor to MS-DOS – Windows 2.x</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MS-DOS 的真正繼任者只有一個：Windows 2.x</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47900451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47900451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47900451'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores an alternate-history angle where Windows 2.x could have been the true successor to MS-DOS, contrasting GUI-centric design with DOS&#x27;s command-line heritage. The piece reflects on how early GUI decisions shaped modern Windows architecture and developer workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探討若 Windows 2.x 成為 MS-DOS 的真正繼任者，會如何改寫歷史，對比圖形介面導向設計與命令列遺產。文章反映早期 GUI 决策如何塑造現代 Windows 架構與開發者工作流程。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47884625' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(342) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.sebastianwick.net/posts/how-hard-is-it-to-open-a-file/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Hard Is It to Open a File?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>打开一个文件有多难？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47884625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47884625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47884625'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open file operations can be surprisingly fiddly across languages and runtimes, with tradeoffs between low-level OS calls and high-level abstractions. The piece dissects how design choices affect reliability, performance, and developer experience, and provides mental models to reason about file access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>跨语言和运行时打开文件的过程往往错综复杂，底层系统调用与高层抽象之间存在权衡。本文剖析了设计选择如何影响可靠性、性能与开发体验，并提供理解文件访问的思维模型。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47866215' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47866215' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(343) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nikomc.com/2026/04/22/pcr/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PCR is a surprisingly near-optimal technology</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PCR竟然是接近最优的技术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47866215'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PCR is argued to be near-optimal given physical constraints, suggesting there may be limited room for dramatic efficiency gains over current PCR-based diagnostics and amplification methods.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 PCR 在物理约束下接近最优，说明在现有 PCR 基础上的效率提升空间可能有限，尤其在诊断与扩增应用中。讨论了速度、成本和准确性之间的权衡，以及对现场检测的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nikomc.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/pcr.jpg' data-full='https://nikomc.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/pcr.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
