<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47857509' data-tags='Energy,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47857509' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(344) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/04/global-growth-in-solar-the-largest-ever-observed-for-any-source/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Global growth in solar &quot;the largest ever observed for any source&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球太阳能增长创历史最高纪录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47857509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47857509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47857509'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Global solar capacity growth is the largest ever observed for any energy source, driven by rapid deployment and falling costs. The surge accelerates the energy transition and has implications for grids, storage, and policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球太阳能容量增长速度达到有史以来任何能源来源的最高水平，得益于部署速度提升和成本下降。这一增长推动能源转型，对电网、储能和政策带来深远影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/GettyImages-1322960375-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/GettyImages-1322960375-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47851377' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47851377' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(345) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://argemma.com/blog/long-lived-keys/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You don&#x27;t want long-lived keys</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你不该使用长期有效的密钥</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47851377'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues for short-lived keys and frequent rotation to limit exposure from leaks or compromises. It warns that long-lived keys silently accumulate risk and proposes strategies like ephemeral credentials and automatic rotation in secure systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张使用短寿命密钥并频繁轮换，以降低被泄露或被攻破时带来的风险。长期密钥会悄然累积风险，建议在安全系统中采用短期凭证和自动轮换等策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://argemma.com/og-image.png' data-full='https://argemma.com/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47877811' data-tags='Gaming,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47877811' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(346) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asteriskmag.com/issues/14/shall-we-play-a-game' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The route from Prussian military headquarters to Gary Gygax’s basement</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从普鲁士军事总部到 Gary Gygax 的地下室之路</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47877811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47877811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47877811'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article traces how military and strategic thinking from Prussian headquarters influenced the origin of modern tabletop gaming, culminating in Gary Gygax’s basement-era creations. It highlights how hierarchical planning and war-gaming culture seeded a hobby that evolved into RPGs and nerd culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文追溯普鲁士军事总部的战略思想如何影响现代桌面游戏的起源，最终在 Gary Gygax 的地下室中孕育出早期角色扮演游戏。揭示了等级化的规划与战争博弈文化如何催生这一爱好，并演变成如今的 RPG 与科幻次文化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://asteriskmag.com/media/pages/issues/14/shall-we-play-a-game/a08c01c037-1776727502/jon-peterson-wargaming-1600x900-crop-sharpen50.png' data-full='https://asteriskmag.com/media/pages/issues/14/shall-we-play-a-game/a08c01c037-1776727502/jon-peterson-wargaming-1600x900-crop-sharpen50.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47874631' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47874631' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(347) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/04/sneaky-spam-in-conversational-replies-to-blog-posts/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spam in conversational replies to blog posts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>博客文章对话式回复中的垃圾邮件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47874631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47874631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47874631'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post analyzes how spam has infiltrated conversational reply threads on blogs, using bot-generated, human-like interactions to spread spam or harvest engagement. It covers tactics, detection strategies, and the need for better anti-spam tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章分析了垃圾邮件如何渗透博客的对话式回复，利用类人机器对话来传播垃圾信息或刷取互动。介绍了作案手法、检测策略以及更强的反垃圾工具的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Antispam-Bee.webp' data-full='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Antispam-Bee.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47871817' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47871817' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(348) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.scattered-thoughts.net/writing/borrow-checking-without-type-checking/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Borrow-checking without type-checking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无需类型检查的借用检查</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47871817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47871817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47871817'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece proposes borrow-checking without relying on a full type checker, exploring lightweight or alternative mechanisms to enforce safe aliasing and mutation. It weighs trade-offs and potential performance gains for language design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章提出在不依赖完整类型检查的前提下实现借用检查，探讨用轻量化或替代机制来强制正确的别名与变更控制。讨论了权衡、潜在的性能收益，以及此类思路对语言设计的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.scattered-thoughts.net/writing/borrow-checking-without-type-checking/box.webp' data-full='https://www.scattered-thoughts.net/writing/borrow-checking-without-type-checking/box.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(349) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.allaboutcircuits.com/news/how-two-motorola-transistors-became-the-worlds-default-npns/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Motorola’s 2N2222 and 2N3904 transistors became the default NPNs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>摩托罗拉的 2N2222 与 2N3904 晶体管如何成为默认的 NPN</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47810211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47810211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47810211'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An historical look at how Motorola’s 2N2222 and 2N3904 transistors became the de facto standard NPN devices, shaping electronics for decades.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾摩托罗拉的 2N2222 与 2N3904 如何成为默认的 NPN 晶体管，并影响了几十年的电子设计。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek-V4 debuts with fast inference capabilities and a path to verified RL using SGLang and Miles. It showcases how open-source tooling can bridge speed and safety in RL applications, with potential use in instruction following and planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek-V4 首次亮相，提供快速推理能力并通过 SGLang 与 Miles 实现验证型强化学习路径。展示了开源工具如何在 RL 任务中兼顾速度与安全性，并对指令执行与规划等场景具备潜在应用。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft&#x27;s Teams SDK now supports bringing your own agent into Teams, enabling AI-driven workflows and automated interactions inside channels and chats. The move foreshadows richer, agent-powered collaboration features and opens opportunities for developers to integrate custom assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软 Teams SDK 现支持将自有代理带入 Teams，实现 AI 驱动的工作流和聊天中的自动化交互。这一举措预示着更丰富的智能协作功能，并为开发者提供了集成自定义助手的新机会。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(355) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kcra.com/article/california-more-money-than-projected-newsom-miscalculated-budget/71056376' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>California has more money than projected after admin miscalculated state budget</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州预算因行政计算失误而多出资金</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47854125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47854125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47854125'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>California revises its budget upward after an administration miscalculation, freeing additional funds for spending, debt management, or tax relief. The episode highlights how budgeting errors can ripple through state finances and policy planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州在行政预算计算失误后上调预算，释放更多资金用于支出、债务管理或减税等用途。这一事件凸显了预算误差对州财政与政策规划的连锁影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(356) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://redixhumayun.github.io/databases/2026/04/14/zero-copy-pages-in-rust.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zero-Copy Pages in Rust: Or How I Learned to Stop Worrying and Love Lifetimes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust中的零拷贝页：从生命周期中解放出来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47789291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47789291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47789291'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into implementing zero-copy pages in Rust, showing how careful lifetime and borrow management can avoid data copies in database-like workloads. The piece illustrates Rust techniques for safe, high-throughput memory layouts and the trade-offs involved.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章深入探讨在Rust中实现零拷贝页的技巧，展示如何通过小心的生命周期与借用管理避免数据拷贝，从而提升数据库等工作负载的吞吐率。还讨论了这种做法的权衡与安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://redixhumayun.github.io/assets/img/zero-copy/read_write_lifecycle.png' data-full='https://redixhumayun.github.io/assets/img/zero-copy/read_write_lifecycle.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(357) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://evdc.me/blog/css-query' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CSS as a Query Language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将 CSS 当作查询语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47893427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47893427' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47893427'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proposes using CSS selectors as a formal query language for data extraction from HTML, highlighting how pattern matching with CSS can power scraping and data mining with a familiar syntax. The idea emphasizes declarative, browser-native querying.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出用 CSS 选择器作为对 HTML 进行数据提取的查询语言，展示用熟悉语法实现模式匹配、网页抓取和数据挖掘的潜力，强调声明式、浏览器原生的查询方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(358) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/3669940.3707274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Optimizing Datalog for the GPU</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47875597'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A research paper detailing methods to map Datalog queries to GPU architectures, achieving parallel dataflow and speedups for logic programming workloads. It highlights the feasibility of GPU acceleration for deductive databases and potential uses in big data reasoning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文介绍将 Datalog 查询映射到 GPU 架构的方法，通过并行数据流实现对逻辑编程工作负载的加速，展示在推理型数据库和大数据场景中使用 GPU 的可行性与潜在应用。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47901884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47901884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47901884'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cold-water immersion can trigger dangerous cardiac responses, especially within the first sixty seconds. The piece explains the physiology of cold shock and offers safety considerations for cold-water exposure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>跳入冷水可能引发危险的心脏反应，尤其是在最初的几秒钟内。文中解释了冷水冲击的生理机制，并给出涉水安全建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(368) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.recurse.com/blog/192-redesigning-the-recurse-center-application' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Redesigning the Recurse Center application to inspire curious programmers</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47892660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47892660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47892660'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Recurse Center redesigned its application to minimize credential bias and emphasize curiosity and learning potential. The update includes new prompts and evaluation criteria intended to surface creative problem solvers rather than résumés.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Recurse Center 重新设计申请流程，减弱对资历的依赖，强调好奇心与学习潜力。新流程引入新的问题和评估标准，旨在发现具备创造性解决问题能力的申请者。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(369) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ML supports existence of unrecognized transient astronomical phenomena</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47890456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47890456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47890456'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Machine learning models help identify transient astronomical events that are not cataloged, enabling discovery of rare phenomena and expanding observational capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>机器学习模型有助于识别尚未被编目、但可能存在的瞬变天文事件，推动发现稀有天体现象并扩展观测能力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(370) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260424-chernobyl-wildlife-forty-years-on' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chernobyl wildlife forty years on</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913382' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913382' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47913382'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Forty years after the disaster, wildlife in and around Chernobyl demonstrates resilience and ecological shifts, offering nuanced insights into radiation&#x27;s long-term ecological effects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>切尔诺贝利灾难四十年后，区域内外的野生动物展现出韧性与生态变迁，为放射性长期影响提供了更细致的解读。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47855832'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic appears to remove Claude Code from the Pro plan for new users, signaling a change in feature availability and pricing for code-generation. Existing customers may experience shifts in access or cost for that capability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 似乎将新用户的 Pro 计划中的 Claude Code 移除，标志着功能与定价策略的调整。现有用户在该功能的可用性与成本方面可能会有所变化。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(372) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://verus-lang.github.io/verus/guide/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Verus is a tool for verifying the correctness of code written in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Verus：用于验证 Rust 代码正确性的工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47840252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47840252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47840252'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Verus is a Rust verification framework that enables proving correctness properties of Rust programs, integrating formal methods with the Rust toolchain. It shows how rigorous proofs can coexist with practical Rust development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Verus 是一个 Rust 验证框架，支持对 Rust 程序的正确性属性进行形式化证明，并与 Rust 工具链结合，展示形式化方法在实际开发中的应用潜力。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(377) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pmasschelier.github.io/x86_64_strings/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Writing string.h functions using string instructions in asm x86-64 (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 x86-64 汇编中使用字符串指令编写 string.h 函数</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47807609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47807609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47807609'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A write-up demonstrates implementing common string.h routines in x86-64 using string instructions to squeeze extra performance, highlighting micro-optimizations and the care needed to avoid pitfalls. It shows how low-level hand-optimized code can complement modern compiler optimizations. A practical read for systems programmers chasing micro-ops gains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文档展示了在 x86-64 汇编中利用字符串指令实现 string.h 常用函数，凸显微优化与避免坑点的要点。该内容说明手工优化的低级代码如何与现代编译器优化共存。对追求微优化的系统程序员具有实用价值。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47893620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47893620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47893620'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CC-Canary is a Canary-like tool to detect early signs of regressions in Claude Code, helping maintain model quality and catch drift before it harms users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CC-Canary 是一个用于在 Claude Code 中发现回归早期信号的工具，有助于维持模型质量并在问题显现前捕捉漂移。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47884225' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47884225' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(379) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-04-physicists-revive-1990s-laser-concept.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Physicists revive 1990s laser concept to propose a next-generation atomic clock</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>物理学家复兴 1990 年代激光概念，提出下一代原子钟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47884225' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47884225' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47884225'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Physicists revived a 1990s laser concept to design a next-generation atomic clock with improved precision and stability. If realized, the clock could enable more accurate timekeeping for GPS, telecommunications, and fundamental physics tests.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>物理学家重新提出一项1990年代的激光概念，设计出更高精度和稳定性的下一代原子钟。如果落地，将提升全球定位、通信以及基础物理实验中的时间基准精度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/2026/physicists-revive-1990.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/2026/physicists-revive-1990.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(380) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.npmjs.com/package/ooko' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I spent 6 years building my Kanban as I hated how managers run the boards</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我花了六年打造自己的看板系统，因为我讨厌管理者管理看板</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47877277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47877277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47877277'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A long-form case study of building a personal Kanban tool as a response to manager-driven boards. It highlights friction with traditional Kanban governance and lessons for developer-led workflow tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者用六年的时间打造自己的看板工具，以回应对传统看板管理的不满。文章揭示了以开发者为中心的工作流工具如何解决治理和协作中的痛点。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47888694' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47888694' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(381) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.os2museum.com/wp/learn-something-old-every-day-part-xx-8087-emulation-on-8086-systems/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>8087 Emulation on 8086 Systems</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>8087 在 8086 系统上的仿真</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47888694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47888694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47888694'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Software emulation of the 8087 FPU on 8086 hardware shows how early PCs handled floating-point operations without dedicated hardware. The piece surveys methods used to emulate the FPU, including inline software routines and timing considerations, and reflects on performance trade-offs. This is a nostalgic look at how compatibility was maintained in constrained systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在没有硬件 8087 浮点协处理器的 8086 系统上，对 8087 的软实现曾经广泛使用。文章回顾了通过内联软件例程等技术来仿真浮点运算，并讨论了性能与时序的权衡。这也是对在受限环境中实现仿真层的有趣案例，适合对计算机体系结构感兴趣的读者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.os2museum.com/wp/wp-content/uploads/2012/02/os2floppy.jpg' data-full='https://www.os2museum.com/wp/wp-content/uploads/2012/02/os2floppy.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述 Notepad++ 面向 macOS 的社区移植项目，涵盖兼容性调整、性能考量和许可问题，展示社区项目如何将经典工具带入新的生态系统。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(383) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arkaung.github.io/interactive-turboquant/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TurboQuant: A First-Principles Walkthrough</a></p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive that rebuilds TurboQuant from first principles, stripping away hype to show how its core components fit together and where performance gains can be found. It offers practical guidance on architecture choices, data flow, and optimization strategies for developers. The takeaway is a practical blueprint for engineers evaluating or contributing to similar tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文从基础原理出发，对 TurboQuant 进行从零到一的分解，揭示核心思想、数据流和组件之间的互动。强调按第一性原理构建、发现优化点，为评估或贡献类似工具的工程师提供实践蓝图。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(386) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jchandra.com/posts/hae-ols/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>High-Fidelity KV Cache Summarization Using Entropy and Low-Rank Reconstruction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>高保真键值缓存摘要：基于熵与低秩重建</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47823549' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47823549' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47823549'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A method for summarizing large key-value caches uses entropy measures and low-rank reconstruction to reduce memory while preserving access patterns. It targets high-read, low-latency systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过利用熵度量和低秩重构，对大型键值缓存进行摘要，减少内存占用同时保留访问模式。该方法面向高读负载、低延迟要求的系统。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(387) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/04/25/your-money/fidelity-investments-fraud-alert.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Her life savings mysteriously disappeared after a systems glitch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>系统故障后她的全部积蓄神秘消失</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47905681' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47905681' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47905681'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The life savings vanished after a systems glitch, exposing the fragility of automated financial systems and the difficulty of rectifying digital faults. The case highlights the need for stronger consumer protections and robust error-correction processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>系统故障导致她的全部积蓄神秘消失，暴露了自动化金融系统的脆弱性以及纠错的困难。事件凸显了加强用户保护与健全错误纠正机制的必要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47866293' data-tags='Finance,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47866293' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(389) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/artificial-intelligence/google-cloud-customer-wakes-up-to-usd18-000-bill-despite-usd7-budget-thanks-to-forgotten-public-api-key-attacker-put-in-60-000-requests-and-blasted-through-usd1-400-spending-cap' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Cloud customer wakes up to $18,000 bill despite $7 budget</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google Cloud 客户设定7美元预算却被收1.8万美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866293' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866293' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47866293'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Google Cloud customer woke up to an $18k bill despite a $7 monthly budget after an attacker used a public API key to issue thousands of requests. It underscores the risk of unsecured keys and the need for better budget alerting and key hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名 Google Cloud 客户在预算仅为 7 美元的情况下，因公共 API 密钥被滥用、数万次请求而产生 1.8 万美元账单。说明了未保护的密钥风险，并需要改进预算告警与密钥管理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/YGsMMUMGmWx6peWh8ZooKC-1920-80.png' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/YGsMMUMGmWx6peWh8ZooKC-1920-80.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47892655' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47892655' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(390) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://parl.ai/projects/params_vs_compute/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Which one is more important: more parameters or more computation? (2021)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>参数更多还是计算更多：哪个更重要？（2021）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47892655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47892655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47892655'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of the size- vs compute- trade-off in AI shows that more compute can outperform simply increasing model size under certain conditions. The takeaway challenges the notion that scaling parameters is always the best route to better performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 AI 的规模与计算资源之争进行了分析，结果在某些情境下提高计算能力比单纯增大模型参数更有效。结论挑战了‘一味扩展参数即可提升性能’的观点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://parl.ai/static/img/icon.png' data-full='https://parl.ai/static/img/icon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47878737' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47878737' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(391) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/podcast/917029/software-brain-ai-backlash-databases-automation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>People Do Not Yearn for Automation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人们并不渴望自动化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47878737' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47878737' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47878737'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Verge podcast argues that people do not yearn for automation; instead, they seek control and meaningful work, with AI debates focusing on autonomy, job design, and societal impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这一集播客认为人们并不渴望普遍的自动化，而是追求掌控感和有意义的工作。讨论聚焦自动化对工作设计、自主性与社会影响的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/04/VRG_DCD_Software_Brain.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/04/VRG_DCD_Software_Brain.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(392) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xbow.com/blog/mythos-like-hacking-open-to-all' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-5.5: Mythos-Like Hacking, Open to All</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47879330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47879330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47879330'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocateur stance argues for a Mythos-inspired hacking ethos around GPT-5.5, advocating open access, hands-on experimentation, and community-driven security research. The piece champions lowering barriers so more researchers and enthusiasts can probe, tinker, and improve AI safety and capability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张在 GPT-5.5 周围建立类似 Mythos 的黑客文化，推动开放获取、动手实验和以社区为驱动的安全研究。强调降低门槛，让更多研究者和爱好者参与 AI 安全与能力提升的探索。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(394) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newrepublic.com/article/208876/tech-world-evil-musk-bezos-thiel' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How the Tech World Turned Evil</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科技世界为何走向邪恶</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47881724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47881724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47881724'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical examination traces how tech platforms and AI ambitions have reshaped power dynamics, privacy norms, and public discourse, arguing that surveillance capitalism and hubris contributed to a corrosive tech culture. The piece calls for accountability and more humane design choices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章从权力、隐私与公共话语的角度，批判性地审视科技平台和 AI 追求如何改变了行业生态。认为监控资本主义与行业自大促成了有害的科技文化，并呼吁问责与更具人性化的设计。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47824051' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(395) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://binaryigor.com/modern-frontend-complexity.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Modern Front end Complexity: essential or accidental?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现代前端复杂性：本质需求还是偶然产物？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824051' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824051' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47824051'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Modern Front end Complexity debates whether the complexity is intrinsic to building rich web interfaces or an accidental byproduct of tooling and abstractions. The piece argues that simplifying toolchains could unlock productivity while preserving capability. It prompts engineers to reassess unnecessary abstractions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现代前端的复杂性到底是构建丰富网页界面的本质还是工具链与抽象带来的副作用？该文认为简化工具链可能释放生产力，同时保留能力，并促使开发者重新评估哪些抽象确实必要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://binaryigor.com/assets/og-image.png' data-full='https://binaryigor.com/assets/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47835837' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(396) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.owlposting.com/p/the-printing-press-for-biological' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A printing press for biological data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>生物数据的印刷机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835837' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835837' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47835837'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A platform described as a &#x27;printing press&#x27; for biological data aims to democratize how biology-related information is produced and shared. It promises scalable, reproducible generation of biological data artifacts—think DNA data storage, printed biological records, or lab outputs that can be mass-produced.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个被称为生物数据印刷机的系统，试图像印刷机一样放大生物数据的生成与传播。它可能实现生物信息的快速、可重复输出和分发，为研究和教育带来更高的可及性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47868832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47868832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47868832'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hybrid portrait of J.G. Ballard that blends biography, criticism, and fiction to reveal a nontraditional reading of the writer&#x27;s work and influence. The book challenges conventional life-story framing and emphasizes Ballard’s enduring relevance to technology, media, and modern urbanism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这本书以传记、评论与虚构混合的形式，给出对球尔德的非常规画像，挑战传统的生平叙事。文本强调 Ballard 对技术、媒介与城市生活的持续影响，重新评估他的文学地位。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(398) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://atomicapp.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Atomic – Local-first, AI-augmented personal knowledge base</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Atomic——本地优先、AI 增强的个人知识库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47889110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47889110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47889110'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Atomic offers a local-first personal knowledge base augmented by AI, enabling private, on-device indexing and search. It emphasizes offline readiness and user control over data, with AI providing suggestions without sending data to cloud.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Atomic 提供本地优先的个人知识库，结合 AI 增强的搜索与整理能力，数据始终在本地设备上。它强调离线可用性与数据控制，AI 给出建议时不需要上传到云端，提升隐私与响应速度。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(400) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.historytoday.com/archive/feature/panipat-rise-mughals' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Panipat: The rise of the Mughals</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>潘帕特：莫卧儿帝国的崛起</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47859514' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47859514' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47859514'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Historical feature tracing how the Mughal empire rose to power, focusing on Panipat&#x27;s strategic significance, key leaders, and political shifts that expanded imperial rule. The piece connects military campaigns with governance innovations that helped cement Mughal authority across much of the Indian subcontinent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文梳理莫卧儿帝国崛起的歷史脈絡，聚焦潘帕特在地緣與軍事上的重要性、領袖與政治變革如何拓展統治。透過戰事與治理創新的敘述，揭示莫卧兒在印度次大陸建立帝國權威的過程。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(401) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sfconservancy.org/blog/2026/apr/16/badgeware-onlyoffice-nextcloud-affero-gpl/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AGPLv3§74 Empowers Users to Thwart Badgeware Like OnlyOffice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AGPLv3 第74条：让用户有能力抵制徽章化软件，如 OnlyOffice</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47907543' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47907543' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47907543'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AGPLv3 Section 74 empowers users to push back against &#x27;badgeware&#x27;—excessive branding and tracking in software like OnlyOffice. The post argues this strengthens user rights and free-software norms, potentially reshaping how online services integrate branding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AGPLv3 第74条赋予用户抵制“徽章化”现象的权利，针对像 OnlyOffice 这类软件中过度标识与追踪的问题提供保护。文章认为这加强了用户权利与自由软件准则，或推动线上服务在品牌整合方面的改变。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47870439' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(403) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/04/20/science/desmond-morris-dead.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Desmond Morris, 98, Dies; Zoologist Saw Links Between Humans and Apes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Desmond Morris：98岁逝世，揭示人类与猿猴联系的动物学家</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47870439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47870439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47870439'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Desmond Morris, a zoologist and author of The Naked Ape, died at 98. His work framed human behavior as rooted in primate evolution and highlighted continuities with other apes, influencing popular science and our understanding of biology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>动物学家Desmond Morris去世，享年98岁。他的研究将人类行为置于灵长类进化的框架内，强调人与猿猴之间的共性，从而影响了公众对生物学和行为的认知。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个实用案例展示在 ESP32-S3 的第二核心上将裸机 Rust 与 ESP-IDF 并行运行，凸显多语言嵌入式栈的共存与潜在性能提升。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(405) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/esutcu/planb-lpm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A cache-friendly IPv6 LPM with AVX-512 (linearized B+-tree, real BGP benchmarks)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于 AVX-512 的缓存友好 IPv6 LPM（线性化 B+ 树，真实 BGP 基准测试）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47830071' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47830071' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47830071'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cache-friendly IPv6 LPM designed around AVX-512 uses a linearized B+-tree to boost route lookups under real BGP workloads. Benchmarks show meaningful improvements for high-speed routers and network software, with implications for future data-plane design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于 AVX-512 的缓存友好 IPv6 LPM 通过线性化 B+ 树提升实际 BGP 工作负载下的路由查找速度。基准测试显示对高带宽路由器和网络软件有显著提升，对未来数据平面设计具有潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c89dc4e145490ff2c658741cc22948f8f117c62f2de75157280dd0c50cd28e64/esutcu/planb-lpm' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c89dc4e145490ff2c658741cc22948f8f117c62f2de75157280dd0c50cd28e64/esutcu/planb-lpm' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47902339' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47902339' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(406) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mnot.net/blog/2026/04/24/agents_as_collective_bargains' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What&#x27;s missing in the &#x27;agentic&#x27; story: a well-defined user agent role</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“代理性”叙事中的缺失之处：需要明确定义的用户代理角色</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47902339' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47902339' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47902339'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that the &#x27;agentic&#x27; AI narrative lacks a clearly defined user agent role, clarifying who sets goals, constraints, and responsibility. It calls for organizing agent behavior around explicit user-agent concepts and collective bargaining to manage autonomy and safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为当前的“代理性”叙事缺乏对用户代理角色的清晰定义，应该明确谁设定目标与约束、谁承担责任。主张将代理行为构建在拥有明确权利与义务的用户代理框架之上，以更好地管理自治与安全。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.mnot.net/blog/image/trains.jpeg' data-full='https://www.mnot.net/blog/image/trains.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47869042' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(407) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S0277379126001824' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Insights into firewood use by early Middle Pleistocene hominins</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>早期中更新世人类对柴火使用的洞见</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47869042' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47869042' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47869042'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New evidence sheds light on how early Middle Pleistocene hominins used firewood, suggesting fire management and cooked foods influenced diet and energy budgets. The findings have implications for social organization and cognitive evolution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新证据表明早期中更新世人类对柴火有系统性使用，涉及火源管理和烹煮食物。此类活动对能量获取、社会组织与认知演化有重要含义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(409) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://piccalil.li/blog/the-end-of-responsive-images/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The end of responsive images</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>响应式图片的终结</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47875420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47875420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47875420'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that the classic responsive images paradigm is fading as container queries, per-element sizing, and modern image formats redefine how images are served. The post suggests adopting newer primitives and standards to balance performance with quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中认为传统的响应式图片思路正在消退，因为容器查询等新标准将重新定义图片的加载与显示。提出在实际开发中采用更新的原语和标准，以在性能和画质之间取得平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://piccalil.b-cdn.net/api/og-image?slug=the-end-of-responsive-images/' data-full='https://piccalil.b-cdn.net/api/og-image?slug=the-end-of-responsive-images/' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47869496'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A profile of a colorful local figure known as the Neon King who anchors New Orleans&#x27; neon-lit nightlife and brand. The piece threads history of signage, design, and street culture to show how a single personality can shape a city&#x27;s glow and memory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章聚焦新奥尔良一位被称为“霓虹王”的人物，如何塑造城市的霓虹夜景与文化形象。通过讲述灯光设计、街头文化与历史，展现个人魅力如何影响城市记忆。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47857964' data-tags='Programming,Web,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47857964' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(411) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/justrach/kuri' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kuri – Zig based agent-browser alternative</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kuri——基于 Zig 的代理浏览器替代方案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47857964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47857964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47857964'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kuri is a Zig-based project offering an agent-style browser alternative, focusing on safety and performance from a systems-programming perspective. It aims to provide a minimal, fast browser-like toolchain with a focus on security and explicit control over resources.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kuri 是一个基于 Zig 的代理浏览器替代方案，强调系统编程中的安全性与性能。它致力于提供一个简洁、快速的浏览器相关工具集，对资源的使用进行明确控制。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47903690' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(417) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://getkloak.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Kloak, A secret manager that keeps K8s workload away from secrets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kloak：把 Kubernetes 工作负载与密钥分离的秘密管理器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903690' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903690' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47903690'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kloak is a secret manager that keeps Kubernetes workloads away from raw secrets, reducing blast radius if a pod is compromised. It leverages controlled access patterns and encryption boundaries to separate secrets from running workloads, improving security posture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kloak 是一个秘密管理器，确保 Kubernetes 工作负载无法直接访问敏感密钥，从而降低容器被入侵时的影响。它通过受控访问模式和加密边界实现秘密与运行时的分离，提升安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://getkloak.io/transparent-logo.svg' data-full='https://getkloak.io/transparent-logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47901312' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(419) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HEALPix' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HEALPix</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HEALPix：球面均等像素网格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47901312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47901312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47901312'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HEALPix is a spherical pixelization scheme that partitions the sky into equal-area, iso-latitude pixels, enabling efficient storage and analysis of astronomical maps. It’s widely used for CMB and other all-sky surveys, supporting hierarchical refinement and fast transforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HEALPix 是一种把球面划分为等面积像素的网格方法，具备等纬度特性，便于天球图像的存储和分析。在宇宙学和天文观测地图处理中被广泛使用，支持分层细化和快速变换。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/12/HEALPix_projection_SW.svg/1280px-HEALPix_projection_SW.svg.png' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/12/HEALPix_projection_SW.svg/1280px-HEALPix_projection_SW.svg.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47881672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47881672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47881672'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A GitHub Merge Queue incident silently reverted code, interrupting merges and deployments for affected projects. The episode illustrates the fragility of automated pipelines and the need for visible safeguards and reliable rollback mechanisms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 合并队列发生了悄无声息地回滚代码的事件，干扰了相关项目的合并与部署。此事凸显自动化流水线的脆弱性，以及对可见性和可靠回滚机制的需求。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2023年的临床指南汇总了成年自闭症和 ADHD 患者中常见的慢性疾病，强调需要综合照护并关注共病。内容涵盖睡眠障碍、焦虑、胃肠问题等，并提供整体管理的建议。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome 的提示 API 为应用收集和管理 AI 提示提供了标准化接口，简化集成并强化用户隐私控制。文中概述了常见的使用模式，以及这对在浏览器中构建 AI 助手的开发者意味着什么，标志着 AI 集成趋于统一。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(434) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15039' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Prefill-as-a-Service:KVCache of Next-Generation Models Could Go Cross-Datacenter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>预填充即服务：下一代模型的KV缓存或将跨数据中心</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47822117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47822117'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper proposes a cross-datacenter KV cache for model prefill, enabling faster cold-start and consistent latency across regions for next-gen models. It discusses architecture trade-offs, consistency, and potential cost implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文提出了一个跨数据中心的 KV 缓存用于模型预填充，提升新一代模型的冷启动速度和跨区域的一致性延迟。讨论了架构权衡、一致性以及潜在的成本影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>8800 美元房屋设计有助于预防非洲儿童致命疾病</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47896664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47896664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47896664'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An affordable housing design around $8,800 focuses on ventilation, lighting, and sanitation to reduce disease exposure in African children, potentially lowering fatal disease risk. The concept demonstrates how low-cost architecture can improve health outcomes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款约 8800 美元的低成本房屋设计，着重于通风、光照与卫生条件，以降低非洲儿童暴露于疾病的风险，可能有助于减少致命疾病的发生。该概念展示了低成本建筑如何改善健康结果。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47837260' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47837260' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(436) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-04-colorado-river-geological-million-years.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Colorado River disappeared record for 5M years: now we know where it was</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>五百万年前消失的科罗拉多河：如今揭示它的曾经位置</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47837260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47837260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47837260'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Geologists reconstruct where the Colorado River flowed over 5 million years, revealing the river&#x27;s route as it vanished from the landscape. The work uses sedimentology and basin analyses to map ancient drainage patterns. It enriches our understanding of regional geology and landscape evolution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>地质学家追踪五百万年前科罗拉多河的河道走向，揭示了它在地表消失前的路线。研究通过沉积学和盆地分析重建了古河道分布，拓展了对区域地质与地貌演化的理解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2022/colorado-river.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2022/colorado-river.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47908166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47908166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47908166'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece revisits APL&#x27;s cultural lineage, arguing that its history and usage are more rooted in French-speaking communities than English-speaking ones. It sheds light on the sociolinguistic aspects of programming language ecosystems and how design decisions propagate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章追溯 APL 的文化脉络，认为其历史与使用更深植于法语社群而非英语世界。它揭示了编程语言生态中语言和地区文化的影响，以及设计选择如何在社区中流传。</p>
</div>
</div>

These limits affect yield, cost, and performance, prompting material and process innovations to push Moore’s law in new directions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>业界开始聚焦高级封装的极限，包括3D-IC 整合、热管理与互连瓶颈。这些限制影响良率、成本与性能，正推动材料与工艺创新，将摩尔定律推向新的发展方向。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(445) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.furrtek.org/?a=esl' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reverse-engineering infrared-based electronic shelf labels</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>逆向工程基于红外的电子货架标签</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47858982'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reverse-engineering infrared-based electronic shelf labels exposes how these labels operate and where the security weaknesses lie. The work uncovers protocols and IR schemes used, informing security researchers and retailers about potential spoofing, eavesdropping, or denial-of-service risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>揭示基于红外的电子货架标签的工作原理及潜在安全弱点。分析了它们所用的协议与红外方案，为安全研究人员和零售商提供警示，涉及潜在的欺骗、窃听或拒绝服务风险。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introducing Tiao, a new two-player turn-based board game with online play and potential AI opponents. The project highlights approachable game design and opportunities for algorithmic competition.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(454) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tech.yahoo.com/ai/meta-ai/articles/exclusive-meta-start-capturing-employee-162745587.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta to start capturing employee mouse movement, keystrokes for AI training data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 将开始捕获员工鼠标移动与按键用于 AI 训练数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851086' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851086' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47851086'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta plans to start capturing employee mouse movements and keystrokes for AI training data, raising workplace privacy concerns and governance questions. The policy could influence enterprise data practices and worker autonomy across tech firms. It spotlights the tension between improving AI capabilities and protecting user privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 将开始收集员工鼠标移动和按键以用于 AI 训练数据，引发对职场隐私与治理的担忧。此举可能影响科技公司层面的数据实践与员工自治权，凸显提升 AI 能力与保护隐私之间的矛盾。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/BCEE414F807BC7E0036F680200E221B912363E83670DA246713BD550BB3F028E/subgraphmysterio/resizefit_w1200;quality_90;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fs.yimg.com%2Fos%2Fen%2Freuters.com%2Fad2dc91f4f33788522d57f9dd847d6a8' data-full='https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/BCEE414F807BC7E0036F680200E221B912363E83670DA246713BD550BB3F028E/subgraphmysterio/resizefit_w1200;quality_90;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fs.yimg.com%2Fos%2Fen%2Freuters.com%2Fad2dc91f4f33788522d57f9dd847d6a8' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47840851' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(455) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/zeroecco/holos' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Holos – QEMU/KVM with a compose-style YAML, GPUs and health checks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Holos——使用类似 compose 的 YAML、GPU 与健康检查的 QEMU/KVM</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47840851' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47840851' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47840851'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Holos introduces a compose-like declarative YAML for configuring QEMU/KVM deployments, including GPU passthrough and health checks. It aims to simplify complex virtualization setups and improve reliability through automated validation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Holos 引入类似 compose 的 YAML 声明式配置来管理 QEMU/KVM 虚拟化环境，支持 GPU 直通和健康检查。目标是简化复杂的虚拟化部署并通过自动化校验提高可靠性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/711737da741ddd970edcad15eb0c09f3308d862fd84f3b92d40fdd9b58d27aab/zeroecco/holos' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/711737da741ddd970edcad15eb0c09f3308d862fd84f3b92d40fdd9b58d27aab/zeroecco/holos' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47834134' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(456) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260417-ai-chatbots-could-be-making-you-stupider' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI chatbots could be making you stupider</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 聊天机器人可能让你变得更愚蠢</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47834134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47834134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47834134'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>BBC Future warns that AI chatbots could erode critical thinking and memory, urging design and literacy strategies to keep minds sharp. It emphasizes balancing convenience with cognitive skills.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BBC 未来专栏警示 AI 聊天机器人可能削弱批判性思维与记忆力，呼吁通过设计与数字素养策略维持认知能力。强调在便利性与认知技能之间寻求平衡。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(457) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://solar.lowtechmagazine.com/2026/04/rediscovering-the-handcart/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rediscovering the Handcart</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47873192'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into the handcart, showing how a simple, human-powered transport tool can inform discussions about energy, climate, and resilient design. The piece reflects on how low-tech approaches can complement modern systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章重新审视手推车这种简单的人力运输工具，探讨其在能源、气候与韧性设计方面的启示。指出低科技方法可以在现代系统中发挥互补作用。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47843569'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Prediction markets are breaking news and becoming their own beat, as traders and researchers mine events for probabilities and monetize forecasts. The shift challenges traditional newsroom models and raises questions about credibility, latency, and bias.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>预测市场正在成为新闻源并逐渐形成独立的报道板块，参与者通过对事件结果的概率进行交易与分析。此趋势对传统新闻业提出挑战，也引发关于可信度、时效性与偏见的问题。</p>

Rubin Observatory has Discovered 11,000 New Asteroids</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Vera C. Rubin Observatory has cataloged roughly 11,000 newly discovered asteroids as its survey ramps up, signaling the scale of data to come for planetary science and defense planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>鲁宾天文台在观测工作逐步展开的过程中，约新增一万一千颗小行星的发现量，预示行星科学与潜在威胁评估将迎来海量数据。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(475) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/google-labs-code/design.md' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Design.md: A format spec for describing a visual identity to coding agents</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47887123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47887123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Design.md proposes a machine-readable format to describe a brand&#x27;s visual identity for coding agents and AI systems. This enables automated design reasoning, synthesis of branding assets, and more predictable branding across AI-powered workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Design.md 提出一种机器可读的格式，用于向编码代理描述品牌的视觉识别要素。这有助于 AI 驱动的设计推理、品牌资产的自动生成，以及在工作流中实现更可预测的品牌一致性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains escrow security for iCloud Keychain, including how recovery keys work and the trust chain between devices and Apple&#x27;s servers. Highlights security implications for password recovery, cross-device syncing, and potential attack considerations.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(478) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thepointmag.com/criticism/a-history-of-erasures/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A History of Erasures Learning to Write Like Leylâ Erbil</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47837857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47837857' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47837857'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores erasures as a literary method and how to write in the style of Leylâ Erbil, a modernist Turkish author. It delves into memory, marginalization, and the politics of language, offering readers a method for engaging with erased histories.</p>

It shows how to deliver rich, WYSIWYG-like feedback in a terminal, useful for developers who want fast tooling without leaving the command line.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47827991' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47827991' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(480) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.brutman.com/Adventures_In_Code/EtherSlip_ARP/EtherSlip_ARP.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hunting a 34 year old pointer bug in EtherSlip</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>追踪 EtherSlip 中一处持续34年的指针漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47827991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47827991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47827991'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A long-running pointer bug in EtherSlip ARP code is traced, showcasing debugging detective work across decades of software. The write-up highlights subtle aliasing and memory-safety traps, and the importance of disciplined debugging practices in complex systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 EtherSlip 的 ARP 实现中追踪到一个持续了34年的指针漏洞，展示了跨久的调试侦探过程。文章强调了内存安全、别名及指针相关的陷阱，以及在复杂系统中进行细致调试的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.brutman.com/Adventures_In_Code/EtherSlip_ARP/arp_format.gif' data-full='https://www.brutman.com/Adventures_In_Code/EtherSlip_ARP/arp_format.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
