<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47907271' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(481) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sfconservancy.org/blog/2026/apr/02/fcc-router-ban/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What the FCC router ban means for FOSS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FCC 路由器禁令对自由与开源软件的影响</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47907271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47907271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47907271'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FCC&#x27;s router ban could reshape how freely open-source firmware and software can be installed on devices, affecting user control and the viability of projects like OpenWrt. The post analyzes implications for FOSS router ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FCC 的路由器禁令可能重塑设备上自由开放固件和软件的安装方式，影响用户控制权以及像 OpenWrt 之类项目的可行性。文中分析了对自由软件路由器生态的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sfconservancy.org/static/img/conservancy-logo.png' data-full='https://sfconservancy.org/static/img/conservancy-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47874507' data-tags='Hardware,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47874507' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(482) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.xda-developers.com/steamos-now-runs-on-every-amd-handheld-and-valve-didnt-even-make-a-big-deal-about-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SteamOS now runs on every AMD handheld</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SteamOS 现已在所有 AMD 手持设备上运行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47874507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47874507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47874507'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SteamOS now runs on every AMD handheld, a discovery that Valve quietly enabled across devices. This broadens Linux gaming availability on portable hardware and lowers barriers for developers targeting handheld form factors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SteamOS 现在在所有 AMD 手持设备上可用， Valve 并未做广泛宣传。此举扩大了便携硬件上的 Linux 游戏生态，可能影响独立手持市场和开发者的设备选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static0.xdaimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/wm/2025/07/obsidian-steam-deck-6.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://static0.xdaimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/wm/2025/07/obsidian-steam-deck-6.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47852572' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47852572' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(483) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lists.archlinux.org/archives/list/arch-dev-public@lists.archlinux.org/thread/44PW52T547MACXFU5HZ5U7SIPAX3BAXS/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Arch Linux now has a bit-for-bit reproducible Docker image</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Arch Linux 现已推出逐比特可重复的 Docker 镜像</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47852572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47852572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47852572'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Arch Linux now offers a bit-for-bit reproducible Docker image, ensuring deterministic builds across environments. This enhances reproducibility for CI pipelines, debugging, and compliance, making Arch deployments more predictable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Arch Linux 现已提供逐比特可重复的 Docker 镜像，确保跨环境的确定性构建。这提升了持续集成、调试和合规性方面的可重复性，使 Arch 的部署更加可预测。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lists.archlinux.org/static/hyperkitty/img/logo.png' data-full='https://lists.archlinux.org/static/hyperkitty/img/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tim Davis discusses probabilistic engineering and the 24-7 employee concept, arguing for probabilistic thinking in reliability and availability.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47874422' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(492) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eurekalert.org/news-releases/1124525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>98% of all recent environmental claims can be categorized as &quot;greenwashing&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>近年环境声明的 98% 可归为绿色漂洗</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47874422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47874422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47874422'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new analysis argues that about 98% of recent environmental claims are greenwashing, signaling widespread misrepresentation of sustainability efforts. The piece underscores the need for verifiable metrics and stricter regulatory oversight of corporate environmental marketing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项新研究认为近 98% 的环境声明属于绿色漂洗，揭示企业在可持续性宣传中的广泛歪曲。文章强调需要可核验的指标和更严格的监管来约束企业环境营销。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eurekalert.org/images/EurekAlert-bluebg_Twitter_601X601.png' data-full='https://www.eurekalert.org/images/EurekAlert-bluebg_Twitter_601X601.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(493) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/shreeve/rip-lang' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rip language. Compiles to ES2022. Built-in reactivity</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47834913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47834913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47834913'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rip is a new language that compiles to ES2022 with built-in reactivity, aiming to simplify building reactive web apps. The design focuses on ergonomic syntax and frontend performance for modern developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 Rip 语言——一门将目标编译为 ES2022、内置响应式特性的全新语言，旨在简化构建响应式网页应用的工作。设计重点在于提升前端开发者的编码体验与性能。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(497) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rockcat/HATS' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI agents that argue with each other to improve decisions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>互相辩论的AI代理以提升决策</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47903471'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI agents that argue with each other can surface better explanations and catch errors, pushing decisions toward higher quality outcomes. The approach shows promise for decision support, though it raises calibration and safety questions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>让 AI 代理相互辩论以改进决策的思路显示出提高解释力与发现错误的潜力，可能成为支持决策的有力工具。此方法前景乐观，但也需关注校准和安全性等挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece recounts Hershey&#x27;s involvement in building an electric railway in Cuba in 1916, blending corporate ambition with political and social context, and how it reflects the intersection of technology and colonial enterprise. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾了 1916 年可可力在古巴建设电气铁路的历史，揭示企业野心与政治、社会背景之间的关系，以及科技与殖民开发之间的相互作用。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(501) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/Linux-7.1-Input' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux 7.1 Removes Drivers for Bus Mouse Support</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 7.1 移除对总线鼠标的驱动支持</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47890365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47890365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47890365'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This release drops the legacy bus mouse drivers, reflecting Linux&#x27;s ongoing pruning of old hardware support. It could impact users still relying on ancient mice and invites alternative input methods, like USB or PS/2, but marks a trend toward modern peripherals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该版本移除了对总线鼠标的驱动，显示出 Linux 在精简对旧硬件的支持。这对仍依赖老式鼠标的用户可能造成不便，同时推动改用 USB/PS2 等现代输入方式，但也体现了向现代外设的转变趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.phoronix.net/image.php?id=2026&amp;image=bus_mouse' data-full='https://www.phoronix.net/image.php?id=2026&amp;image=bus_mouse' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47878560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 13</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47878560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47878560'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canonical releases Ubuntu 26.04 LTS &#x27;Resolute Raccoon,&#x27; a long-term support release with updated kernel, desktop, and security features, signaling stability for enterprises and enthusiasts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Canonical 发布 Ubuntu 26.04 LTS（Resolute Raccoon），提供长期支持、更新的内核、桌面环境和安全特性，面向企业及爱好者的稳定性需求。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(504) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://anderegg.ca/2026/04/22/llm-pricing-has-never-made-sense' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLM pricing has never made sense</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LLM 定价从未讲得通</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47875694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47875694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47875694'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article contends that current large language model pricing lacks a coherent rationale, mixing per-token costs, inference vs training, and platform fees in confusing ways. It proposes conceptual fixes or practical guidance for builders and buyers navigating model access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为当前大语言模型的定价缺乏连贯的逻辑，混合了按 token、推理/训练成本和平台费等多种维度，导致定价难以理解。提出了可能的改进思路和对开发者与买家的建议。</p>
